Result: 成功推导出量子软覆盖与隐私放大的精确可靠性函数：软覆盖的可靠性函数由夹层Rényi互信息与混合阶二阶Rényi互信息联合表征；隐私放大的可靠性函数则由对应的条件熵表征。这为混合阶Rényi散度提供了明确的操作性解释。

Conclusion: 所提出的混合阶Rényi散度具有重要的操作意义，能够精确刻画量子软覆盖和隐私放大的可靠性函数。这是学界首次对量子软覆盖的可靠性函数实现精确表征，为量子信息处理任务的性能分析提供了新的理论框架。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a novel mixed-order Rényi divergence and investigate its fundamental properties. Using this divergence, we define a family of mixed-order order-two Rényi mutual information and Rényi conditional entropy. We derive exact reliability functions of quantum soft covering and privacy amplification under the sandwiched Rényi divergence with order $α\in[2,\infty)$. The former is jointly characterized by the sandwiched and mixed-order order-two Rényi mutual information quantities, while the latter is characterized by the corresponding conditional entropies. These results provide operational interpretations of the proposed mixed-order Rényi divergence. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first exact characterization of the reliability function for quantum soft covering.

</details>


### [95] [Quantum random-number generator with non-demolition measurements: semi-device-independent implementation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27025)
*Paolo Solinas,Giovanni Chesi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于三方量子系统的新型量子随机数生成器，利用量子非破坏测量方案，使一个探测器认证量子效应，另一个生成高熵随机数，实现了随机数生成与认证的同步进行，且无需类空间隔，便于小型化和实际应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有设备无关量子随机数生成器依赖于探测器间的类空间隔，这一约束限制了设备的实际实现和小型化。本文旨在设计一种无需类空间隔即可同时完成随机数生成与认证的协议，以推动可扩展量子技术的发展。

Method: 构建一个三方量子系统（一个二能级系统 + 两个三能级系统），采用量子非破坏（QND）测量方案。其中一个探测器用于认证系统演化中真实量子效应的存在，另一个探测器从可优化分布中生成随机数。通过优化使输出分布接近三结果均匀分布，从而最大化熵值。

Result: 成功生成接近均匀的三结果分布随机数，熵值接近最大，实现了近乎最优的随机性生成。随机数生成与认证过程同步完成，且认证不依赖探测器间的类空间隔。

Conclusion: 该协议解除了设备无关方法中对类空间隔的依赖，显著降低了实现难度，使设备小型化成为可能，是面向可扩展量子技术的有前景的候选方案。

Abstract: We propose and analyze a novel quantum random-number generator based on a tripartite quantum system in which two subsystems act as detectors. Within a quantum non-demolition measurement scheme, one detector is used to certify the presence of genuine quantum effects in the system's evolution, while the second generates random numbers from a distribution that can be optimized to maximize their entropy. Using one two-level system and two three-level systems, we generate random numbers from a nearly uniform three-outcome distribution, yielding close-to-maximal entropy and therefore near-optimal randomness generation. A key feature of the protocol is that randomness generation and certification occur simultaneously. Moreover, certification does not rely on spacelike separation between detectors, removing a major constraint of device-independent approaches. This property enables practical implementation and facilitates the miniaturization of the device, making the protocol a promising candidate for scalable quantum technologies.

</details>


### [96] [Unconventional Thermalization of a Three-Wave-Mixing Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27028)
*Evangelos Varvelis,Miriam Resch,Joachim Ankerhold*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种具有非局域三波混频的多体系统，发现全局能级间距统计显示可积性，而动力学可观测量和逆参与比却表明遍历性——这一悖论源于运动学约束驱动的强希尔伯特空间碎片化，而非显式全局对称性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究量子热化与局域化之间的边界是凝聚态物理和量子信息科学的核心前沿问题。特别关注长程三体相互作用系统中，运动学约束如何导致非传统的热化行为。

Method: 采用精确对角化技术，分析具有非局域三波混频的通用模型（近期在超导量子比特镜终止的微波法布里-珀罗腔中实现）。计算全局能级间距统计、动力学可观测量、本征态逆参与比、非时序关联器（OTOC）以及本征态纠缠熵涨落。

Result: 发现全局能级间距统计呈现可积性特征，但动力学可观测量和逆参与比却表明遍历性和退局域化，这一悖论源于运动学约束导致的强希尔伯特空间碎片化。在碎片化子空间内部，OTOC显示快速的信息扰乱；但全局输运严重受阻，表现为对数弛豫至平衡态。本征态纠缠熵在同能量下的涨落进一步佐证了这一图像。晚期OTOC平均值随系统尺寸标度，可作为三体动力学瓶颈的实验可探测特征。

Conclusion: 该模型揭示了运动学约束驱动的希尔伯特空间碎片化可导致可积性统计与遍历动力学共存的奇特现象，为理解多体量子系统中热化与局域化的边界提供了新视角，并提出了实验可验证的OTOC标度行为作为诊断工具。

Abstract: Understanding the boundaries between quantum thermalization and localization in many-body systems remains a central frontier of condensed matter and quantum information science. In this work, we investigate the dynamics and spectral properties of a generic model with long-range three-body-interaction, namely, a system with non-local three-wave-mixing. This model has been realized recently with a microwave Fabry-Perot cavity terminated on one end by a superconducting qubit mirror. Utilizing exact diagonalization techniques, we uncover a striking paradox: the global energy level spacing statistics show integrability, even though all dynamic observables and inverse participation ratios of the eigenstates indicate ergodicity and delocalization. We show that this behavior is a hallmark of strong Hilbert space fragmentation driven by kinematic constraints rather than an explicit global symmetry. Inside these sectors, dynamics scramble rapidly, as evidenced by the out-of-time-ordered correlator (OTOC), while global transport is heavily bottlenecked, resulting in a logarithmic relaxation to equilibrium. This picture is further confirmed by fluctuations in eigenstate entanglement entropy at the same energy. Finally, we demonstrate that the late time OTOC average scales with system size, providing a distinct experimentally accessible signature of the underlying three-body kinetic bottlenecks.

</details>


### [97] [Quasi-polar Decomposition of Quantum Neural Networks via Adaptive Non-local Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27051)
*Shih-Hao Ho,Yan Li,Huan-Hsin Tseng,Hsin-Yi Lin,Samuel Yen-Chi Chen,Shinjae Yoo*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出使用对角自适应非局域可观测量（DANO）作为变分量子电路模型演化的正则分解框架。该框架将可观测量分解为对角谱（径向坐标）和酉基（角坐标），将训练过程转化为谱空间和李代数空间中的轨迹。实验表明，DANO径向谱扩展与分类准确率相关，角坐标揭示了一个与准确率相关的主导分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分量子电路模型的训练过程缺乏直观的几何或结构化的表征手段，难以从内部理解模型演化行为。作者希望提供一种新的正则分解视角，将训练动态映射到谱空间与李代数空间，以便更好地刻画和解释量子模型的行为。

Method: 采用对角自适应非局域可观测量（DANO）作为正则分解工具。将每个学习到的可观测量分解为对角谱（谱权重作为径向坐标）和酉基（通过李群对应作为角坐标），形成准极坐标描述。由此将训练过程转化为谱空间和李代数空间中的轨迹，并在此框架下分析模型演化。

Result: 在两个分类任务上的实验表明：(1) DANO径向谱扩展与模型准确率正相关；(2) DANO角坐标中存在一个与准确率强相关的主导分量。该框架为量子模型行为提供了有效的表征视角。

Conclusion: DANO框架通过谱-李代数分解，为变分量子电路的训练动态提供了一种新颖的几何化理解方式，有望成为分析和诊断量子模型行为的通用工具。

Abstract: We use Diagonal Adaptive Non-local Observables (DANO) as a canonical decomposition for studying Variational Quantum Circuit model evolution. Separating each learned observable into a diagonal spectrum and a unitary basis gives a quasi-polar description: the spectral weights are viewed as radial coordinates, while the unitary circuit serves as angular coordinates through Lie group identifications. This turns the training process into a trajectory in spectral and Lie-algebra space.
  Experiments on two classification tasks show that DANO radial spectral expansion correlates with accuracy. DANO angle coordinates reveal a dominant accuracy-correlated component. The framework provides a different perspective to characterize quantum model behavior.

</details>


### [98] [Optimising Trotter-Suzuki Simulations of Markovian Open Quantum Systems via Classical Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27060)
*S. M. Pillay,I. J. David,I. Sinayskiy,F. Petruccione*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对数字量子计算机上模拟开放量子系统的Trotter-Suzuki积公式，推导了一阶和二阶确定性与随机性方法所需Trotter步数的解析界限，并提出了一种基于单李雅维illian项钻石范数和二分搜索的高效经典算法来优化步数。数值结果表明，理论解析界限通常过于保守，而经验优化界限可大幅减少步数，其中二阶随机TS-PF资源需求最低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数字量子计算机上利用Trotter-Suzuki积公式模拟开放量子系统时，精确估计所需Trotter步数对最小化计算成本至关重要，但现有理论界限往往过于保守，导致资源估算不切实际。

Method: (1) 基于已有理论保证，推导一阶和二阶确定性及随机化TS-PF的解析界限，将Trotter步数与模型参数、演化时间和精度直接关联；(2) 提出一种经典算法，利用单个李雅维illian项的钻石范数估计结合二分搜索，显著降低达到目标精度所需的Trotter步数；(3) 在两个原型模型（带边界驱动和局域退相干的XX自旋链、横场Ising模型）上进行数值验证。

Result: 理论解析界限通常过度保守；经验优化界限在相同精度下所需Trotter步数显著减少。在考察的方法中，二阶随机化TS-PF通常实现最低的资源需求，尤其在大系统中优势明显。

Conclusion: 经验边界策略对于实现马尔可夫开放量子系统更高效的资源模拟具有重要意义，二阶随机化TS-PF是当前最具资源效率的选择。

Abstract: Simulating an open quantum system on a digital quantum computer often involves the use of Trotter-Suzuki (TS) Product Formulas (PF) to approximate the system's time evolution. Precise estimates for the required number of Trotter steps (and hence the overall gate count) can be crucial for minimising the computational cost of these methods. Building on established theoretical guarantees, we derive analytic bounds for the First- and Second-Order Deterministic and Randomised TS-PF, directly relating the number of Trotter steps to the model parameters, evolution time and precision. These bounds enable concrete resource estimation for each method. We then present a computationally efficient classical algorithm that uses diamond norm estimates of individual Liouvillian terms and a binary search to significantly reduce the Trotter steps required for a target precision. Our numerical results on two prototypical models - an XX-Spin Chain with boundary driving and local dephasing, and a Transverse-Field Ising Model - show that the theoretical (analytic) bounds are often overly conservative, whereas the empirical (optimised) bounds yield a significantly smaller number of Trotter steps for the same precision. Among the methods investigated, the Second-Order Randomised TS-PF typically achieves the lowest resource demands, especially for larger systems. These findings emphasise the significance of empirical bounding strategies in achieving more resource-efficient simulations of Markovian open quantum systems.

</details>


### [99] [Adaptive Multi-Backend Simulation of Near-Clifford Quantum Circuits via Spatial Stabilizer-Frame Partitioning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27075)
*Héctor J. García*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合Feynman路径求和与stabilizer-frame模拟的Clifford+T量子电路精确振幅模拟器，通过递归多层二分、自动回退密集态向量及代价模型驱动的划分选择，克服了传统割边数最小化启发式的缺陷，在分层基准上实现高达47.9倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有stabilizer-based Schrödinger-Feynman方法依赖割边数最小化启发式，但该代理指标不可靠——割边更少的划分可能因T门密度不均导致两侧stabilizer-frame膨胀，反而增加实际运行时间。此外，叶节点振幅提取存在二次渐近低效问题，亟需更精确的代价模型与高效的单振幅内积方法。

Method: (1) 递归多层二分：将量子电路沿平衡比特划分递归构建为二叉树，每层独立进行Feynman路径求和与stabilizer-frame模拟；(2) 自动回退机制：当叶节点stabilizer-frame规模超过内存上限时，自动切换为密集态向量模拟；(3) 代价模型驱动划分选择：以2^w（stabilizer-frame界）替代2^n（密集态向量界）建模每侧代价，并显式建模单振幅读出成本，替换原有O(F·s·n)单振幅内积方法消除二次渐近低效；(4) 路径求和过程具有尴尬并行性，通信量恒定。

Result: 在结构化分层n=16基准上，递归模拟器相较单片stabilizer-frame模拟加速92倍至17,645倍；相同割边下比密集半态向量基线每条路径快79倍；相较生产级态向量模拟器端到端加速最高47.9倍（中位数约5倍）。在对抗性随机Clifford+T电路上，当割边数超过n/2时密集态向量反超——代价模型准确预测了该交叉点。

Conclusion: 割边数最小化是经典量子电路模拟中不可靠的代理指标，应被精确的代价模型取代。递归多层二分框架结合自适应回退与代价驱动划分，在大规模分层电路上显著优于现有方法，且其主导计算（Feynman求和）具有天然并行优势。

Abstract: We present an exact amplitude simulator for Clifford+T quantum circuits that combines a Feynman path sum across a balanced qubit bipartition with stabilizer-frame simulation on each half. The construction extends prior stabilizer-based Schrödinger-Feynman methods in three directions: recursive multilevel bipartition into a binary tree, automatic fallback to dense state-vector simulation when a leaf's stabilizer frame would exceed its memory ceiling, and a cost-model-driven partition selector that replaces the standard cut-count minimization heuristic.
  We show cut-count minimization is an unreliable proxy in practice: a globally cleaner partition can reduce cross-cut count yet increase wall-clock time, because it imbalances T-gate density across halves and inflates per-half stabilizer-frame size. Our cost model substitutes the stabilizer-frame bound 2w for the dense 2n ceiling per side and explicitly models per-amplitude readout cost; isolating that term uncovered a quadratic-asymptotic inefficiency in the leaf simulator's end-of-path amplitude extraction, fixed by replacing it with an existing O(F * s * n) single-amplitude inner product.
  On a structured hierarchical n=16 benchmark the recursive simulator beats monolithic stabilizer-frame simulation by 92x to 17,645x, wins by 79x per path against a dense half-state-vector baseline under an identical cut, and beats a production state-vector simulator end to end by up to 47.9x (median ~5x). On adversarial random Clifford+T circuits the dense state vector wins past a crossover near n/2 cross-cut gates -- the regime the cost model identifies. The dominant cost, the cross-cut Feynman sum, is embarrassingly parallel with constant inter-worker communication, unlike recent matrix-product-state stabilizer-tensor methods whose inner contraction loop is sequential.

</details>


### [100] [Native CCZ Gate with Fluxonium Qubits and a Microwave-Driven Coupler](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27094)
*Grigoriy S. Mazhorin,Tatyana A. Chudakova,Alena S. Kazmina,Nikolai G. Berezkin,Arina V. Zotova,Artyom M. Polyanskiy,Nikolay N. Abramov,Mikhail A. Tarkhov,Alexander M. Mumlyakov,Igor V. Trofimov,Elizaveta A. Krivko,Nikita Yu. Rudenko,Maxim V. Chichkov,Vladimir I. Chichkov,Ilya A. Simakov*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 实验实现了基于fluxonium量子比特的65纳秒原生CCP（Toffoli）门，保真度达99.39(5)%，所需控制简单且可扩展，若用传统分解需CZ门保真度约99.94%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原生多量子比特门可减少分解为单/双比特门的开销，但能否同时实现高保真度、简单控制以及对寄生相互作用的鲁棒性仍不明确。

Method: 在fluxonium量子比特构成的三比特处理器单元中，通过微波驱动的transmon耦合器，使用单个控制脉冲实现65纳秒的受控-受控相位（CCP）门，门操作仅需简单的校准流程即可达到相干极限性能。

Result: 实现了保真度99.39(5)%的原生Toffoli等效门，传统分解实现相同操作需CZ门保真度约99.94%；该处理器单元可自然扩展至二维布局且寄生相互作用低。

Conclusion: 原生多量子比特门是超导量子处理器中一种可行的硬件高效原语，为可扩展架构提供了新路径。

Abstract: Native multi-qubit gates could reduce the overhead associated with decompositions into single- and two-qubit operations, but whether they can simultaneously provide high fidelity, simple control and robustness against parasitic interactions in scalable architectures remains unclear. Here we experimentally realize a 65-ns native controlled-controlled-phase operation, locally equivalent to the Toffoli gate, with a fidelity of 99.39(5)% in a three-qubit processor unit based on fluxonium qubits coupled via a microwave-driven transmon coupler. The implemented operation would require CZ fidelities of approximately 99.94% if realized through a conventional decomposition. The gate is implemented with a single control pulse, that relies on a simple calibration procedure yielding coherence-limited performance. This processor unit naturally extends to scalable two-dimensional layouts with low parasitic interactions. Altogether, these results establish native multi-qubit gates as a viable hardware-efficient primitive for scalable superconducting quantum processors.

</details>


### [101] [Effective Hamiltonians for Predictive Quantum Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27111)
*Razvan Stanescu,Hugo Ribeiro*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了超导transmon量子比特单量子门控制中模型选择的敏感性，比较了标准Duffing近似与对角化transmon本征基哈密顿量两种模型。结果表明，在Duffing模型下优化的修正脉冲迁移到更精确的对角化模型后效果显著下降，甚至在高保真度快门区域失效；模型间能谱与驱动算符表示的微小差异会在含时演化中积累，导致不同的误差生成元和AC Stark相位失配。作者据此提出包含泄漏通道和高阶误差的扩展修正框架，以提升门性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高保真量子门依赖精确的驱动动力学模型。当前广泛使用的Duffing近似可能因忽略高阶能级效应和驱动算符的精确表示而导致模型误差。本文旨在定量评估Duffing近似与更精确的对角化transmon模型之间的差异如何影响控制脉冲的设计和门保真度，揭示模型依赖性对量子控制优化的根本制约。

Method: 作者构建了两种哈密顿量模型——标准Duffing近似和通过数值对角化transmon本征基得到的精确哈密顿量。对两种模型采用相同的修正脉冲构造框架，分别推导修正场；随后将Duffing模型导出的修正脉冲与对角化模型中独立校准的基准脉冲结合，比较门误差。通过分析能谱差异、驱动算符表示差异以及AC Stark相位积累的失配，诊断动力学模型依赖性的来源。进一步提出扩展的误差修正框架，纳入泄漏路径和高阶误差通道。

Result: Duffing近似导出的修正场能显著降低该模型自身预测的门误差，但迁移至对角化transmon模型后效果大幅减弱；在快门区域，迁移后的修正甚至不如未修正的对角化模型基准脉冲。模型间能谱和驱动算符表示的微小差异在含时演化中积累复合，导致不同的预测误差生成元。AC Stark相位积累的失配是模型依赖性的一个典型诊断信号。纳入泄漏通道和高阶误差的扩展修正框架能利用相同物理控制资源有效提升门性能。

Conclusion: 量子控制脉冲的设计高度依赖于所采用的物理模型。Duffing近似虽计算简便，但其与精确对角化模型之间的差异足以导致修正策略失效。要突破当前门保真度瓶颈，必须采用更精确的模型并引入覆盖泄漏和高阶误差的扩展修正框架。模型依赖性应成为量子控制优化中的核心考量因素。

Abstract: High-fidelity quantum control relies on accurate models of driven dynamics. We examine this re- quirement for single-qubit gates in superconducting transmons by comparing control pulses derived from the standard Duffing approximation and from a Hamiltonian constructed by diagonalizing the transmon eigenbasis. Using the same correction-pulse construction for both models, we show that correction fields derived from the Duffing approximation can substantially reduce the gate error pre- dicted by that model while remaining less effective when combined with an independently calibrated baseline pulse in the diagonalized-transmon model. In the fast-gate regime, such transferred correc- tions can even fail to improve over the uncorrected diagonalized-transmon baseline. We show that small model-dependent differences in both the energy spectrum and the representation of the drive operator can compound during driven evolution, resulting in different predicted error generators and correction pulses. A mismatch in the accumulated AC Stark phase provides one illustrative di- agnostic of this dynamical model dependence. We further demonstrate that the model Hamiltonian informs the choice of control framework: Omitting relevant leakage pathways or higher-order error channels can lead to an overly restricted correction strategy. Including these channels motivates an extended correction framework that improves the gate performance using the same physical control resources.

</details>


### [102] [Ky Fan majorization for binary tensor products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27116)
*Michael M. Wolf,Yuanheng Zhou*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文给出了矩阵二元张量积之和的奇异值的Ky Fan型受控关系的一个简短证明，将Alhejji的两项和正矩阵的结果推广到任意项和任意矩阵。作为应用，证明了完全正映射的奇异值与其Kraus算子的奇异值之间的受控关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Alhejji (arXiv:2410.18254) 此前建立了两个正矩阵的二元张量积之和的奇异值的Ky Fan型受控关系。本文旨在将该结果从两个加项推广到任意多个加项，并将矩阵条件从正矩阵放宽到任意矩阵，从而大幅扩展该不等式的适用范围。

Method: 作者提供了一个简短的证明方法，利用奇异值与Ky Fan范数之间的对偶刻画，通过Ky Fan k-范数的定义及矩阵分析中的标准技巧，将二元张量积之和的奇异值受控关系从两加项推广到多加项情形，同时摆脱了矩阵正性的限制。

Result: 成功建立了任意多个任意矩阵的二元张量积之和的奇异值的Ky Fan型受控不等式。作为推论，得到了完全正映射的奇异值与其Kraus算子的奇异值之间的受控关系。

Conclusion: 本文以简洁的证明将Alhejji的Ky Fan型受控关系显著推广至多加项和任意矩阵情形，并通过完全正映射与Kraus算子的奇异值受控关系展示了结果在量子信息理论中的应用价值。

Abstract: We provide a short proof of a Ky Fan-type majorization relation for the singular values of a sum of binary tensor products of matrices. This generalizes Alhejji's result (arXiv:2410.18254) from two summands to arbitrary sums and from positive matrices to arbitrary matrices. As an application, we show a majorization relation between the singular values of a completely positive map and those of its Kraus operators.

</details>


### [103] [The Keyl-Werner algorithm is not optimal for spectrum estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27117)
*Angelos Pelecanos,Jack Spilecki,Ewin Tang,John Wright*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅需 $n = O(d^2 \cdot (\log\log(d)/\log(d))^2)$ 份量子态副本即可在总变差距离下以常数误差估计密度矩阵 $ρ$ 特征值的算法，首次改进 Keyl-Werner 算法 $Θ(d^2)$ 的样本复杂度，解决了 2001 年的开放问题并推翻 2016 年 Wright 的猜想。核心技术是一种新的层析成像保证，其误差按 $\langle w|ρ|w\rangle$ 缩放。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态的特征值（谱）估计是量子信息中的基本问题。经典结果 Keyl-Werner 算法需 $Θ(d^2)$ 份副本，与完整态层析的样本复杂度相同。Keyl 和 Werner 于 2001 年提出是否存在更高效算法的开放问题，Wright 在 2016 年猜想 $Θ(d^2)$ 是最优的。本文旨在打破这一壁垒，证明特征值估计可以比完整态层析更高效。

Method: 核心贡献是一种新的"相对误差"层析成像保证：在任意方向 $|w\rangle$ 上的层析误差以 $\langle w|ρ|w\rangle$ 为尺度缩放，且该界限对所有方向同时成立。基于这一更强的保证，作者构造了特征值估计算法，仅需 $n = O(d^2 \cdot (\log\log(d)/\log(d))^2)$ 份副本即可实现总变差距离下的常数误差估计。

Result: 算法将特征值估计的样本复杂度从 $Θ(d^2)$ 降至 $O(d^2 \cdot (\log\log(d)/\log(d))^2)$，首次实现严格低于完整态层析的复杂度，解决了 Keyl 和 Werner 提出的开放问题，并推翻 Wright 的 2016 年猜想。作为推论，还得到 Bures 距离下主成分分析和 $χ^2$-散度下层析成像的更优算法。

Conclusion: 本文证明了量子态特征值估计可以比完整态层析更高效，打破了 Keyl-Werner 的 $Θ(d^2)$ 壁垒。新的相对误差层析框架具有独立理论价值，可推广至其他量子学习任务。

Abstract: We give an algorithm which, given $n = O(d^2 \cdot (\log\log(d)/\log(d))^2)$ copies of $ρ$, estimates the eigenvalues of $ρ$ to constant error in total variation distance. Thus, we can learn the eigenvalues of a quantum state with fewer copies than the $Θ(d^2)$ needed to run full state tomography. This is the first improvement to spectrum estimation over the influential Keyl-Werner algorithm, which uses $n = Θ(d^2)$ copies, thereby resolving a question raised by Keyl and Werner in 2001 and refuting a 2016 conjecture of Wright.
  Our main technical tool is a new tomography guarantee, where the error of tomography in a particular direction $|w\rangle$ scales with $\langle w | ρ|w\rangle$ for all directions simultaneously. From this stronger "relative-error" bound, we recover better algorithms for principal component analysis in Bures distance and tomography in $χ^2$-divergence as corollaries.

</details>


### [104] [Hardware-efficient erasure-error detection with an integer fluxonium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27123)
*Junyoung An,Helin Zhang,Jeffrey M. Gertler,Kate Azar,Renée DePencier Piñero,Michael Gingras,Junghyun Kim,Bethany M. Niedzielski,Ilan T. Rosen,Mollie E. Schwartz,Joel Î-j. Wang,Terry P. Orlando,Jeffrey A. Grover,Max Hays,Kyle Serniak,William D. Oliver*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文在整数磁通量子比特（integer fluxonium）中实现了擦除错误的转换与电路中段检测，利用|g⟩和|f⟩态编码逻辑信息、|e⟩态编码擦除信号。通过抑制直接|f⟩→|g⟩跃迁并将主导的|f⟩→|e⟩跃迁转换为可检测擦除，同时消除两逻辑态间的谐振频移，实现无辅助比特的电路中段擦除检查。实验结果显示：|f⟩态寿命提升8.4倍，Hahn回波时间提升1.38倍，单比特门错误率从0.061(2)%降至0.030(5)%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错中擦除错误检测可通过揭示错误发生的时间和位置来提升纠错效率。然而，现有方案通常需要额外辅助比特或复杂架构。本工作旨在利用整数fluxonium的能级结构特性，在单一量子比特中实现硬件高效的擦除转换与检测，避免辅助比特开销，同时提升逻辑态相干性能和门保真度。

Method: 采用整数fluxonium量子比特：以|g⟩和|f⟩作为逻辑态，|e⟩作为擦除态。关键设计包括：(1)利用整数fluxonium天然抑制|f⟩→|g⟩直接跃迁，使主导衰变通道|f⟩→|e⟩可被转换为可检测的擦除事件；(2)通过参数设计空间搜索，消除两逻辑态间的谐振频率偏移，从而可用同一读取谐振腔实现无辅助比特的电路中段擦除检查；(3)在电路中段执行擦除检测并丢弃检测到的擦除事件，以提升整体性能。

Result: 通过丢弃检测到的擦除事件：(1)|f⟩态寿命提升8.4倍；(2)Hahn回波时间提升1.38倍；(3)单比特门错误率从0.061(2)%降低至0.030(5)%。

Conclusion: 整数fluxonium被证明是一个硬件高效的擦除错误检测与转换平台。该方案无需辅助比特即可实现电路中段擦除检查，显著提升了相干时间和门保真度。作者同时指出，要构建具有高擦除偏置（erasure bias）的有效擦除量子比特，仍需进一步改进相关性能指标。

Abstract: Erasure-error detection can improve the efficiency of quantum error correction by revealing the times and locations of their error events. In this work, we demonstrate erasure conversions and mid-circuit erasure detections in a single integer fluxonium, in which the states $\mathrm{|g\rangle, |f\rangle}$ encode the logical states and $\mathrm{|e\rangle}$ encodes the erasure state. The integer fluxonium suppresses direct $|\mathrm{f} \rangle \rightarrow |\mathrm{g} \rangle$ transitions and allows the dominant $|\mathrm{f} \rangle \rightarrow |\mathrm{e}\rangle$ transitions to be converted into detectable erasures. Furthermore, we identified a design space that nullifies the resonant-frequency shift between the two logical states, enabling ancilla-free mid-circuit erasure checks using the same resonator employed for final readout. By discarding the detected erasure events, we achieved an 8.4-fold increase in the $|\mathrm{f}\rangle$ state lifetime, a 1.38-fold increase in the Hahn-echo time, and a reduction of single-qubit gate error from 0.061(2)% to 0.030(5)%. Our results establish integer fluxonium as a hardware-efficient platform for erasure-error detection and conversion, while identifying the improvements required to realize an effective erasure qubit with high erasure bias.

</details>


### [105] [Parity-Based Time-Bin Encoding Enabling SWAP Between Polarization and Time-Bin Qubits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27144)
*Adam Sultan,Connor Kupchak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出一种基于奇偶校验的时间-bin编码方案，使物理延迟可实现双向逻辑比特翻转，进而通过三个CNOT门级联实现偏振与时间-bin自由度之间的确定性SWAP操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统early/late时间-bin编码不支持从late到early的双向逻辑翻转，限制了多自由度光子量子处理中某些量子操作的实现。需要一个支持双向操作的原语来实现偏振与时间-bin自由度之间的SWAP。

Method: 引入奇偶校验时间-bin编码：逻辑|0⟩和|1⟩分别对应偶数倍和奇数倍的间隔Δt，使得Δt的物理延迟可实现|0⟩↔|1⟩的双向翻转。利用偏振控制延迟线实现CNOT_{P→T}，利用周期性折射率调制实现CNOT_{T→P}，三个CNOT门顺序级联构成确定性SWAP。并分析了EOM驱动电子学和光子探测对偏振旋转误差概率和时序分辨率的约束。

Result: 基于奇偶校验编码，成功实现了偏振→时间-bin和时间-bin→偏振的两个方向CNOT门，三个CNOT级联后可确定性地完成偏振与时间-bin自由度间的SWAP操作。同时给出了场基调制偏振旋转误差概率和时序分辨率约束的分析结果。

Conclusion: 奇偶校验时间-bin编码是实现偏振-时间bin自由度间确定性SWAP的关键使能技术，为多自由度光子量子处理架构提供了重要的路由原语。

Abstract: Multi-degree-of-freedom photonic quantum processing requires routing between degree-of-freedom (DOF) qubit encodings on a single photon. A SWAP between polarization and time-bin qubits is Multi-degree-of-freedom photonic quantum processing requires routing between degree-of-freedom (DOF) qubit encodings on a single photon. A SWAP between polarization and time-bin qubits is an advantageous primitive for such architectures, however conventional early/late time-bin encoding does not support bidirectional logical time-bin flips from late to early which limits the ability to implement certain quantum operations. We introduce a parity-based time-bin encoding in which logical $\vert 0 \rangle_T$ and $\vert 1 \rangle_T$ correspond to even and odd multiples of a spacing $Δt$, so that a physical delay of $Δt$ implements $\vert 0 \rangle_T \leftrightarrow \vert 1 \rangle_T$. This encoding is the enabling ingredient that makes a polarization-controlled delay line implement $\mathrm{CNOT}_{P \rightarrow T}$ and aligns naturally with periodic refractive index modulation for $\mathrm{CNOT}_{T \rightarrow P}$. Composing three such CNOT operations sequentially results in a deterministic SWAP between polarization and time-bin degrees of freedom. We analyze field-based modulation polarization-rotation error probability and timing-resolution constraints set by both EOM drive electronics and photon detection.

</details>


### [106] [Improved Methods for Determining Quantum Error Correcting Code Performance and Fault Tolerance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27153)
*Michael Mullan,Matthew Weippert,Winton Brown*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对量子纠错码在低错误率区域性能评估的难题，提出两种加速MCMC收敛的方法：剪枝算法消除可纠正错误，子区域MCMC通过调节每次重采样的错误比例来在蒙特卡洛与单步MCMC之间插值，实现更快收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠错码在低物理错误率下的性能无法直接通过蒙特卡洛模拟获得，需从高错误率外推。但外推的准确性依赖于对不可纠正错误模式最低权重的了解，而该最低权重常因实现细节而异且未知。Bravyi-Vargo的MCMC方法虽有效，但Metropolis算法仅对当前逻辑错误模式做微小改动，导致收敛缓慢。

Method: （1）剪枝算法：识别并移除典型错误模式中大量可纠正的错误分量，聚焦于恶性的低权重核心。（2）子区域MCMC：一种新的Metropolis-Hastings算法族，通过参数控制每步重采样的错误模式比例，在蒙特卡洛（全量重采样）与单步MCMC（极小改动）之间实现平滑插值。合理选择该参数可大幅加速收敛。

Result: 通过合理选择子区域MCMC的重采样比例参数，该方法相比Bravyi-Vargo原有的Metropolis算法实现了远更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: 利用错误模式中"恶性核心+大量可纠正错误"的结构特性，剪枝与子区域MCMC两种策略均能有效提升低错误率区域量子纠错码性能评估的效率与可行性。

Abstract: One of the central challenges in quantum error correction is determining the performance of a code in the low-error regimes needed to implement utility-scale computations. While performance at these error rates is not amenable to direct Monte Carlo simulation, it can be extrapolated from simulations at higher logical error rates, assuming the logical error rate scales predictably with increasing distance or decreasing physical error rate. However, the expected scaling depends sensitively on the minimum weight of uncorrectable error patterns. In many cases, the minimum weight is unknown since it depends not only on the theoretical code distance, but also on details of the implementation. Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) methods, as adapted to quantum error correction by Bravyi and Vargo, provide a way to estimate logical failure rates in these low-error regimes via simulation. While offering significant gains over Monte Carlo, the described Metropolis algorithm makes small changes to the current logical failure patterns which results in slow convergence. In this paper, we argue that typical failure patterns include a large number of easily correctable errors that coexist alongside a malignant core. This observation motivates two new approaches to better evaluate code performance. First, we describe a pruning algorithm designed to obviate these correctable errors and focus on the problematic low-weight core. Second, we develop a novel family of Metropolis-Hastings algorithms, referred to as subregion MCMC. This technique is parameterized by the fraction of the error pattern that is resampled at each step, effectively interpolating between Monte Carlo and single step MCMC. We show that a judicious choice of this parameter results in far faster convergence than prior work.

</details>


### [107] [Training Quantum Dragons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27168)
*Muhammad Yusf,Bhola Devkota,Mark A. Novotny,Gautam Rupak*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次在量子计算机上实现了非平衡格林函数（NEGF）方法，通过将NEGF散射问题转化为线性方程组，利用HHL和变分量子线性求解器（VQLS）算法计算量子龙纳米器件的透射系数，并在IBM量子处理器上进行了仿真和实际验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NEGF是纳米尺度电子输运的标准理论框架，但其计算复杂度高。量子计算有望加速此类问题，然而此前尚无NEGF的量子计算实现。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索量子计算在纳米电子学中的应用前景。

Method: 将NEGF散射问题重新表述为线性方程组Ax=b，其中解x编码透射和反射振幅。采用HHL算法和VQLS算法进行求解。研究对象为量子龙纳米器件（单带紧束缚模型），这类器件在全导带内具有完美透射特性，不受内部无序影响。通过相似变换将线性系统块对角化以降低Pauli分解复杂度。分别在2位点（3物理量子比特）和6位点（4物理量子比特）的龙器件上进行理想仿真、噪声感知仿真及IBM量子处理器实际计算。

Result: 成功在量子计算机上实现了NEGF计算。量子龙器件在2位点和6位点构型下分别仅需3个和4个物理量子比特，电路高度紧凑。噪声感知仿真结果与实际IBM量子处理器运行结果一致，验证了量子NEGF方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 首次证明了量子计算机可用于执行NEGF电子输运计算，为量子计算在纳米电子学领域的应用开辟了新途径。量子龙器件的特殊性质（完美透射）使其成为验证量子NEGF算法的理想测试平台。

Abstract: The Non-Equilibrium Green's Function (NEGF) is the standard formalism for nano-scale electron transport. By recasting the NEGF scattering problem as a linear system of equations whose solution encodes the transmission and reflection amplitudes, we present the first quantum computerized implementation of NEGF. We apply both the Harrow--Hassidim--Lloyd and Variational Quantum Linear Solver algorithms to compute the transmission coefficient $T(E)$ of quantum dragon nanodevices within the single-band tight-binding model. Quantum dragon devices exhibit perfect transmission across the full conducting band regardless of internal disorder. The problem maps onto compact circuits of 3 and 4 total physical qubits for the 2-site and 6-site dragon devices, respectively. A similarity transformation block-diagonalizes the NEGF linear system reducing the Pauli decomposition of the block-encoded matrix. We demonstrate the feasibility of quantum computation by performing ideal and noise-aware simulations and computations on physical IBM quantum processor.

</details>


### [108] [OQRAM: Oblivious Quantum Random Access Memory for Securing Delegated Quantum Queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27171)
*Shifan Xu,Yizhuo Tan,Yongshan Ding,Jakub Szefer*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了 oblivious QRAM（不经意量子随机存取存储器），一种保护隐私的委托式相干查询访问框架，通过加密与置换的数据库布局、量子安全伪随机置换或量子一次性密码本进行地址掩码，结合诱饵检测抵御恶意服务器，在保持适度量子开销的同时，相比全盲量子计算大幅降低了量子通信和资源需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在委托计算场景中，标准 QRAM 查询接口会向服务器泄露敏感信息（如访问模式），而现有的全盲量子计算方案虽然能解决隐私问题，但需要指数级量子资源。因此需要一种更轻量的密码学抽象，专门针对私有委托式 QRAM 访问进行隐私保护。

Method: 采用两阶段协议：离线刷新阶段和在线受保护查询阶段。数据库以加密且乱序的布局存储。在线查询通过相干地址掩码保护，具体有两种方案：(1) 基于量子安全伪随机置换（qPRP）的方法；(2) 基于量子一次性密码本（qOTP）的方法。qPRP变体还支持将数据库刷新分布到多次查询中以减少经典通信。针对恶意服务器，引入诱饵检测机制以增强隐私保护并实现概率性篡改检测。

Result: 在采用的客户端模型下，在线保护仅增加适度的量子开销（超出查询寄存器部分），避免了等效本地 QRAM 构造所需的指数级量子资源。与全盲量子计算相比，该框架在客户端和服务器端的量子资源需求均显著降低，且实现了量子通信量的指数级缩减。

Conclusion: oblivious QRAM 作为一种更轻量的密码学抽象，在委托式 QRAM 访问场景中实现了隐私保护与资源效率的有效平衡，为隐私保护的相干查询访问提供了实用化的解决方案。

Abstract: Quantum query is a basic subroutine in many quantum algorithms, and Quantum Random Access Memory (QRAM) provides a natural way to realize such coherent query access. In delegated settings, however, a standard QRAM query interface can expose sensitive information to the server. This paper introduces oblivious QRAM, a cryptographic abstraction for privacy-preserving delegated coherent query access. The protocol consists of an offline refresh phase and an online protected query phase. The database is stored in an encrypted and shuffled layout, and each query is protected by coherent address masking using either a quantum-secure pseudorandom permutation (qPRP) based method or a quantum one-time pad (qOTP) based method. In the adopted client model, the online protection adds only modest quantum overhead beyond the query register, avoiding the exponential quantum resources that would otherwise be required by an equivalent local QRAM construction. The qPRP-based variant also supports multi-query use by distributing database refresh across multiple queries to reduce classical communication. To address malicious servers, decoy checks are further incorporated to strengthen privacy protection and enable probabilistic tampering detection. Compared with fully blind quantum computing, this framework provides a lighter abstraction tailored to private delegated QRAM access, significantly reducing quantum resource requirements on both the client and server sides and achieving an exponential reduction in quantum communication.

</details>


### [109] [Classical and Quantum MacWilliams Transforms as Spin Kinematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27173)
*Eric Kubischta,Ian Teixeira*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示自旋是MacWilliams变换背后隐藏的统一引擎，通过将错误空间分裂为平凡和非平凡部分，将MacWilliams变换运动学地推导为两个规范基之间的Wigner-D旋转，并证明该旋转在改变码长时保持形式不变、仅以不同自旋表示重现，而在固定码长时改变旋转轴则可在经典与量子理论间迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示MacWilliams变换在经典编码、量子比特和量子dit权重枚举理论中表现出的"动物园"现象背后的深层统一原理，从而为这些看似不同的变换提供一个基于自旋的统一几何图像。

Method: 采用运动学方法，仅从错误空间分裂为平凡错误与非平凡错误这一基本结构出发，将MacWilliams变换构造为两个规范基之间的Wigner-D旋转。进一步分析码长n变化时旋转的不变性（以自旋n/2的不同表示重现），以及在固定n下旋转轴变化如何对应经典与量子理论间的迁移。

Result: 证明了MacWilliams变换可统一理解为Wigner-D旋转：改变码长n时，同一旋转元素在自旋n/2表示中重现；固定n时，旋转轴的不同选择对应经典码与各类量子码的MacWilliams变换。

Conclusion: 自旋结构是MacWilliams变换在经典与量子编码理论中普遍存在的根本原因，这一统一视角深化了对权重枚举理论代数结构的理解，并将经典与量子编码理论纳入同一几何框架。

Abstract: Spin is the hidden engine behind the zoo of MacWilliams transforms in weight enumerator theories - not only for qubits and qudits, but even for classical codes. From nothing more than a split into trivial and nontrivial errors, we kinematically derive the MacWilliams transform as a Wigner-$D$ rotation between two canonical bases. Within each classical and quantum theory, changing the length $n$ leaves the rotation untouched: the same element simply reappears at spin $n/2$. And at fixed $n$, changing the rotation axis simply moves between the various classical and quantum theories.

</details>


### [110] [Reconfigurable Optical Platform for One-way Quantum Communication Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27181)
*Francesco Mazzoncini,Hugo Defienne,Romain Alléaume,Sylvain Gigan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多模光纤与波前整形的可重构光学平台，用于实现单向量子通信复杂度任务，并通过实验验证了一个已知存在指数级量子-经典分离的协议，同时数值模拟表明该架构可推广至更高维度和更通用的通信任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算优越性的实验演示仍面临巨大技术挑战，而通信复杂度领域为在当前光子平台上展示量子优势提供了一条更有前景的路径。作者旨在构建一个实用、可重构的硬件平台，以实现单向量子通信复杂度协议，从而推动量子优势的实际演示。

Method: 利用多模光纤中的模式复用特性，结合空间光调制器进行波前整形，构建了一套可重构的单向量子通信复杂度实验平台。实验上实现了一个已知存在指数量子-经典通信分离的单向量子通信复杂度问题；数值上通过互补仿真验证了该解码架构对更通用通信任务的支持能力，并探索了无需增加硬件复杂度即可实现高维扩展的路径。

Result: 实验成功验证了指数量子-经典通信分离的单向协议；数值模拟表明同一可重构解码架构在性能相当的前提下可支持更通用的单向通信任务，且能在不增加硬件复杂度的情况下实现向更高维度的扩展。

Conclusion: 多模光纤波前整形平台是单向通信复杂度研究的理想硬件载体，为实现更强量子-经典分离的实用化量子优势演示提供了清晰的路线图。

Abstract: Demonstrating a practical quantum advantage remains a central goal in quantum information science. While quantum computational supremacy is still technologically demanding, communication complexity offers a promising route to showcase quantum advantage with current photonic platforms. Here we introduce a reconfigurable optical platform for one-way quantum communication complexity based on multimode fibers and wavefront shaping. We experimentally validate it by implementing a genuine one-way quantum communication complexity problem for which an exponential quantum--classical communication separation is known. Complementary numerical simulations show that the same reconfigurable decoding architecture can support more general one-way communication tasks with comparable performance, while also offering a route to higher-dimensional implementations without increasing hardware complexity. Together, these results establish multimode-fiber wavefront shaping as a versatile hardware platform for one-way quantum communication complexity and provide a concrete roadmap toward more demanding protocols, where stronger quantum--classical separations could enable practical demonstrations of quantum advantage.

</details>


### [111] [Robust quantum state certification and uncertainty principles for total influence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27184)
*Andrea Coladangelo,Jerry Li,Joseph Slote*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明，对于几乎所有目标态，仅需非自适应单量子比特泡利测量即可判定未知量子态是否接近目标态。测试使用信息论最优的拷贝数。核心技术是一个关于布尔函数加权总影响的测不准原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子态认证（quantum state certification）通常需要复杂的联合测量。本文试图回答：能否仅用最简单的非自适应单量子比特泡利测量来完成此任务？目标是建立一种对几乎所有目标态都有效、且拷贝数达到信息论最优的测试方案。

Method: 构建一个测试器：随机选择泡利可观测量进行非自适应单量子比特测量，利用测量结果估计加权总影响（weighted total influence）。核心创新是证明了一个关于超立方体上布尔函数的"测不准原理"——即函数及其Fourier变换的总影响之和为Ω(n)，并将其推广到Glauber动力学下的Dirichlet能量加权情形。该不等式将统计距离与可观测量期望值联系起来，从而只需估计泡利期望值即可判定量子态的接近程度。

Result: 对于除2^{-Ω(n)}比例外的所有n量子比特目标态|ψ⟩，测试器使用O(ε^{-2} log(1/δ))个ρ的拷贝，以置信度1-δ判断ρ是ε-接近还是Ω(ε)-远离|ψ⟩。此拷贝数即使在允许任意联合测量的协议中也是信息论最优的。

Conclusion: 非自适应单量子比特泡利测量足以实现近乎通用的量子态认证，突破了此前需要纠缠测量的局限。加权总影响的测不准原理作为技术核心，在布尔函数分析与量子信息之间建立了深刻联系。

Abstract: We show that nonadaptive single-qubit Pauli measurements suffice to test whether an unknown $n$-qubit state $ρ$ is $\varepsilon$-close to or $O(\varepsilon)$-far from an ideal target state $|ψ\rangle$, for all but a $2^{-Ω(n)}$ fraction of target states. The test uses $O(\varepsilon^{-2}\log(1/δ))$ copies of $ρ$ to achieve confidence $1-δ$, which is information-theoretically optimal even among protocols with arbitrary joint measurements.
  The main technical innovation is an uncertainty principle for weighted generalizations of the total influence of Boolean functions. As a simple example, the unweighted variant states that $\mathbf{Inf}[f]+\mathbf{Inf}[\widehat{f}] = Ω(n)$, which is a natural hypercube analogue of the Heisenberg uncertainty principle (here $\widehat{\,\cdot\,}$ denotes the $2^{-n/2}$-normalized Fourier transform). The weighted case generalizes $\mathbf{Inf}[\,\cdot\,]$ and $\mathbf{Inf}[\,\widehat{\,\cdot\,}\,]$ to Dirichlet energies associated with Glauber dynamics for certain dual measures on the cube.

</details>


### [112] [Sharp Bounds on Ground State Energy of the SYK Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27185)
*Arpon Basu,Pravesh K. Kothari,Siddhant Midha*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文严格证明了SYK哈密顿量算子范数的期望值为$(1-o(1))\sqrt{2n}/k$（对超常数$k \leq o(\sqrt{n})$），并利用一个显式线性算子$\mathsf{x}$将期望迹矩问题转化为Johnson方案的谱边问题，从而确认了García-García等人的预测，并将结果推广至稀疏SYK，最终得到Basso等人量子算法可计算基态能量的推论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: García-García, Jia和Verbaarschot'18曾对SYK哈密顿量的算子范数做出预测，Feng, Tian和Wei'19则明确提出了证明该预测的问题。本文旨在严格证明这一预测，为随机矩阵理论与量子多体物理的交叉领域提供坚实的数学基础。

Method: 核心技巧是构造一个显式的确定性线性算子$\mathsf{x}$，使得$\mathsf{x}^{2\ell}$的某个固定二次型恰好等于SYK哈密顿量的期望迹矩（对所有$n,k$成立）。该算子可视为超图超边上玻色子的“扭曲”模型。问题归结为求$\mathsf{x}$的谱边，而该谱边由Johnson方案中一个${n \choose k}$维矩阵的谱主导，可用已知结果直接计算。为证明界是紧的，作者构造了一个见证态，将其大二次型转化为$H_{\operatorname{SYK}}$最大二次型的下界证明。

Result: 证明了$\mathbb{E}\|H_{\operatorname{SYK}}\|_{\operatorname{op}} = (1 - o(1))\sqrt{2n}/k$对超常数$k \leq o(\sqrt{n})$成立；结果推广至稀疏SYK；推论出Basso, Chen和Dalzell'24的耗散量子算法可对所有$k<\sqrt{n}/4$计算SYK基态能量至$O(1)$乘法因子。

Conclusion: 作者通过巧妙的算子构造将SYK哈密顿量的随机平均问题转化为Johnson方案的确定性谱分析，严格证明了算子范数渐近公式，解决了领域内的一个开放问题，并为量子算法在SYK模型上的性能提供了理论保证。

Abstract: We study the Sachdev-Ye-Kitaev (SYK) Hamiltonian $H_{\operatorname{SYK}}$ on $n$ Majorana modes with $k$-body interactions, and prove that $\mathbb{E}\|H_{\operatorname{SYK}}\|_{\operatorname{op}} = (1 - o(1))\cdot\sqrt{2n}/k$ for super-constant $k\leq o(\sqrt{n})$, where the expectation is over the disorder variables in the Hamiltonian. This confirms the predictions due to Garcia-Garcia, Jia and Verbaarschot'18 and answers a question posed in Feng, Tian and Wei'19. Our results extend to the sparse SYK Hamiltonian. As a corollary, we obtain that the dissipative quantum algorithm of Basso, Chen and Dalzell'24 provably computes the ground state energy of the SYK Hamiltonian up to an $O(1)$-multiplicative factor for all $k < \sqrt{n}/4$.
  Our key technical idea is identifying an explicit, deterministic linear operator $\mathsf{x}$ such that a fixed quadratic form of $\mathsf{x}^{2\ell}$ exactly equals the expected trace moments of the SYK Hamiltonian for every $n$ and $k$. This linear operator can be naturally viewed as a \emph{twisted} model of bosons on the space of hyperedges of a hypergraph. The problem thus reduces to identifying the spectral edge of $\mathsf{x}$, which we show is dominated by the spectrum of a natural ${n \choose k}$-dimensional matrix from the \emph{Johnson} scheme and is straightforward to compute using known results.
  To show that our bound is sharp, we construct a witness state with a large quadratic form on $\mathsf{x}$ and transform it into a certificate of a lower bound on the largest quadratic form on $H_{\operatorname{SYK}}$.

</details>


### [113] [Very Strong Irreversibility of Quantum Entanglement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27195)
*Tulja Varun Kondra,Raphael Brinster,Hermann Kampermann,Dagmar Bruß,Nikolai Wyderka*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了量子纠缠操纵的不可逆性在非纠缠操作下依然存在，且任何恢复可逆性的尝试都会导致随拷贝数指数增长的误差。作者在指数强逆蒸馏纠缠与指数强逆纠缠成本之间建立了严格分离，解决了Lami和Regula提出的猜想，并将不可逆性推广到多项式增长误差层面，同时给出了半定规划下界及CPT保持操作下的解析可解族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子纠缠操纵中已知存在不可逆性——混合纠缠态的制备需要纯纠缠，但无法通过LOCC从中回收纯纠缠。这一不可逆性即使在最大类别的非纠缠操作下依然存在，揭示了纠缠理论与热力学之间的本质区别。本文旨在探究：试图恢复可逆性是否会必然导致随拷贝数指数增长的误差？这源于Lami和Regula (Nat. Phys. 2023) 提出的猜想。

Method: 作者通过构建具体案例，证明指数强逆蒸馏纠缠与指数强逆纠缠成本之间存在严格分离。技术上引入了强逆指数（strong converse exponent）分析框架，在半定规划（SDP）下推导非纠缠操作下指数强逆成本的下界。同时，针对完全PPT保持操作类，构造了具有解析可解性的反对称态族，展示了指数强逆不可逆性。

Result: 成功解决了Lami和Regula的猜想，并进一步强化——证明纠缠不可逆性即使在多项式增长的误差水平下依然存在。在非纠缠操作下给出指数强逆成本的SDP下界；在完全PPT保持操作下，构造出反对称态的解析可解族以展示指数强逆不可逆性。该结果在LOCC这类更受限操作下目前尚无类似分离结果。

Conclusion: 纠缠理论的不可逆性具有极强的鲁棒性：不仅无法通过增大操作类别（非纠缠操作）来规避，而且任何试图恢复可逆性的努力都会付出指数级代价。这一结果在多项式误差水平上同样成立，深刻区分了纠缠理论与热力学在可逆性上的本质差异。

Abstract: The manipulation of quantum entanglement is fundamentally irreversible: some mixed entangled states require pure entanglement for their preparation, although no pure entanglement can be recovered from them by local operations and classical communication. This irreversibility is known to persist even under the maximal class of operations that do not generate entanglement, revealing a fundamental distinction between entanglement theory and thermodynamics. We construct cases for which any attempt to restore reversibility necessarily incurs an error that increases exponentially with the number of copies. Technically, we demonstrate a strict separation between the exponential strong-converse distillable entanglement and the exponential strong-converse entanglement cost. Our result resolves a conjecture posed by Lami and Regula (Nat. Phys. 19, 184-189 (2023)) and strengthens it by showing that the irreversibility of entanglement persists even at the level of polynomially (in the number of copies) growing error. We further derive a semidefinite-programming lower bound on the exponential strong-converse cost under non-entangling operations. Finally, for the class of completely PPT-preserving operations, we construct analytically solvable families of antisymmetric states exhibiting the exponential strong-converse irreversibility. Remarkably, to our knowledge, no analogous separation between exponential strong converse cost and the analogous distillable entanglement is currently known even under the more restrictive class of LOCC operations.

</details>


### [114] [Fault-Tolerant Logical Operations and Efficient State Preparation in Modular Quantum Architectures with Noisy Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27204)
*Siddardha Chelluri,Riccardo Mengoni,Tom Darras,Julien Laurat,Eleni Diamanti,Ioannis Lavdas*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 研究了模块化量子计算架构中，接口错误和QPU内部错误对容错计算的影响。通过电路级仿真分析了基于旋转表面码的分布式CNOT门逻辑错误率，发现接口可容忍比内部高一个数量级的噪声。还提出了制备分布式GHZ态的高效协议，将辅助量子比特最小化归约为顶点覆盖问题并给出启发式算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模块化量子计算是超越单片设备限制的主流范式，但模块间接口的噪声对容错计算的影响尚未被充分量化研究。需要在电路级别理解接口噪声对逻辑错误率和容错阈值的影响，为可扩展量子计算提供定量依据。

Method: 采用旋转表面码编码量子比特，通过格点手术（lattice surgery）在有噪Bell对连接的QPU之间实现非局域CNOT门，进行电路级仿真分析逻辑错误率；将分布式GHZ态制备中的辅助量子比特最小化问题归约为图上的顶点覆盖问题，并提出多项式时间启发式算法求解低开销方案。

Result: 接口可容忍比QPU内部噪声高约一个数量级的噪声，且对容错阈值仅有轻微降低；提出的GHZ态制备协议显著减少了辅助量子比特开销、时间和非局域Bell对消耗。

Conclusion: 分布式量子纠错能够在模块化架构中实现可扩展、容错的量子计算，接口噪声容限较高为实际工程实现提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Modular quantum computing is a leading paradigm for scaling quantum computation beyond the resource limitations of monolithic devices. In this architecture, multiple quantum processing units (QPUs), employing identical or distinct qubit modalities, are interconnected via shared entanglement. Here, we investigate how errors at module interfaces and within individual QPUs affect fault-tolerant computation when qubits are encoded using the rotated surface code. Going beyond the logical-memory benchmark, we perform circuit-level simulations of fault-tolerant nonlocal CNOT gates implemented via lattice surgery between QPUs connected by noisy Bell pairs, and analyze the resulting logical error rates. Our results show that interfaces can tolerate noise up to an order of magnitude higher than intra-QPU noise, with only a minor reduction in the fault-tolerance threshold. We further develop an efficient protocol for preparing distributed fault-tolerant logical GHZ states, reducing ancilla overhead, time, and nonlocal Bell-pair consumption. We show that ancilla minimization in this setting is equivalent to a vertex-cover problem on an associated graph, and introduce a polynomial-time heuristic algorithm for finding low-overhead solutions. Our results provide quantitative evidence that distributed quantum error correction can enable scalable, fault-tolerant quantum computation in modular architectures.

</details>


### [115] [Practical Quantum Topological Data Analysis with Applications to High-Dimensional Feature Extraction and Time Series Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27206)
*Jason Iaconis,Sayonee Ray,Samwel Sekwao,Claudio Girotto,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出将量子拓扑数据分析（TDA）重新定位为一种面向下游数据分析的特征提取方法，通过从组合拉普拉斯矩阵中提取低阶谱信息作为高维拓扑的代理。作者从应用（fMRI疾病分类、金融时间序列分析）和算法（基于矩的量子算法）两方面论证了该方法的可行性，并给出了电路构造、资源估算及离子阱量子系统的实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典TDA计算高维拓扑特征（如Betti数）的成本极高，而现有量子TDA方法多聚焦于精确或高精度Betti数估计，导致实用量子优势的窗口显得狭窄。作者试图突破这一瓶颈，将量子TDA重新定义为特征提取工具，以低阶谱信息替代完整高维拓扑计算，从而拓宽量子TDA的实用前景。

Method: 1. 应用验证：在两个时间序列任务（fMRI神经退行性疾病分类、金融市场不稳定性识别）上验证高阶TDA特征的预测性能提升；2. 算法设计：开发基于矩（moment-based）的量子算法，论证低阶矩（包括相对迹）与高维Betti信息之间存在强相关性，即使相对Betti数较小时亦然；3. 实验实现：在类似IonQ Tempo的Barium离子阱开发系统上进行电路构造、资源估算、量子-经典交叉预测及实验验证，从图实例中提取拉普拉斯可观测量并与精确Betti信息定量比较。

Result: 1. 高阶TDA特征在fMRI分析和金融时间序列两个应用中均提升了预测性能；2. 低阶矩（如相对迹）与高维Betti信息高度相关，验证了以低阶谱信息代理高维拓扑的可行性；3. 在离子阱量子硬件上成功提取了拉普拉斯可观测量，并与精确Betti信息进行了定量比对，给出了量子-经典交叉的资源估算。

Conclusion: 量子TDA可作为一种实用的拓扑特征提取方法，通过低阶谱信息有效代理高维拓扑特征，从而在经典计算困难的数据场景中实现量子优势，为量子机器学习与拓扑数据分析的交叉应用开辟了可行路径。

Abstract: Topological data analysis (TDA) provides a powerful framework for extracting information about the shape of complex, unstructured data, but the classical cost of computing high dimensional topological features limits its application. Quantum algorithms for TDA offer a route around this bottleneck, yet existing approaches typically focus on exact or high precision Betti number estimation, making the regime for practical quantum advantage appear narrow. Here, we instead frame quantum TDA as a feature-extraction method for downstream data analysis by extracting low-order spectral information from the combinatorial Laplacian as a proxy for high-dimensional topology.
  We support this perspective from both the application and algorithmic sides. First, we show that higher-order TDA features improve predictive performance in two time-series applications: functional MRI analysis for neurodegenerative disease classification and financial time-series analysis for identifying market instability. Second, we develop a moment-based quantum algorithm and show that low-order moments, including the relative trace, are strongly correlated with high-dimensional Betti information, even when the relative Betti number is small. Finally, we present circuit constructions, resource estimates, quantum-classical crossover projections, and experimental results from a Barium development system similar to the forthcoming IonQ Tempo line, extracting Laplacian-derived observables from graph instances and quantitatively comparing them with exact Betti information.
  Together, these results establish quantum TDA as a practical approach for extracting topological features from classically challenging data

</details>


<div id='physics.plasm-ph'></div>

# physics.plasm-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Coupling model of metallic target ablation-plasma evolution-radiation under nanosecond laser irradiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26081)
*Ying Zhou,Jian Wu,Hao Sun,Jinghui Li,Xiaoxuan Li,Shuzhi Huang,Jiayao He,Xingyu Liu,Yuhua Hang,Cuixiang Pei,Xingwen Li*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个针对纯铁的全链条自洽多物理场耦合模型，统一描述了纳秒激光烧蚀过程中从激光能量沉积、相变、等离子体膨胀到光谱辐射的完整演化。模拟揭示了等离子体屏蔽效应、早期烧蚀产物的超音速输运机制（占81.6%），并成功复现了等离子体羽流从高温高电离态到低温中性原子态的转变及其辐射特征动态演化。通过与实验光谱及PrismSPECT、NIST LIBS程序的定量对比，该模型在多个通道上取得了最高综合评分，验证了全链条自洽建模在描述等离子体非均匀性与辐射输运方面的必要性和优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳秒激光与金属相互作用涉及多个复杂物理过程，现有研究难以用一个自洽模型统一描述所有阶段。因此，需要构建一个能够覆盖从激光能量沉积到光谱辐射全链条的自洽多物理场耦合模型，以准确捕捉等离子体演化及其辐射特性。

Method: 以纯铁为对象，建立多物理场耦合模型，涵盖激光能量沉积、固-液相变、气-液界面动力学输运、等离子体膨胀与电离、光谱辐射等过程。数值求解采用分区方法：靶材侧使用隐式紧致差分格式，等离子体侧使用Mac-Cormack显式格式。

Result: 1）揭示了等离子体屏蔽效应及其对蒸发过程的抑制作用，证实早期烧蚀产物中81.6%通过超音速膨胀模式输运；2）成功捕捉了等离子体羽流从高温高电离态到低温中性原子态的完整演化；3）光谱计算显示了辐射特征从早期强连续背景、离子线主导到后期连续背景衰减、原子线凸显及自吸收出现的动态演化；4）与实验光谱及PrismSPECT、NIST LIBS程序的定量对比中，本模型在多个通道上取得最高综合评分。

Conclusion: 全链条自洽建模方法在描述等离子体非均匀性和辐射输运方面具有必要性和优越性。该模型能够有效捕捉等离子体空间非均匀性导致的光学厚度效应（自吸收现象），并通过系统性定量评估验证了其相较于现有光谱程序在综合性能上的优势。

Abstract: The interaction of nanosecond laser pulses with metallic materials involves multiple complex physical processes. It is challenging to construct a self-consistent model capable of uniformly describing all stages. This work establishes a multi-physics coupling model for pure iron, encompassing laser energy deposition, solid-liquid phase transition, gas-liquid interfacial kinetic transport, plasma expansion and ionization, and spectral radiation. The numerical solution adopts a partition method, utilizing an implicit compact difference scheme for the target and a Mac-Cormack explicit scheme for the plasma. The simulations elucidate the emergence of plasma shielding and its inhibitory effect on the evaporation process, thereby confirming that 81.6% of the early-stage ablation products are transported through a supersonic expansion mode. The model successfully captures the complete evolution of the plasma plume from a high-temperature, highly ionized state to a low-temperature, neutral atomic state. Based on this, spectral calculations demonstrate the dynamic evolution of radiative characteristics from an early stage featuring a strong continuum background dominated by ion lines to a later stage where the continuum attenuates, atomic lines become prominent, and self-absorption appears. The emergence of self-absorption proves the ability of the model to effectively capture the optical thickness effects arising from spatial inhomogeneity within the plasma. Through systematic comparison between experimentally measured spectra and calculated results from the PrismSPECT and NIST LIBS spectral programs, the model presented here achieves the highest comprehensive scores in quantitative evaluations of multiple channels. This validates the necessity and superiority of the full-chain self-consistent modeling approach, especially in describing plasma inhomogeneity and radiation transport.

</details>


### [117] [SPECTRE: A robust solver for 3D equilibria with arbitrary topology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27135)
*E. Balkovic,J. Loizu,E. Lanti,C. Smiet,C. Lazzati,R. Ramasamy,A. Goodman,J. Geiger,J. P. Graves*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: SPECTRE 是一个基于多区域松弛 MHD 模型的新型三维平衡代码，通过改进的力公式和信赖域最小二乘优化，能够稳健地计算包含磁面、磁岛和混沌场的复杂拓扑结构三维平衡，在真空和有限 β 条件下均得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 MRxMHD 求解器 SPEC 在计算具有复杂磁拓扑（如磁岛、混沌场）的三维平衡时存在性能和鲁棒性不足，亟需一个更快速、更稳定的求解工具。

Method: SPECTRE 基于多区域松弛 MHD 模型，采用新的力公式和稳定的信赖域最小二乘极小化方案，支持固定边界和自由边界模式，能够处理磁面、磁岛和混沌场并存的一般磁拓扑。

Result: 在真空和有限 β 的固定/自由边界模式下验证：成功复现准轴对称装置的真空平衡、W7-X 自由边界真空平衡、经典仿星器有限 β 自由边界平衡，以及在优化准等动力学有限 β 构型中计算出与 HINT 结果一致的带芯部磁岛的强成形平衡。

Conclusion: SPECTRE 作为 SPEC 的改进版本，在计算速度、稳定性和适用范围上均有显著提升，为复杂三维磁约束等离子体平衡研究提供了可靠的工具。

Abstract: We present SPECTRE, a new equilibrium code based on the Multi-Region relaxed MHD model for robustly calculating 3D equilibria with general magnetic topology, allowing for flux surfaces, magnetic islands, and chaos. The code builds on a previous MRxMHD solver, SPEC, but performs significantly better thanks to a new formulation of force, the use of a stable trust-region-based least squares minimization scheme, as well as several additional features. SPECTRE is verified through application to configurations with known equilibrium solutions, in vacuum and with finite beta, both in the fixed boundary and the free boundary mode. Notably, these include vacuum equilibria of a quasi-axisymmetric (QA) device in fixed-boundary mode, and of W7-X in the free-boundary mode, along with a classical stellarator finite-beta free-boundary case. Finally, the solver is applied to a modern optimized finite-beta quasi-isodynamic (QI) configuration, where we demonstrate calculation of a strongly-shaped equilibrium with a core island which is in agreement with a HINT calculation.

</details>

### [118] [Scale-dependent universality class crossover in magnetic skyrmion polymers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26289)
*R. L. Silva,R. C. Silva,R. L. Stamps*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 磁性斯格明子链的统计力学呈现出独特的幂律温度依赖性，其指数在单键极限下为1、三键极限下为1/2，且该行为对磁场强度和相互作用的具体形式具有鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶极磁性斯格明子可自组装成交替螺旋度的链状结构，但其统计力学行为违背了在肌动蛋白、DNA和微管等经典一维聚合物中普遍观察到的普适谐振标度律，需要从第一性原理出发揭示其背后的物理机制。

Method: (1) 从第一性原理计算斯格明子间的对势，得到具有两个特征衰减长度的双指数形式竞争相互作用；(2) 采用多尺度模拟研究温度依赖性；(3) 分析蠕虫链极限（单键）和三键极限下的幂律行为，并检验磁场强度和相互作用具体形式（如Morse势或双Yukawa势）的影响。

Result: 发现温度呈幂律依赖：单键极限下指数为1，三键极限下指数为1/2；该幂律行为对磁场强度不敏感；竞争径向相互作用导致四次横向约束；Morse或双Yukawa等相互作用形式不影响温度依赖关系。

Conclusion: 斯格明子链的统计力学由竞争相互作用的双指数特征决定，呈现出与经典一维聚合物截然不同的幂律标度，其核心在于竞争径向相互作用引入的四次横向约束，且该行为具有普适性——不受磁场和势函数具体形式的显著影响。

Abstract: Dipolar magnetic skyrmions can assemble into chains with alternating helicity that act as one-dimensional polymers, yet their statistical mechanics violates the universal harmonic scaling observed in actin, DNA, and microtubules. From first principles, we compute the inter-skyrmion pair potential and find a bi-exponential form of competing interactions with two characteristic decay lengths that encode the distinct microscopic mechanisms of repulsion and attraction. Multiscale simulations reveal a power-law temperature dependence with exponent $1$ in the worm-chain limit of a single bond, and exponent $1/2$ in the three-bond limit. We find that the power-law behavior is remarkably independent of magnetic field strength, and the crossover is due to competing radial interactions responsible for the bonds, resulting in a quartic transverse confinement. We show that the precise form of the competing interactions (e.g., Morse or double-Yukawa) does not affect the temperature dependence.

</details>

### [119] [Where to Find ICEC? - Screening 2442 Systems for Interparticle Coulombic Electron Capture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26116)
*Fabian L. Hofmann,Elena M. Jahr,Elke Fasshauer*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文对原子-原子和原子-分子体系中的粒子间库仑电子俘获（ICEC）进行了全面筛选，覆盖2442种组合，利用渐近近似方法预测截面和电子能谱，识别出多类具有良好ICEC性能的候选体系，指出其在大气和天体化学环境中的潜在相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ICEC是一种环境辅助的电子附着途径，可与光复合竞争，但此前缺乏对原子-原子和原子-分子体系的系统性筛选，亟需找到适合未来实验研究的有前景的候选体系。

Method: 采用高效的渐近近似方法计算ICEC截面和电子能谱；纳入分子内核运动效应，但忽略粒子间核动力学以支持大规模筛选。共筛选2442种原子-原子和原子-分子组合。

Result: 识别出多类ICEC截面和ICEC-光复合比均较优的体系，包括卤素-卤化物体系，以及质子与双原子分子（N₂、O₂、CO、NO）的组合。

Conclusion: ICEC在大气和天体化学环境中可能具有重要相关性，筛选出的候选体系为未来实验验证提供了明确方向。

Abstract: Interparticle Coulombic Electron Capture (ICEC) provides an environment-assisted pathway for electron attachment that can compete with photorecombination. Here, we present the first comprehensive survey of ICEC in atom-atom and atom-molecule systems, screening 2442 combinations in search of promising candidates for future experimental investigation.
  We employ the efficient asymptotic approximation to predict ICEC cross sections and electron spectra. Intramolecular nuclear motion is included, while interparticle nuclear dynamics is neglected to permit an extensive survey.
  We identify several classes of atom-atom and atom-molecule systems with favourable ICEC cross sections and ICEC-to-photorecombination ratios, including halogen-halide systems and systems involving a proton and diatomic molecules such as N$_2$, O$_2$, CO, and NO. These findings point to atmospheric and astrochemical environments in which ICEC may be relevant.

</details>

### [120] [Machine-Checked Certificates for the Geometric Half of the Minimum Kochen-Specker Bound](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26413)
*Shayaan Siddique,Ibrahim Mian*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文弥合了Kochen-Specker定理中最小向量系统下界证明的几何部分缺乏可检查证明对象的缺陷。作者引入精确有理数"案例树证书"来证明图的非嵌入性，并用Python和Lean 4两种独立检查器重放验证，最终将每条非嵌入性结果形式化为Lean内核中可判定的封闭定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最佳下界（R³中24个向量）的证明依赖计算手段：组合部分可生成DRAT证明，但几何部分（用Z3的非线性实数算术判定数千候选图的非嵌入性）不产生可检查的证明对象。这削弱了整个证明的可信度，作者旨在为证明的"阻塞数据库"弥合这一可信性缺口。

Method: 作者提出精确有理数的"案例树证书"（case-tree certificates），其分裂节点基于多项式因式分解和有理数平方和分解，叶节点通过单射性、理想成员性（ideal-membership）或Positivstellensatz形式的正性论证来消解。在此基础上，为已发布流水线中阶10至阶13的全部291行源代码（180个不同图）生成了证书。验证方面：构建了两个与生成器无共享代码的独立检查器——一个基于纯Python的精确分数重放器，以及一个在Lean 4中实现并证明可靠性的总检查器。可靠定理（接受证书意味着不存在单射且正交的实数向量赋值实现该图）经过内核检查，且无GCD有理数算术层使整个判定计算可内核归约，最终每条图的非嵌入性结果都是通过decide策略证明的封闭内核定理。

Result: 成功为所有目标图生成了可检查证书。形式化过程还揭示了已发布流水线中的若干问题：(1)嵌入性概念中存在一个关键的"承载性"单射侧条件；(2)存在对Z3工作流不可见的隐藏WLOG（不失一般性）情形的义务；(3)发现了一个与已发布产物不一致的不可复现的候选图计数。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为Kochen-Specker最小向量系统下界证明的几何部分提供了完整、可机器检查的证明对象，显著提升了整个下界结果的可信度。所有证书、检查器和证明均可从单一构建中获取和重放，实现了完全可复现的形式化验证。

Abstract: The best known lower bound for the minimum Kochen-Specker vector system in $\mathbb{R}^3$ -- 24 vectors -- rests on a computational proof whose combinatorial half emits DRAT proofs but whose geometric half does not: the non-embeddability of thousands of candidate graphs is established by Z3's nonlinear real arithmetic, which produces no checkable proof objects. We close this gap for the proof's blocking database. We introduce exact rational case-tree certificates of real non-embeddability, whose splits are polynomial factorizations and rational sum-of-squares decompositions and whose leaves are discharged by injectivity, ideal-membership, or Positivstellensatz-shaped positivity arguments, and we certify all 291 source lines (180 distinct graphs) of the published pipeline's order-10 to order-13 blocking lists. Certificates are replayed by two independent checkers that share no code with the generator: a pure-Python replay over exact fractions, and a total checker implemented and proved sound in Lean 4. The soundness theorem -- acceptance implies that no injective-on-rays, orthogonality-respecting assignment of nonzero real vectors realizes the graph -- is kernel-checked with axiom closure {propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound}, and a gcd-free rational arithmetic layer makes the entire verdict computation kernel-reducible, so each per-graph non-embeddability result is a closed kernel theorem proved by decide. The formalization surfaced findings about the published pipeline, including a load-bearing injectivity side condition in its embeddability notion, hidden WLOG case obligations invisible to Z3-based workflows, and an unreproducible candidate count that we resolve against the published artifacts. All certificates, checkers, and proofs are available and replayable from a single build.

</details>

### [121] [Strong imposition of Dirichlet boundary velocities in structure-preserving discretizations of elastodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26248)
*Cristobal Ponce,Hector Ramirez,Yongxin Wu,Ning Liu,Yann Le Gorrec*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于加性运动学分解的升阶方法，将位移和速度场分解为边界为零的相对动态分量和向域内延伸的预设升阶函数，从而在有限元离散后将端口哈密顿系统保持为ODE形式并可强施加Dirichlet边界速度，同时证明了该方法在特定形函数下等价于经典矩阵分块方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 端口哈密顿弹性动力学有限元模型中，施加边界速度通常依赖拉格朗日乘子导致DAE系统，而弱施加方法则常在Dirichlet边界处精度不足。需要一种既能保持ODE结构、又能精确强施加边界条件的方法。

Method: 在连续层面引入加性运动学分解：将位移/速度场分解为在边界上消失的"相对动态分量"和延伸至域内的"预设升阶函数"。该分解诱导出一个分布式端口，将边界驱动效应映射到域内。将此映射融入虚功率原理，导出升阶端口哈密顿系统模型，有限元离散后自然退化为ODE系统并强施加Dirichlet边界速度。分别推导了适用于2场和4场PHS几何表示的公式，并证明离散模型中的升阶框架等价于经典计算力学中的代数矩阵分块方法。

Result: 通过数值仿真验证了所提方法的能量平衡特性和计算性能。证明了在特定形函数下，升阶框架可恢复标准FEM格式，升阶法等价于经典矩阵分块技术。

Conclusion: 所提出的升阶运动学分解框架成功解决了端口哈密顿系统有限元模型中边界速度施加的DAE困境，既保持了ODE结构又实现了Dirichlet边界的强施加，具有良好的能量一致性和计算性能，且与经典计算力学实践兼容。

Abstract: The imposition of boundary velocities in finite element models of port-Hamiltonian elastodynamics typically relies on Lagrange multipliers, yielding Differential-Algebraic Equations (DAEs). Alternatively, weak imposition methods that maintain an Ordinary Differential Equation (ODE) structure often exhibit poor accuracy at Dirichlet boundaries. To address these limitations, this paper introduces an additive kinematic decomposition at the continuous level, splitting the displacement and velocity fields into a relative dynamic component that vanishes on the boundary and a prescribed lifting function extending into the interior domain. This decomposition induces a distributed port that maps the effects of the boundary actuation inside the domain. By incorporating this mapping into suitable virtual power principles, we derive lifted port-Hamiltonian system (PHS) models that, upon finite element discretization, reduce to ODE systems in which Dirichlet boundary velocities are strongly imposed. The framework is applied to derive 2-field and 4-field formulations suited to distinct PHS geometric representations. Furthermore, we show that under specific shape functions, standard FEM schemes are recovered, demonstrating that the lifting framework in the discrete models is equivalent to the classic algebraic matrix partitioning in computational mechanics practice. The energy-balance properties and computational performance of the proposed methodology are verified through numerical simulations.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [122] [An Equilibrating Parton Shower for Jet Quenching and Medium Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26143)
*Ismail Soudi,Adam Takacs*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文展示了QCD有效动力学理论如何在高能区退化为标准喷注淬火描述，同时保持热尺度附近的一致性，并从中导出一个统一描述硬部分子能量损失与热平衡的部分子簇射，进而将其推广到非均匀扰动情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将喷注淬火（高能部分子能量损失与角展宽）与介质热化统一到一个基于QCD有效动力学理论（EKT）的框架中，并进一步连接喷注演化与介质的流体动力学响应。

Method: 从QCD有效动力学理论出发，导出完整的线性化玻尔兹曼方程；在高能极限下证明其退化为标准能量损失与角展宽描述；构建一个结合弹性和非弹性碰撞的部分子簇射算法；将EKT推广到非均匀扰动情形以捕捉尾流、马赫锥等效应。

Result: 得到了一个直接源自EKT的部分子簇射，能够同时处理硬部分子能量损失和热平衡；该簇射成功推广到非均匀扰动，可描述喷注与介质流体动力学响应的耦合，包括尾流、马赫锥和多粒子关联。

Conclusion: 基于EKT的部分子簇射为统一描述喷注淬火与介质热化提供了自洽框架，并能够在同一理论基础上连接高能喷注物理与介质的集体流体动力学现象。

Abstract: In the weak-coupling approach, thermalization of the quark-gluon plasma and the evolution of jet-like perturbations in the medium can be described by the same underlying QCD effective kinetic theory (EKT). In this work, we show how the EKT reduces to the standard energy-loss and angular-broadening evolution of jet-quenching in the high-energy regime, while providing a consistent description near the thermal scales. Building on this, we derive a parton shower directly from EKT that reproduces the full linearized Boltzmann equation, combining elastic and inelastic collisions to capture both hard-parton energy loss and thermal equilibration on an equal footing. This shower lets us extend EKT to inhomogeneous perturbations, connecting jet evolution to the medium's hydrodynamic response, including wakes, Mach cones, and multi-particle correlations.

</details>


### [123] [Particle Production via Rippled Bubble Walls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26569)
*Ryusuke Jinno,Shota Nakagawa,Yuichiro Nakai,Yaoduo Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 研究了具有内部振荡结构的超相对论厚气泡壁在一阶相变中的非热粒子产生，发现气泡壁的波纹微结构可通过共振动量交换显著增强重粒子的产生率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统气泡膨胀粒子产生模型通常假设光滑的气泡壁轮廓，但实际一阶相变中气泡壁可能具有非平庸的内部结构。本文旨在探讨厚气泡壁内部的波纹（ripples）等空间调制如何影响重粒子的产生机制及其宇宙学意义。

Method: 在气泡膨胀粒子产生框架下，作者将气泡壁建模为具有多个波纹的振荡型厚壁轮廓，推导了壁背景中粒子分裂过程的跃迁概率，并识别出由壁微结构动量传递所贡献的新产生通道。

Result: 除常规产生通道外，发现了一种因波纹共振动量交换而增强的产生模式。当波纹数量足够大时，新贡献可主导产生率，并显著提高远重于相变尺度粒子的丰度。

Conclusion: 气泡壁的内部结构在粒子产生中起重要作用，在评估强一阶相变的宇宙学后果时不可忽略。

Abstract: We investigate non-thermal particle production during first-order phase transitions in the presence of ultra-relativistic thick bubble walls with non-trivial internal structure. Extending the framework of bubble-expansion particle production, we consider bubble walls containing multiple ripples and study how such spatial modulations affect the production of heavy particles coupled to the order parameter field. By modeling an oscillatory thick-wall profile, we derive the transition probability for particle splitting processes in the wall background, and identify a new contribution associated with momentum transfer from the wall microstructure. In addition to the conventional channel, we find an enhanced production mode arising from resonant momentum exchange with the ripples. For sufficiently large numbers of ripples, the new contribution can dominate the production rate and significantly increase the abundance of particles much heavier than the phase-transition scale. Our results demonstrate that the internal structure of expanding bubble walls can play an important role in particle production and should be taken into account when assessing the cosmological implications of strongly first-order phase transitions.

</details>


### [124] [A Geometric CPVSM Framework: Dynamical Quark Mass Spurt with Explicit CP Violation in the Standard Mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26681)
*Chilong Lin*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 提出一个基于无量纲二维比值向量$(x,y)$和$(x',y')$的CKM矩阵几何框架，通过代数降维将9自由度质量矩阵约化至2个不变坐标，给出CKM矩阵和Jarlskog不变量的解析闭式表达式，揭示对易条件$[\mathbf{M}^2_R, \mathbf{M}^2_I]=0$导致的四重简并和非微扰Wolfenstein尺度混合，并发现临界几何附近的"夸克质量突涌"现象可解释宇宙重子不对称性，同时将框架推广至PMNS轻子混合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为CKM矩阵的味混合和CP破坏提供一个直观的几何理解，显式分离上型和下型夸克扇区对电弱混合的贡献，并用统一的几何参数化框架同时描述夸克和轻子味结构。

Method: 利用二维比值向量$(x,y)$和$(x',y')$构建笛卡尔式参数化，从复质量平方矩阵$\mathbf{M}^2$的9个实自由度出发，通过代数降维得到5个基线参数，最终约化至酉对角化矩阵$U$中的2个不变比值坐标$(x,y)$；推导CKM矩阵和Jarlskog不变量$J$的解析闭式解，并分析$[\mathbf{M}^2_R, \mathbf{M}^2_I]=0$对易条件导致的四重幅度简并和Wolfenstein尺度混合。

Result: 得到CKM矩阵的精确解析表达式及Jarlskog不变量闭式解，数值收敛至$|J_0| \approx 3.08 \times 10^{-5}$；证明对易条件导致四重简并$|p|=|p'|=|p^*|=|p'^*|$及非微扰Wolfenstein尺度混合；在临界边界几何($xy \to 0$)处发现夸克质量突涌现象，重塑质量层级同时保持非共线CP破坏($xy' - x'y \neq 0$)，可产生足够幅度解释BAU；框架推广至Dirac中微子PMNS矩阵时自然容纳大轻子混合角。

Conclusion: 该自上而下的几何框架实现了CKM矩阵的直观物理映射，经严格代数降维得到解析闭式解；对易条件为零是四重简并和非微扰混合的根源，暗示需要非对易量子修正；夸克质量突涌机制可同时解释质量层级和宇宙重子不对称性，并成功统一推广至轻子扇区。

Abstract: We present a top-down geometric framework within the Standard Model (SM), utilizing dimensionless 2D ratio vectors $(x,y)$ and $(x',y')$ to establish a Cartesian-like parameterization of the Cabibbo-Kobayashi-Maskawa (CKM) matrix. This approach provides an intuitive physical mapping that explicitly disentangles up- and down-type quark sector contributions to electroweak mixing and CP violation. Crucially, we prove an exact algebraic dimensional reduction from the 9 real degrees of freedom of the complex mass-squared matrix $\mathbf{M}^2$ down to 5 baseline parameters, and ultimately to 2 invariant ratio coordinates ($x,y$) in the unitary diagonalization matrix $U$. This baseline yields exact analytical expressions for the CKM matrix and a closed-form Jarlskog invariant $J$, achieving robust numerical convergence to $|J_0| \approx 3.08 \times 10^{-5}$. We demonstrate that the exact commutativity condition $[\mathbf{M}^2_R, \mathbf{M}^2_I] = 0$ is the fundamental origin of an intrinsic fourfold magnitude degeneracy governed by $|p| = |p'| = |p^*| = |p'^*|$, inducing non-perturbative Wolfenstein scale mixing that signals the necessity of non-commuting quantum corrections ($[\mathbf{M}^2_R, \mathbf{M}^2_I] \neq 0$). Remarkably, near critical boundary geometries ($xy \to 0$), the system exhibits a \textit{quark mass spurt} that reshapes the mass hierarchy while geometrically preserving non-collinear CP violation ($xy' - x'y \neq 0$), yielding magnitudes sufficient to account for the cosmological Baryon Asymmetry of the Universe (BAU). Extending this vector mismatch paradigm to the PMNS lepton matrix with Dirac neutrinos ($ν_R$), we naturally accommodate large leptonic mixing angles through angular vector offsets, establishing a unified, rephasing-invariant geometric framework for flavor origins across both quark and lepton sectors.

</details>


### [125] [Constraining $α$-cluster compactness in $^{16}\rm O$ and $^{20}\rm Ne$ at TeV energies using azimuthal anisotropy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26758)
*Aswathy Menon Kavumpadikkal Radhakrishnan,Suraj Prasad,Neelkamal Mallick,Raghunath Sahoo,Gergely Gábor Barnaföldi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究了在LHC能级下，椭圆流（$v_2$）测量能否约束$^{16}$O和$^{20}$Ne原子核的α团簇结构参数，发现末态椭圆流对核几何结构高度敏感，尤其OO碰撞中可区分不同组态，表明TeV能级轻离子碰撞的流观测量可用于优化轻核结构参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超相对论轻离子碰撞中的各向异性流对碰撞核的初始几何结构敏感。近期提出的$^{16}$O和$^{20}$Ne的α团簇核密度分布假说亟需实验检验，而LHC Run 3的OO和Ne–Ne碰撞数据为此提供了契机。作者旨在探究椭圆流观测量能否有效约束这些轻核的团簇结构参数。

Method: 采用IP-Glasma+MUSIC+iSS+UrQMD混合框架，在$\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=5.36$ TeV下模拟OO和Ne–Ne碰撞。分别使用Woods–Saxon分布和不同团簇紧致度的α团簇组态作为初态核密度输入。在ALICE、CMS、ATLAS探测器运动学接受度内计算椭圆流系数$v_2\{2,|Δη|>1\}$，并与Run 3实验数据对比。系统性地改变$^{16}$O和$^{20}$Ne中的团簇尺寸和团簇间距，以确定最佳参数范围。

Result: 末态椭圆流对核几何结构表现出显著敏感性，尤其在OO碰撞中，不同组态导致$v_2$的中心度依赖关系和峰值位置出现明显差异。通过系统扫描团簇参数范围，找到了与实验数据最佳吻合的参数区间，证实了流观测量可用于鉴别轻核的团簇结构。

Conclusion: TeV能级轻离子碰撞中的流观测量是优化轻核结构参数的有效工具。椭圆流$v_2$对α团簇几何高度敏感，尤其是OO碰撞体系，可为核结构研究提供独立于传统低能实验的新途径。

Abstract: Anisotropic flow in ultra-relativistic light-ion collisions is sensitive to the initial geometry of the colliding nuclei. We investigate whether elliptic flow measurements can constrain the parameters of the proposed $α$-clustered nuclear density distributions of $^{16}$O and $^{20}$Ne at LHC energies. Using the hybrid framework IP-Glasma+MUSIC+iSS+UrQMD, we simulate OO and Ne--Ne collisions at $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=5.36$ TeV for the Woods--Saxon and $α$-clustered configurations with varying cluster compactness. The elliptic flow coefficient $v_2\{2,|Δη|>1\}$ is calculated in the kinematic acceptances of ALICE, CMS, and ATLAS detectors and is compared with the Run~3 OO and Ne--Ne experimental measurements. It is observed that the final-state elliptic flow is significantly sensitive to the nuclear geometry, especially in OO collisions, where different configurations lead to distinct centrality dependencies and peak positions of $v_{2}$. By performing a systematic variation of the cluster size and inter-cluster separation in $^{16}$O and $^{20}$Ne nuclei, this work attempts to identify the cluster parameter range that provides the best agreement with the experimental data. These results show that the flow observables in TeV-energy light-ion collisions can be used to optimize the nuclear structure parameters of light nuclei.

</details>


### [126] [Outcomes of Grand Unified Symmetry Breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27158)
*Harish Hemming,Tanmay Vachaspati,Anja Wachowitz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 通过数值模拟研究大统一模型中对称性破缺的结果，发现偏置畴壁与磁单极子的共生物理现象，包括畴壁吸收单极子、畴壁湮灭产生单极子、磁荷壁塌缩形成磁黑洞及偏置畴壁产生随机引力波背景，为大统一时代的宇宙学探测开辟新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大统一模型预言的对称性破缺可能同时产生磁单极子和畴壁等拓扑缺陷，但两者在宇宙学演化中的相互作用及其可观测效应尚缺乏系统的数值研究。作者旨在填补这一空白，探索通过宇宙学观测来探测大统一时代的新途径。

Method: 采用数值模拟方法，研究由大统一模型启发的对称性破缺过程，在参数空间中系统考察偏置畴壁（biased domain walls）与磁单极子的形成及相互作用动力学。

Result: 数值模拟揭示了若干新奇的物理过程：(1) 畴壁对磁单极子的吸收；(2) 畴壁湮灭时磁单极子的产生；(3) 带磁荷的畴壁在引力作用下塌缩可形成磁荷黑洞；(4) 偏置畴壁产生随机引力波背景信号。

Conclusion: 畴壁与磁单极子的相互作用产生了一系列丰富的、可通过宇宙学观测检验的现象，为探测大统一时代提供了全新的观测窗口，尤其随机引力波背景和磁荷黑洞是极具潜力的探针。

Abstract: We numerically study the outcome of symmetry breaking motivated by Grand Unified models. Our results show the formation of biased domain walls for a range of parameters together with magnetic monopoles. The interactions of walls and monopoles leads to novel processes such as the absorption of monopoles on walls, production of monopoles when domain walls annihilate, collapse of walls with magnetic charge that can, with gravity taken into account, produce magnetically charged black holes, and production of a stochastic gravitational wave background from biased domain walls. Our findings open new avenues for probing the grand unification epoch by cosmological observables.

</details>
