### [127] [The Confounder Trap: Treatment-Encoding Representations in Causal Inference with Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26309)
*Marie Neubrander,Graham Tierney,Alexander Volfovsky*

Main category: stat.ME

TL;DR: 从观测文本中估计因果效应时，文本中同时包含处理变量和混杂调整所需的属性，导致传统表示学习方法可能将处理信息编码进调整表示中，形成"混杂陷阱"。本文提出基于掩码的调整表示方法，通过删除或替换处理定义词元来消除表示中的处理信号，从而保持重叠性、稳定效应估计并减少偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从文本估计因果效应的方法通常从全文中学习表示以捕捉潜在混杂因素。但当处理状态本身由文本中的词语编码时（如通过词典定义的处理），这些表示会直接编码处理信息，导致"混杂陷阱"：更丰富的表示使得处理组和控制组文档可分离，即使底层因果问题满足重叠假设，也会引发重叠违反。

Method: 作者研究通过词典或其他处理定义词汇信息编码的隐式文本处理变量，提出基于掩码的调整表示方法：在学习表示之前移除处理相关的词汇信号。具体包括两种掩码策略：(1) 删除掩码（deletion masking），针对词袋模型/主题模型，理论上证明其可保持重叠性；(2) 替换掩码（replacement masking），作为大语言模型的自然扩展，隐藏处理定义词元同时保留词序和上下文信息。

Result: 通过模拟实验，掩码方法在多个维度上表现优越：改善了重叠诊断指标、稳定了处理效应估计、并相较于从未掩码文本学习的调整方法显著降低了估计偏差。

Conclusion: 在文本因果推断中，当处理变量由文本内部的词汇信号定义时，必须警惕"混杂陷阱"。通过在表示学习前对处理定义词元进行掩码，可以有效避免表示引发的重叠失败，从而获得更可靠的因果效应估计。

Abstract: Estimating causal effects of linguistic properties from observational text is difficult because the same document can contain both the treatment of interest and the non-treatment textual attributes needed for adjustment. Existing approaches often learn representations from the full text to capture latent confounding, but when treatment status is itself encoded by words in the text, these representations can directly encode treatment. This creates a confounder trap: richer representations can make treated and control documents separable, inducing overlap violations even when the underlying causal problem satisfies overlap. We study latent text treatments that are encoded through lexicons or other treatment-defining lexical information, and propose masking-based adjustment representations that remove this lexical treatment signal before representation learning. We formalize representation-induced overlap failure, prove that deletion masking preserves overlap for bag-of-words/topic-model representations, and characterize replacement masking as a natural relaxation for large language models that hides treatment-defining tokens while preserving word order and context. Across simulations, masking improves overlap diagnostics, stabilizes treatment effect estimates, and reduces bias relative to adjustment methods that learn from the unmasked text.

</details>


<div id='hep-lat'></div>

# hep-lat [[Back]](#toc)

### [128] [Neural quantum states for non-Abelian lattice gauge theories with dynamical fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26131)
*Gabriel Rouxinol,Julian Bender,Michele Grossi,Patrick Emonts,Jad C. Halimeh*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 提出了一种无符号问题的变分蒙特卡罗框架，用于求解连续SU(2)格点规范理论与动力学交错费米子耦合的基态。该方法在磁基中采用神经网络表示规范波函数，并用规范协变的高斯费米子修正描述费米子，成功绘制了相图并验证了强耦合微扰理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定非阿贝尔格点规范理论与动力学费米子耦合的基态，是理解禁闭和规范-物质系统相结构的关键问题。现有方法面临符号问题或截断近似等困难，需要一种无符号问题且适用于连续规范群的变分方法。

Method: 在磁基中工作，用神经网络表示规范波函数；费米子部分采用基于固定Néel参考态的规范协变高斯费米子修正，该修正由短Wilson线和质量-跃迁哈密顿量的本征矢构造的厄米算符生成，变分参数数目为系统尺寸的多项式量级。高斯结构允许通过费米子占据矩阵解析表达所有费米子对能量和观测量的贡献。

Result: 结果与强耦合微扰理论一致，恢复了预期的有效反铁磁自旋哈密顿量；绘制了独立电耦合和磁耦合(g², λ)平面上的粗粒度基态相图，并证明滞后分析可识别相变的存在；恢复物理关系λ=4/g²后，刻画了L=4,6,8格点尺寸下偏离Néel参考态的行为。

Conclusion: 该方法为连续非阿贝尔规范群与动力学物质提供了一个无符号问题的变分框架，可推广到其他物质内容和更高维格点。

Abstract: Determining the ground state of non-Abelian lattice gauge theories coupled to dynamical fermions is key to understanding confinement and the phase structure of gauge--matter systems. We present a variational Monte Carlo framework for the ground state of the untruncated fully-continuous SU$(2)$ lattice gauge theory coupled to dynamical staggered fermions on an $L\times L$ square lattice. We work in the magnetic basis with a neural-network representation of the gauge wavefunction. The fermions are described by a gauge-covariant Gaussian fermionic correction built on a fixed Néel reference state where, for each sampled gauge configuration $\mathbf{U}$, the correction is generated by a Hermitian operator. This operator is constructed from short Wilson lines and the eigenvectors of the mass--hopping Hamiltonian, with number of variational parameters polynomial in the system size. This Gaussian structure also gives analytical expressions for all fermionic contributions to the energy and related observables in terms of the fermion occupation matrix. The results are validated against strong-coupling perturbation theory, where they recover the expected effective antiferromagnetic spin Hamiltonian. Using this framework, we map a coarse ground state phase diagram in the plane of independent electric and magnetic couplings $(g^2, λ)$ and show that a hysteresis analysis can identify the existence of phase transitions. Restoring the physical relation $λ=4/g^2$, we characterize how increasing the system size and changing the electric coupling $g^2$ move the state away from the reference Néel state, for lattice sizes $L=4,6,8$. More broadly, the method offers a sign-problem-free variational framework for continuous non-Abelian gauge groups with dynamical matter that should extend to other matter content and higher-dimensional lattices.

</details>


### [129] [Quantum Phase Diagram of the $2+1$D Untruncated SU$(2)$ Lattice Gauge Theory with Dynamical Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26132)
*Gabriel Rouxinol,Julian Bender,Patrick Emonts,Michele Grossi,Jad C. Halimeh*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文采用连续群变分蒙特卡洛方法，在未截断的SU(2)格点规范理论中，研究了交错费米子的基态行为，发现了磁通相变和规范-物质离域渡越，揭示了磁通序与费米子相干性如何协同发展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非阿贝尔规范理论（如SU(2)）与动力学物质的基态性质难以从第一性原理获得，而现有方法多涉及对规范场的截断近似。本文旨在不对SU(2)规范场做任何截断的前提下，从第一性原理出发，系统揭示该理论的基态行为。

Method: 采用连续群变分蒙特卡洛（Variational Monte Carlo）方法，完整保留SU(2)规范场而不做截断。在L×L正方格点上研究带交错费米子的SU(2)格点规范理论，分别独立调节磁耦合λ和电耦合g²，并沿物理耦合线λ=4/g²进行有限尺寸（L=4,6,8）计算。

Result: （1）发现λ*=−0.040±0.005处存在磁通相变，且相变点不随电耦合g²变化而发生可分辨的漂移；（2）沿物理耦合线，观测到规范-物质离域渡越：从强电耦合下的磁通无序区过渡到弱耦合下的有序单位磁通区；（3）手征凝聚、规范不变的Wilson线介子关联函数以及局域色密度三者一致地揭示了相干规范辅助物质动力学的涌现。

Conclusion: 该工作在未截断的非阿贝尔格点规范理论中建立了一个统一的物理图像：磁通序与费米子相干性协同发展。连续群变分蒙特卡洛方法被证明是研究此类强关联体系的有力工具。

Abstract: Non-Abelian gauge theories with dynamical matter govern the strong interaction and a broad class of strongly correlated quantum systems, yet their ground-state properties remain difficult to obtain from first principles. Using a continuous-group variational Monte Carlo approach that retains the full SU$(2)$ gauge field without truncation, we determine the ground-state behavior of the SU$(2)$ lattice gauge theory with staggered fermions on an $L\times L$ square lattice. Treating the magnetic and electric couplings $λ$ and $g^2$ independently, we find a magnetic-flux transition at $λ^\ast=-0.040\pm 0.005$, with no resolvable drift of the transition point as the electric coupling is varied. Along the physical coupling line $λ=4/g^2$, for $L=4,6,8$, we uncover a gauge-matter delocalization crossover from a flux-disordered regime at strong electric coupling to an ordered unity-flux regime at weak coupling. The chiral condensate, a gauge-invariant Wilson-line meson correlator, and the local color density consistently reveal the emergence of coherent gauge-assisted matter dynamics. Together, these results provide a unified physical picture of how magnetic-flux ordering and fermionic coherence develop in an untruncated non-Abelian lattice gauge theory.

</details>


### [130] [Static Quark-Antiquark Interactions Under Rotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26939)
*Heng-Tong Ding,Olaf Kaczmarek,Ran Luo,Hai-Tao Shu*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用虚角速度下的淬火格点模拟，研究旋转SU(3)胶子动力学中静态夸克-反夸克相互作用。零温下从Wilson圈提取的静态势未显示显著旋转依赖性；有限温度下，虚旋转对Polyakov圈关联子的色平均自由能产生与几何构型相关的位置依赖性抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转量子场论中存在非平凡效应（如手征涡旋效应、旋转抑制禁闭等），但旋转对静态夸克-反夸克相互作用的影响尚未从第一性原理得到充分研究。本文旨在通过格点QCD提供旋转胶子介质中静态源自由能的定量结果。

Method: 采用虚角速度下的淬灭SU(3)格点模拟。零温时从Wilson圈提取三种几何构型（轴向对齐、单源在转轴上的横向、对称横向）的静态势；有限温度时通过Polyakov圈关联子计算纵向和横向的色平均自由能，并利用轴向-对角比较识别边界效应较小的体区域。

Result: 零温静态势在当前精度内未观察到显著旋转依赖或各向异性。有限温度下，虚旋转抑制纵向和横向自由能；体区域内纵向长程自由能移动满足ΔF_z(R_xy)=A R_xy²+B；横向通道显示相同定性抑制，距离依赖性反映径向源排列并与径向单源自由能移动兼容。在T_c以上，响应随温度升高而减弱。

Conclusion: 首次提供格点证据表明，胶子介质中裸静态源自由能对虚旋转的响应具有位置和几何构型依赖性。这为进一步研究旋转QCD介质中重夸克相互作用奠定了基础。

Abstract: We study static quark--antiquark interactions in rotating SU(3) gluodynamics using quenched lattice simulations at imaginary angular velocity. At zero temperature, we extract the static potential from Wilson loops for quark--antiquark pairs aligned with the rotation axis, for transverse pairs with one source on the rotation axis, and for symmetric transverse pairs across the rotation axis. Within the present accuracy, no significant rotation dependence or anisotropy is observed in the zero-temperature potential. At finite temperature, imaginary rotation suppresses the color-averaged free energies obtained from Polyakov-loop correlators in both longitudinal and transverse geometries. Axial-diagonal comparisons are used to identify a bulk region where open-boundary artifacts are reduced. In this region, the large-distance longitudinal free-energy shift is well described by $ΔF_z(R_{xy})=A R_{xy}^2+B$. The transverse channels exhibit the same qualitative suppression, while their distance dependence additionally reflects the radial arrangement of the static sources and is compatible with a radial single-source free-energy shift in the bulk region. For the finite-temperature observables studied above $T_c$, the response weakens as the temperature is increased. These results provide lattice evidence for a position- and geometry-dependent response of bare static-source free energies to imaginary rotation in a gluonic medium.

</details>

### [131] [The boundary-driven multispecies harmonic process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26262)
*Francesco Casini,Rouven Frassek,Cristian Giardinà*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文引入多物种谐振过程在一维链上的推广，与边界热库接触。该过程是一个连续时间马尔可夫链，每个格点可容纳无限制数量的带色粒子。对称的体动力学与注入/移除粒子的热库耦合，驱动系统远离平衡。该过程的马尔可夫生成元被识别为高秩开量子有理海森堡自旋链的可积哈密顿量。作者推导了算子形式的R-矩阵和K-矩阵，并按照Sklyanin方法构造了双行转移矩阵。与单物种情形类似，R-矩阵分解为两个R-算子，分别对应左行和右行粒子。进一步定义了三个对偶模型：吸收粒子模型、隐参数模型和热传导模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单物种谐振过程推广到多物种情形，为多组分非平衡系统建立可精确求解的框架，利用量子可积系统的数学工具（R-矩阵、K-矩阵、转移矩阵）对开边界驱动的多物种粒子系统进行严格分析。

Method: 构造多物种谐振过程的连续时间马尔可夫链，其对称体动力学与边界热库耦合；将马尔可夫生成元映射为开量子有理海森堡自旋链（高秩）的可积哈密顿量；推导算子形式的R-矩阵和K-矩阵；按照Sklyanin的代数Bethe ansatz框架构造双行转移矩阵；将R-矩阵分解为对应左右行粒子的两个R-算子；定义三个对偶模型。

Result: 成功构造了多物种谐振过程的马尔可夫生成元，并识别为开边界高秩有理海森堡自旋链的可积哈密顿量；导出了算子形式的R-矩阵和K-矩阵；R-矩阵可分解为分别对应左行和右行粒子的两个R-算子；构造了双行转移矩阵；建立了三个对偶模型（吸收粒子模型、隐参数模型、热传导模型）。

Conclusion: 多物种谐振过程可通过量子可积系统方法严格处理，其代数结构与开边界高秩海森堡自旋链对应，R-矩阵的分解性质揭示了左右行粒子的内在结构，对偶模型进一步拓展了该框架的应用范围。

Abstract: We introduce the multispecies version of the harmonic process on a one-dimensional chain in contact with boundary reservoirs. This process is a continuous-time Markov chain where each site can host an unbounded number of colored particles. The symmetric bulk dynamics is put in contact with reservoirs, which inject and remove particles driving the system out-of-equilibrium. The Markov generator of the process is identified with the integrable Hamiltonian of an open rational Heisenberg spin chain of higher rank. We derive the underlying R- and K-matrices in operator form and construct the double-row transfer matrix following Sklyanin. Similar to the monospecies case the R-matrix factorises into two R-operators, each factor corresponding to left and right moving particles. We further define three dual models: an absorbing particle model, a hidden parameter model and a heat conduction model.

</details>


### [132] [Quantum Turing Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26331)
*Kazuki Ikeda*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Lindblad格点动力学中构造了量子图灵斑图，建立了其非线性序和量子涨落的严格理论。作者证明了一类具有有限程耦合的完全正模型存在超临界不稳定性，其解析的条纹解枝在反射不变的周期胞空间中是局部渐近稳定的。在半经典极限下，投影相干态展现广延布拉格序；微观协方差以 O(N^{-1/2}) 收敛到非自治高斯李雅普诺夫流，并将严格的部分转置不确定性违背传递到有限大 N 系统。一个无量纲比值同时控制图灵稳定性行列式和对数负度，从而将波长选择与量子纠缠直接联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图灵斑图（反应扩散系统中由图灵不稳定性驱动的空间结构）已被广泛研究，但量子图灵斑图的严格理论框架尚属空白。作者旨在填补这一空白，将图灵失稳机制推广到开放量子多体系统（Lindblad动力学）中，并建立从微观量子涨落到宏观空间序的完整数学桥梁，特别是揭示波长选择与量子纠缠之间的内在联系。

Method: 作者采用Lindblad主方程框架描述开放量子格点系统的耗散动力学。具体技术路线包括：(1) 构造具有有限程耦合的完全正（CP）模型，使一阶矩方程在非零波数处发生超临界失稳；(2) 解析推导位点中心和键中心的两类公度条纹枝解，并在反射固定的周期胞空间中证明其局部渐近稳定性；(3) 利用投影相干态证明半经典极限下的广延布拉格序；(4) 证明微观协方差以 O(N^{-1/2}) 速率收敛到非自治高斯李雅普诺夫流；(5) 在均匀高斯扇区引入一个无量纲比值，统一刻画图灵稳定性行列式与相反动量对数负度；(6) 通过数值延拓和二维模拟验证条纹、斑点、迷宫等多种斑图形态。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 所构造的CP模型在非零波数处出现超临界图灵失稳，解析条纹枝解在周期胞空间内局部渐近稳定；(2) 半经典极限下投影相干态具有广延布拉格序，在任意有界时间区间上成立；(3) 微观协方差以 O(N^{-1/2}) 收敛到非自治高斯李雅普诺夫流，并将严格的部分转置不确定性违背传递到足够大的有限 N 系统；(4) 在均匀高斯扇区中，单个无量纲比值同时决定图灵稳定性行列式和对数负度，从而直接关联波长选择与量子纠缠；(5) 微分输运将最强相反动量关联从红外区移至所选图灵尺度；(6) 数值模拟显示条纹、斑点、迷宫等形态，其傅里叶模和涨落谱均集中在同一选定波数。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了量子图灵斑图的第一个严格理论框架，将经典斑图形成的图灵失稳机制成功推广到开放量子多体系统中。核心发现是波长选择与量子纠缠通过一个无量纲比值被统一描述，这揭示了量子涨落和空间序之间的深层联系。该理论为理解非平衡量子系统中的自发对称破缺、空间结构形成以及量子-经典对应提供了坚实基础，并可能对量子模拟、量子光学和凝聚态物理中的耗散结构研究产生重要影响。

Abstract: We construct quantum Turing patterns in Lindblad lattice dynamics and establish a rigorous theory of their nonlinear order and quantum fluctuations. For an explicit completely positive family with finite-range couplings, the first-moment equations undergo a supercritical instability at a nonzero wave number and admit analytic site- and bond-centered commensurate stripe branches. These branches are locally asymptotically stable in their reflection-fixed period-cell spaces, and projected coherent states exhibit extensive Bragg order on every bounded time interval in the semiclassical limit. We prove $O(N^{-1/2})$ convergence of microscopic covariances to a nonautonomous Gaussian Lyapunov flow, transferring strict partial-transpose uncertainty violations to sufficiently large $N$. In the homogeneous Gaussian sector, a single dimensionless ratio controls both the Turing stability determinant and the logarithmic negativity of opposite momenta, relating wavelength selection directly to quantum entanglement. Differential transport shifts the strongest opposite-momentum correlations from the infrared to the selected Turing scale. Numerical continuation and two-dimensional simulations display stripe, spot, and labyrinth morphologies whose Fourier modes and fluctuation spectra concentrate at the same selected wave numbers.

</details>


### [133] [Lee-Yang Zeros And Particle Fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26975)
*Mohamed El Hedi Bahri,Ian Jauslin,Joel L. Lebowitz*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文证明了在Lee-Yang零点在实轴某点附近有间隙的条件下，大体积极限与化学势求导可交换，即有限体积压强的所有化学势导数一致收敛到极限压强的对应导数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典粒子系统的热力学极限下，有限体积压强对化学势的导数（如密度、粒子数涨落）是否收敛到极限压强的对应导数是一个基本问题。作者希望建立Lee-Yang零点分布与热力学函数解析性之间的严格联系。

Method: 在巨正则系综框架下，假设粒子对势满足稳定性、缓增性和低正则性条件，考虑一致有界密度的边界条件。核心假设是：对所有充分大体积，巨配分函数在复逸度平面上的Lee-Yang零点始终远离正实轴上某点z₀。在此条件下，利用复分析方法和解析函数列的性质，证明导数的逐点收敛可提升为紧集上的一致收敛。

Result: 证明了若Lee-Yang零点条件成立，则沿立方体取热力学极限时：有限体积压强对化学势的各阶导数在z₀的一个邻域内一致收敛到极限压强的对应导数；密度的极限为β⁻¹∂_μ p，粒子数方差每单位体积的极限为β⁻²∂²_μ p；所有导数的极限值不依赖于边界条件的选择。结果还推广到超稳定势的Procacci-Yuhjtman无界边界条件。

Conclusion: Lee-Yang零点在实轴上的间隙等价于压强函数在该点的解析性，这为通过零点分布判定相变提供了严格的数学基础，并将Ruelle的缓增边界条件结果拓展到更广的边界条件类。

Abstract: We consider classical particles in the continuum in the grand canonical ensemble, with a stable, tempered and lower-regular pair potential and boundary conditions of uniformly bounded density. We prove that if the Lee--Yang zeros of the grand canonical partition function in the complex fugacity plane $z = e^{βμ}$ remain bounded away from a real point $z_0 > 0$ for all sufficiently large volumes, then along cubes the thermodynamic limit and differentiation commute at $z_0$: every derivative of the finite-volume pressure in the chemical potential converges, uniformly in a neighborhood of $z_0$, to the corresponding derivative of the limiting pressure. The limiting values of all derivatives are independent of the boundary condition; in particular, the density and the particle-number variance per unit volume converge to $β^{-1}\partial_μp$ and $β^{-2}\partial^{2}_μ p$, respectively. The result extends to the unbounded boundary conditions of Procacci and Yuhjtman for super-stable potentials in addition to Ruelle's tempered boundary conditions.

</details>


### [134] [Towards First Quantisation Formalism for AKSZ Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26394)
*Leon Menger,Pavel Mnev*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为一类AKSZ理论构建了一个一维的"第一量子化"对应理论，该理论定义在图（graphs）上，其配分函数可重现原理论的费曼图。该一维理论本身也是AKSZ型理论，耦合了一维超引力。在BV-BFV形式下，顶点处的拉格朗日子流形定义了一个Weinstein辛范畴中的循环L∞代数，并决定了路径积分中边上的场的缝合条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将d维AKSZ型拓扑场论（如Chern-Simons理论）的费曼图展开，重新解释为一个定义在抽象图上的1维场论的配分函数，从而实现"第一量子化"图景，并利用BV-BFV形式从经典拉格朗日子流形出发组织量子理论的结构。

Method: 作者构造了一个1维AKSZ理论t，其靶空间由原理论T的底流形M构建，并耦合1维超引力（边的长度T及其微分dT分别解释为引力子和引力微子的零模）。在BV-BFV形式下分析理论t：经典层面上，每个k价顶点对应相空间Φ的k次笛卡尔积中的一个拉格朗日子流形Lk，这些子流形构成Weinstein辛范畴中的循环L∞代数（"去量子化"原理论靶空间的上同调向量场）。路径积分中，Lk决定入射边上场在顶点处的缝合条件。通过选取规范固定，将理论t的传播子联系到具体的量子力学系统（如Witten-Morse超对称量子力学）。

Result: 理论t在度量图空间上产生一个形式（边长度T及其de Rham微分dT），其积分给出原理论T的费曼图之和。当T为非阿贝尔Chern-Simons理论（结构李代数为su(2)）时，明确描述了顶点拉格朗日子流形LW（"Wigner Lagrangian"）。规范固定将一般的T和t与具体的超对称量子力学模型联系起来。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了AKSZ理论与图上的1维理论之间的"第一量子化"对应：原理论的费曼图展开等价于图上天底空间的1维AKSZ理论的配分函数。BV-BFV形式为这一对应提供了自然的数学框架，顶点的拉格朗日子流形编码了相互作用顶点，并通过循环L∞代数结构联系到Weinstein的辛范畴。这一范式在Chern-Simons理论等例子上得到了具体实现。

Abstract: \noindent Given an AKSZ theory $\mathbb{T}$ on a manifold $M$, with target a graded vector space $Y$, we formulate a 1-dimensional theory $\mathbb{t}$ on graphs (the ``first quantisation picture for $\mathbb{T}$''), whose partition functions reproduce the Feynman graphs of $\mathbb{T}$. More precisely, the theory $\mathbb{t}$ is itself a 1d AKSZ theory with the target built out of $M$, and involving a coupling to 1d supergravity. It yields a form on the space of metric graphs (with length $T$ of an edge and its de Rham differential $\mathrm{d} T$ interpreted as the zero-modes of the graviton and gravitino, respectively); its integral yields the sum of Feynman graphs of $\mathbb{T}$. We study the theory $\mathbb{t}$ in the BV-BFV formalism; a gauge-fixing of $\mathbb{T}$ corresponds to a gauge-fixing of $\mathbb{t}$. At the classical level, $\mathbb{t}$ assigns to vertices certain Lagrangian submanifolds $L_k$ in Cartesian powers $Φ^{\times k}$ of the phase space $Φ$ of $\mathbb{t}$. These submanifolds can be thought of as defining a cyclic $\mathrm{L}_\infty$-algebra in Weinstein's symplectic category (``dequantising'' the cohomological vector field on the target %target AKSZ dg structure of $\mathbb{T}$). In the path integral construction of $\mathbb{t}$, Lagrangians $L_k$ determine sewing conditions for fields on the incident edges at a $k$-valent vertex. We give examples of this paradigm, such as when $\mathbb{t}$ on edges is the Witten-Morse supersymmetric quantum mechanics (which corresponds to a particular type of gauge-fixing for $\mathbb{T}$ and $\mathbb{t}$). In the example where $\mathbb{T}$ is the non-abelian Chern--Simons theory with structure Lie algebra $\mathfrak{su}(2)$, we describe the vertex Lagrangian $L_{\mathrm{W}}$ (the ``Wigner Lagrangian'' ).

</details>

### [135] [Critical non-thermal fixed point and the dynamical condensation phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26620)
*Anne-Solène Bornens,Elisabeth Gliott,Nicolas Cherroret*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文利用非微扰量子动力学框架，统一描述了三维玻色气体在BEC相变附近冷却淬火后的远离平衡动力学，揭示了平衡凝聚阈值同时充当动力学临界点，划分出三类不同的非平衡普适类，分别由热固定点、涡旋线扩散重组的粗化固定点以及临界固定点所支配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究尚未建立玻色气体在BEC相变附近远离平衡动力学的统一图景，尤其是平衡相变临界点如何影响非平衡动力学普适类的问题亟待澄清。

Method: 采用非微扰量子动力学框架，追踪动量分布的时空演化，分析不同淬火条件下系统趋向非平衡吸引子的标度行为。

Result: 发现三个动力学区域：(1) 相变以上淬火呈现单时间尺度弛豫至热固定点；(2) 相变以下淬火经历弱湍流到涡旋线扩散重组粗化固定点的渡越；(3) 直接淬火至凝聚阈值受临界固定点支配，表现出临界涨落的超扩散传播和独特的动力学标度指数。

Conclusion: 这些动力学标度律构成了BEC相变的远离平衡对应物，平衡临界点同时组织了系统的长时非平衡动力学。

Abstract: Using a non-perturbative quantum kinetic framework, we develop a unified description of the far-from-equilibrium dynamics of three-dimensional Bose gases following cooling quenches across the Bose-Einstein condensation transition. By tracking the spatio-temporal evolution of the momentum distribution, we show that the equilibrium condensation threshold simultaneously acts as a dynamical critical point, separating distinct far-from-equilibrium universality classes governed by different non-equilibrium attractors. While quenches above the transition exhibit a single-timescale relaxation toward a thermal fixed point, quenches below the transition display a crossover from a transient weak-turbulence regime to a coarsening fixed point governed by the diffusive recombination of vortex lines. Quenches directly to the condensation threshold, finally, are controlled by a previously unidentified critical fixed point characterized by the superdiffusive spreading of critical fluctuations and a distinct set of dynamical exponents. Together, these dynamical scaling laws establish a far-from-equilibrium counterpart of the condensation phase transition, in which the equilibrium critical point also organizes the long-time non-equilibrium dynamics.

</details>

### [136] [Anisotropic Spin Polarization and magnetic spin hall effect in Ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27016)
*Jiabin Wang,Zhenhua Zhang,Wancheng Zhang,Jianxiong Zhao,Yong Liu,Rui Xiong,Zhihong Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文揭示了强自旋轨道耦合铁磁体中自旋相关输运的显著各向异性，其自旋极化和磁自旋霍尔电导率均随磁化方向或电场方向改变而剧烈变化，并展示了通过应变调控该各向异性的可能性，为无外场自旋电子学器件提供了新思路。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统铁磁体中的自旋相关输运研究主要关注磁化方向的影响，但对强自旋轨道耦合铁磁体中输运各向异性的完整图像——尤其是磁化方向与电场方向双重各向异性及其物理起源——仍缺乏系统理解，同时探索应变作为调控手段的潜力。

Method: 以强自旋轨道耦合铁磁体为研究对象，系统计算了当磁化方向偏离晶体易轴、以及电场方向相对晶轴旋转两种情形下的整体自旋极化率和磁自旋霍尔电导率，定量分析自旋轨道耦合对各向异性的贡献，并引入拉伸应变考察其对磁自旋霍尔电导率振荡幅度的调控效应。

Result: 1. 磁化偏离易轴时，整体自旋极化率和磁自旋霍尔电导率均表现出显著各向异性；2. 电场方向旋转同样引发明显的各向异性响应；3. 自旋轨道耦合被确认为上述大各向异性的主要驱动力；4. 拉伸应变可逐步增强磁自旋霍尔电导率的振荡幅度，实现应变的可调性。

Conclusion: 强自旋轨道耦合铁磁体可作为各向异性自旋流产生的理想平台，利用其本征材料各向异性可设计无需外磁场的自旋电子学器件，在提升性能与能效方面具有重要潜力。

Abstract: Spin-dependent transport in ferromagnets underpins the development of high-density spintronic memories. Spin-dependent transport in strong spin-orbit-coupled ferromagnets exhibits a significant anisotropy. Both the overall spin polarization during charge transport and the magnetic spin Hall conductivity are found to exhibit pronounced anisotropy when the magnetization is tilted away from the crystallographic easy axis or when the electric field is rotated relative to the crystal axes. These anisotropic responses originate primarily from spin-orbit coupling, which is identified as the key driver of the large anisotropy observed in ferromagnet. Furthermore, strain tunability of the magnetic spin Hall anisotropy is demonstrated, with tensile strain progressively enhancing the oscillatory amplitude of the spin Hall conductivity. These findings establish strong spin-orbit-coupled ferromagnets as a platform for anisotropic spin-current generation and field-free spintronic devices that exploit intrinsic material anisotropy for improved performance and energy efficiency.

</details>


### [137] [Majorana current induced by charge current in Kitaev magnet/graphene bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26943)
*Takehito Yokoyama*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究了在Kitaev磁体与石墨烯耦合体系中，通过电荷流诱导马约拉纳流（Majorana current）的机制。作者基于Kondo型马约拉纳-电子耦合的微扰理论，推导了电荷流诱导的马约拉纳流密度的解析表达式，并进一步讨论了电荷流诱导的马约拉纳热流及其量级估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马约拉纳费米子作为电中性的准粒子，其操控手段有限。本文旨在探索通过电荷流以"拖拽"方式间接控制Kitaev磁体中马约拉纳费米子的可能性，即利用石墨烯中的电子流经Kondo耦合拖动Kitaev磁体中的马约拉纳费米子，实现电子学手段对电中性马约拉纳费米子的调控。

Method: 采用Kondo型马约拉纳-电子耦合模型描述石墨烯电子与Kitaev磁体中马约拉纳费米子的相互作用，基于微扰理论计算马约拉纳流密度，推导出电荷流诱导的马约拉纳流的解析表达式，并将其推广至马约拉纳热流的计算。

Result: 获得了电荷流诱导的马约拉纳流密度的解析表达式，验证了通过电子电荷流拖拽电中性马约拉纳费米子的可行性；同时给出了马约拉纳热流的表达式及其量级估计。

Conclusion: 通过石墨烯中的电荷流，可借助Kondo型耦合以拖拽机制诱导Kitaev磁体中的马约拉纳流和马约拉纳热流，这为实现电子学手段控制电中性马约拉纳费米子提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: We investigate Majorana current induced by charge current in Kitaev magnets coupled with graphene through the mechanism by which Majorana fermions in the Kitaev magnet are dragged by electrons in graphene. We calculate Majorana current density based on the perturbation with respect to a Kondo type Majorana-electron coupling and obtain an analytical expression of the Majorana current induced by charge current, indicative of electronic control of charge-neutral Majorana fermions. We also discuss Majorana heat current induced by charge current and give estimations of their magnitudes.

</details>


### [138] [Magnetic Breakdown and Anomalous Quantum Oscillation in Rhombohedral Tetralayer Graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27207)
*Jing-Yu Zhao,Yang-Zhi Chou,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 研究了菱形四层石墨烯中范霍夫奇点附近的磁击穿现象，发现反常的Shubnikov-de Haas量子振荡，表现为朗道扇形图中的环状结构和高频峰，这些特征可归因于三个相邻费米口袋间的磁击穿重构，并为手性超导态的实验探测提供了判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验在电子掺杂的菱形四层石墨烯中发现了手性超导，但与此相关的范霍夫奇点附近的费米面几何及其在强磁场下的量子振荡行为尚不清楚，需要建立磁击穿诱导的反常量子振荡作为探测VHS相关费米面结构的通用手段。

Method: 采用无相互作用能带结构结合Kubo公式，计算了菱形四层石墨烯在磁场下的Shubnikov-de Haas振荡，分析了朗道扇形图、频谱中的高频峰，并考察温度和弱无序对振荡的影响；进一步将分析拓展到半经典图像失效的强VHS区域。

Result: 发现了朗道扇形图中的环状结构以及频谱中的反常高频峰，这些特征源于VHS分隔的三个邻近费米口袋之间的磁击穿重构；反常特征在强VHS区域仍定性保持；温度和弱无序依赖关系也得到阐明。

Conclusion: 磁击穿诱导的反常量子振荡为VHS相关的费米面几何提供了通用的、具有特征性的实验探针，并可解释近期菱形四层石墨烯中的量子振荡实验观测。

Abstract: We investigate magnetic breakdown near Van Hove singularities (VHSs) in the electron-doped rhombohedral tetralayer graphene, where chiral superconductivity has recently been reported. Using the noninteracting band structure and Kubo formula, we identify anomalous Shubnikov-de Haas effects: Ring-like structures in the Landau fan and anomalous high-frequency peaks in the frequency spectra. These anomalous quantum oscillations can be understood by the reconstruction from magnetic breakdown among three nearby Fermi pockets separated by VHSs. Remarkably, these qualitative anomalous features persist into a stronger-VHS regime, where the semiclassical picture breaks down, and chiral superconductivity emerges. The temperature and (weak) disorder dependence of the oscillations are also investigated. Our results establish that the magnetic-breakdown-induced anomalous quantum oscillation provides a general distinctive probe for the underlying Fermi-surface geometry associated with VHSs and may explain the recent quantum oscillation experiment in rhombohedral tetralayer graphene [arXiv:2606.05356].

</details>


### [139] [A Degenerate Singlet-Triplet Qubit with All-Electrical Orthogonal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27067)
*Phuong X. Nguyen,Konstantinos Tsoukalas,Jann H. Ungerer,Julian Santen,Valentin John,Stefan D. Oosterhout,Lucas Stehouwer,Stefano Bosco,Giordano Scappucci,Menno Veldhorst,Amir Yacoby*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文展示了一种基于锗双量子点中空穴自旋的简并单态-三重态（DST）量子比特，利用电调各向异性g因子，实现了ΔE_Z与J的独立全电正交控制，获得99.53%的平均单量子比特门保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单态-三重态量子比特中，塞曼能量差ΔE_Z通常由局部磁场梯度或g因子不均匀性固定，仅交换耦合J可调，导致无法实现旋转轴的正交控制，且空闲期间引入不必要态旋转。本文旨在实现ΔE_Z和J的独立电控正交操控。

Method: 利用锗双量子点中两个空穴自旋的电调各向异性g因子，找到ΔE_Z和J同时为零的简并工作点（S与T₀态简并）。通过仅施加基带电压脉冲独立控制J和ΔE_Z，实现完全正交的Z轴和X轴旋转，并采用随机基准测试评估门保真度。

Result: 在约100 ns的门操作时间内，平均物理单量子比特门保真度达99.53%。此外，可在宽范围磁场取向下电调简并点，实现增强相干时间的操作机制，并为共享全局磁场下的多量子比特扩展提供路径。

Conclusion: 锗基空穴自旋DST量子比特通过电调各向异性g因子，首次实现了ΔE_Z和J的全电独立正交控制，克服了传统ST量子比特的关键限制，为高保真度、可扩展的半导体量子计算提供了有前景的新平台。

Abstract: Singlet-triplet qubits offer an attractive encoding for semiconductor quantum computing, combining ancilla-free readout, reduced sensitivity to common-mode noise, and baseband voltage control. However, the Zeeman energy difference $ΔE_\mathrm{Z}$ is typically fixed by local magnetic field gradients or $g$-factor inhomogeneities, leaving the exchange interaction $J$ as the only dynamically tunable parameter. This always-on $ΔE_\mathrm{Z}$ precludes orthogonal control of the qubit's rotation axes and introduces unwanted state rotations during idling. Here we demonstrate all-electrical orthogonal control of a degenerate singlet-triplet (DST) qubit formed by two hole spins in a germanium double quantum dot. Exploiting the electrically tunable anisotropic $g$-factors of the two spins, we identify a regime where both $ΔE_\mathrm{Z}$ and $J$ vanish, making the $S$ and $T_0$ states degenerate at the idle point. By applying only baseband voltage pulses, we independently control both $J$ and $ΔE_\mathrm{Z}$, enabling fully orthogonal $Z$- and $X$-axis rotations. Randomized benchmarking yields an average physical single-qubit gate fidelity of 99.53\% for a gate duration of approximately 100 ns. Finally, we electrically tune the degenerate point across a wide range of magnetic field orientations, enabling operation in a regime of enhanced coherence time and offering a route towards multi-qubit scaling under a shared global magnetic field.

</details>

### [140] [Fluctuation force induced by quenched random polarizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26747)
*Bing-Sui Lu*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 研究随机冻结电偶极子在零温下诱导的涨落力，针对两种几何构型（平行半无限平板和原子-平板），发现涨落力随偶极矩均方值增长，并呈现不同的距离幂律衰减规律，且淬火偶极无序可产生排斥性涨落力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弛豫铁电体等材料中存在被冻结、无法热涨落的随机电偶极子，其在零温下诱导的涨落力行为尚不清楚，需要系统研究此类淬火偶极无序的卡西米尔类涨落力效应。

Method: 采用统计力学方法，考虑体相和表面两类淬火随机偶极无序，针对两种几何构型——(1) 间距为 ℓ 的共面半无限平板；(2) 真空中性原子位于含无序半无限平板上方 ℓ 处——推导涨落力/压强的解析表达式。

Result: 涨落力强度正比于单位体积（或面积）的淬火偶极矩均方值。体相无序诱导的平板间涨落压强按 ℓ⁻³ 衰减，表面无序按 ℓ⁻⁴；原子-平板构型中，体相无序力按 ℓ⁻⁴ 衰减，表面无序按 ℓ⁻⁵。淬火偶极无序还可产生排斥力，增强满足 DLP 条件的三层介电体系中的"纳米悬浮效应"。

Conclusion: 淬火随机偶极无序在零温下可产生显著的电磁涨落力，其距离依赖和符号特征（可排斥）为纳米尺度操控和弛豫铁电体相关应用提供了新的理论依据。

Abstract: We investigate the zero-temperature behavior of the fluctuation force induced by random electric dipoles that are frozen into a material and incapable of fluctuating thermally. Examples of such materials are relaxor ferroelectrics. In terms of the setup and geometry, we focus on a layered system comprising two coplanar semi-infinite slabs separated by a distance $\ell$ as well as a system comprising a neutral atom in the vacuum located at a distance $\ell$ above the surface of a semi-infinite slab. For both systems, we consider the cases where the quenched random dipolar disorder occurs inside the bulk as well as on the surface of the slabs. In all of these cases, we find that the bulk (surface) dipolar disorder-induced fluctuation force grows with the mean square quenched electric dipole moment per unit volume (area). The bulk (surface) dipolar disorder-induced fluctuation pressure between two semi-infinite single-layered slabs decays with $\ell^{-3}$ ($\ell^{-4}$), whereas the bulk (surface) dipolar disorder-induced fluctuation force on an atom in the vacuum near a slab containing the disorder decays with $\ell^{-4}$ ($\ell^{-5}$). We also find that the quenched dipolar disorder-induced fluctuation force between two coplanar slabs can be repulsive and serve to enhance the ``nanolevitation effect" in a three-layered dielectric system that obeys the Dzyaloshinskii-Lifshitz-Pitaevskii condition.

</details>


### [141] [High-dimensional theory of the glass transition revisited: hopping and local defects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26793)
*Harukuni Ikeda,Francesco Zamponi*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文推广了复制液体理论，允许分子仅包含部分复制体，以描述粒子级别的复制失配（如跳跃运动）。在高维硬球和简谐球体系中，该理论预测硬球的动力学转变密度显著提高，与随机堆积的严格下界一致；简谐球的热力学理想玻璃转变点也因此发生偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统复制液体理论假设不同复制体中的粒子围绕共同质心振动形成完整分子，忽略了非振动性运动（如粒子跳跃）导致的复制失配。本文旨在突破这一限制，构建能描述粒子级别复制失配的推广理论，以更准确地刻画玻璃化转变。

Method: 将复制液体理论与密度泛函理论结合，推广传统复制假定：允许每个分子仅包含全部复制体的一个子集，从而引入复制失配自由度。在高维极限下将该理论应用于硬球和简谐球模型，其中平均场描述预期精确成立。

Result: 对硬球体系，复制失配使玻璃亚稳态失稳，动力学转变的堆积分数显著提高，但热力学玻璃转变不变；转变密度在高维主导阶与Campos等人用离散贪婪随机顺序吸附得到的随机球堆积严格下界一致。对有限温度简谐球，即使达到热力学理想玻璃转变，玻璃态仍含有限复制失配分数，致使转变点偏离传统复制假定预测。

Conclusion: 推广的复制液体理论揭示了复制失配在玻璃化转变中的关键作用：硬球动力学转变与随机顺序吸附算法存在深层对应，暗示巨正则动力学在高维空间比正则动力学更高效；简谐球的热力学玻璃转变点需重新定位。

Abstract: The replicated liquid theory provides a microscopic mean-field description of the glass transition by combining the density functional theory of liquids with the replica method originally developed for spin glasses. In the conventional replica liquid theory, a glassy state is described by assuming that particles in different replicas undergo vibrational motion around common centers of mass, thereby forming molecules that contain one particle from every replica. Here we revisit this assumption by allowing each molecule to contain only a subset of replicas. This generalized formulation describes particle-level replica mismatches, which may be associated with non-vibrational motions such as particle hopping. We apply the theory to high-dimensional hard and harmonic spheres, where the mean-field description is expected to become exact. For hard spheres, replica mismatches destabilize the glassy metastable state and shift the dynamical transition to a significantly higher packing fraction, while leaving the leading thermodynamic glass transition unchanged. The resulting transition density agrees, at leading order in high dimensions, with the recent rigorous lower bound for random sphere packings obtained by Campos, Jenssen, Michelen, and Sahasrabudhe by using a discretized version of greedy Random Sequential Absorption, suggesting an algorithmic interpretation of the transition: grandcanonical dynamics is more efficient in high dimensional spaces than canonical one. For harmonic spheres at finite temperature, the glassy state contains a finite replica-mismatch fraction even at the thermodynamic ideal-glass transition, thereby shifting the transition point from that predicted by the conventional replica ansatz.

</details>


### [142] [On the robustness of noisy solutions in non-convex neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27000)
*Enrico M. Malatesta,Alessandra Passalacqua,Riccardo Zecchina*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 本文将零温下二元感知器中的重叠间隙性质(OGP)扩展到有限温度情形,证明有限能量区域可在超越零温OGP阈值后持续存在,并通过数值实验展示热噪声能在计算困难区域实现有效泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零温下,OGP严格限制了算法找到零训练误差解的能力。然而实际训练中常允许非零训练误差(有限温度),理解有限温度下解空间几何结构如何变化、算法可访问区域如何扩展,对解释神经网络优化行为至关重要。

Method: 首先证明冻结的一步副本对称破缺(1RSB)解在任意有限温度下存活;推导基于单模式Gibbs权重在决策边界附近光滑性的通用判据,判断有限温度松弛是否消除冻结;将OGP构造推广到有限温度;在师生场景下分析有限能量区域的泛化能力;使用有限能量消息传递算法进行数值验证。

Result: 冻结1RSB解在任意有限温度下持续存在;推导出温度松弛何时消除冻结的判据;证明了稠密、算法可访问的有限能量配置区域可持续到α_OGP之外,其阈值α_OGP(ε)随允许训练误差ε增长;师生场景下这些宽泛的有限能量区域仍保持良好泛化;数值实验证实热噪声能在零温解和教师恢复均计算困难的区域实现有效泛化。

Conclusion: 有限温度引入了新的算法可访问区域,扩展了可解参数空间范围。热噪声不仅是一种正则化手段,更从根本上改变了损失景观的几何结构,使得在零温下计算不可行的区域变得可访问,同时保持良好的泛化性能。

Abstract: Optimization in non-convex neural network models is strongly influenced by the geometry of the solution space: sparse, isolated, point-like clusters are typically algorithmically inaccessible, whereas wide and flat regions can be found efficiently despite being relatively rare. At zero temperature this picture has been formalized in binary perceptrons through the overlap gap property (OGP), which limits algorithmic access to configurations with zero training error above a critical constraint density $α_{\rm OGP}$. Here we extend this description to finite temperature, where a positive training error is allowed and statistically penalized. We first show that the frozen one-step replica-symmetry-breaking solution, dominating the zero temperature equilibrium measure, survives at any finite temperature. We furthermore derive a general criterion, based on the smoothness of the single-pattern Gibbs weight near the decision boundary, that determines when a finite-temperature relaxation of the loss removes freezing. We then extend the OGP construction to finite temperature and show that dense, algorithmically accessible regions of finite-energy configurations persist beyond $α_{\rm OGP}$, up to a threshold $α_{\rm OGP}(ε)$ that grows with the allowed training error $ε$. Finally, in the teacher-student setting, we show that these wide, finite-energy regions still retain good generalization. Using a finite energy message-passing algorithm, we demonstrate numerically that thermal noise enables effective generalization in the regime of constraint densities where both recovering the teacher and finding a zero temperature solution are computationally hard.

</details>

### [143] [Quantum spin-glass criticality in disordered frustrated dimer magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26151)
*Darshan G. Joshi,Matthias Vojta*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 研究了三角晶格双层海森堡模型中莫特绝缘体从自旋玻璃相到无特征顺磁相的量子相变。键无序通过偶极纹理干涉破坏反铁磁序，诱导自旋玻璃有序，导致近临界量子自旋玻璃中出现异常弱的玻璃性、低能激发的强局域化倾向，以及希格斯模式谱权重因干涉效应被强烈抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索无序量子磁体中自旋玻璃相与量子顺磁相之间的量子相变机制，理解键无序如何改变临界行为、激发谱和集体模式。

Method: 以三角晶格双层海森堡模型为平台，键算符理论（bond-operator theory）的变体为理论工具，计算热力学可观测量和激发谱。

Result: (1) 键无序在相变附近导致强烈非均匀性，压制非共线性并产生异常弱的玻璃性；(2) 低能激发表现出强烈的空间局域化倾向；(3) 希格斯（振幅）模式的谱权重因干涉效应被强烈抑制。

Conclusion: 无序诱导的非均匀性和干涉效应在自旋玻璃-量子顺磁体量子相变中起核心作用，导致近临界区反常弱玻璃性、激发局域化和希格斯模式谱权重抑制等独特现象。

Abstract: We study quantum phase transitions of Mott insulators between spin-glass and featureless paramagnetic phases. Specifically, we consider a triangular-lattice bilayer Heisenberg model with bond disorder. The clean system has two phases, a dimer quantum paramagnet and a non-collinear antiferromagnet, separated by a quantum critical point. Bond disorder destroys the antiferromagnetic phase via the interference of dipolar textures and results in spin-glass order, such that the system features a quantum phase transition between spin-glass and dimer phases. We study the vicinity of this transition using a variant of bond-operator theory and calculate thermodynamic observables as well as excitation spectra. Bond disorder leads to strong inhomogeneities near the transition which suppresses non-collinearities and leads to anomalously weak glassiness in the near-critical quantum spin glass. We characterize the low-energy excitations which have a strong tendency towards spatial localization, and we track the behavior of the amplitude (i.e. Higgs) mode across the glass phase whose spectral weight we find to be strongly suppressed due to interference effects.

</details>


### [144] [Hidden symmetry at the diabolical points of a biaxial spin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26308)
*Shadan Ghassemi Tabrizi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在旋转框架中，双轴自旋哈密顿量可映射为有限紧束缚链，其跳跃振幅由外场调控。本文揭示了Kececioglu和Garg发现的精确锥形交点格点的隐藏对称性：在每个格点处，链在两个不同旋转框架中被两次切断，由两个与哈密顿量对易但彼此不对易的投影子实现。由此构造的算符将简并能级配对，其秩给出格点的重数，并证明了每个锥的下能级携带Chern荷-1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Garg曾预言双轴自旋哈密顿量中存在隐藏对称性，该对称性可解释Kececioglu和Garg发现的精确锥形交点（diabolical points）格点结构。本文旨在揭示这一隐藏对称性的具体实现方式，并用更严格的代数方法替代此前依赖连续性和拓扑论证的重数确定方法，同时明确每个锥的Chern荷取向。

Method: 作者将双轴自旋哈密顿量在旋转框架中映射为有限紧束缚链，跳跃振幅依赖于外场。链的谱非简并，仅当外场"切断"链时才出现简并。通过分析两个不同旋转框架中的独立切断，找到两个与哈密顿量对易但互不对易的投影子，由此构造配对算符。利用该算符的秩确定格点重数，并通过计算行列式（显式负定）确定每个锥下能级的Chern荷。

Result: 在每个锥形交点格点处，链在两个不同旋转框架中被两次切断，对应两个独立确定的键。这两个切断由与哈密顿量对易但互不对易的投影子实现，从而精确实现了Garg预言的隐藏对称性。配对算符的秩给出了每个格点的简并重数，取代了此前的连续性拓扑论证。两个简并伙伴占据链的不相交延伸段，由此可显式证明某行列式为负，确定每个锥的下能级携带Chern荷-1。

Conclusion: 本文完整揭示了双轴自旋哈密顿量中隐藏对称性的代数结构，证明Kececioglu-Garg锥形交点格点上的简并源于两个互不对易投影子实现的独立切断。该方法为确定简并重数和Chern荷取向提供了严格的代数框架，深化了对该模型拓扑性质的理解。

Abstract: In a rotated frame the biaxial spin Hamiltonian $k_1S_x^2+k_2S_y^2-\mathbf{h}\cdot\mathbf{S}$ is a finite tight-binding chain whose hopping amplitudes are tuned by the applied field. A chain with no vanishing hopping has a nondegenerate spectrum, so a degeneracy can occur only where the field severs the chain. We show that at every point of the exact diabolical-point lattice found by Kececioglu and Garg the chain is severed twice over, in two different rotated frames and at two bonds that are fixed independently. The two severings are carried by projectors that commute with the Hamiltonian but not with each other. In that form they realize the hidden symmetry anticipated by Garg. A single operator built from them pairs the degenerate levels; its rank gives the multiplicity of every lattice point, replacing an earlier continuity and topological argument. Because the two partners of a doublet occupy disjoint stretches of the chain, an exact and manifestly negative determinant fixes the orientation of every cone. At every degeneracy of the model the lower level therefore carries Chern charge -1 in the convention used here.

</details>


### [145] [Quantum Geometry-Driven RKKY: From Flat to Dispersive Bands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26516)
*Zhenggang Zhou,Chuang Li,Lun-Hui Hu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该论文研究了孤立填充平带中的RKKY交换相互作用，发现其衰减长度并非仅由量子度规决定，而是同时依赖于平带几何与带隙。在小带隙下，衰减长度与量子度规长度呈非单调关系。此外，恢复色散后，反演表示可通过几何选择效应将RKKY尾迹从常规的1/R²改变为1/R³。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带系统中量子度规被认为决定了超流权重、相干长度等物理可观测量的尺度，暗示空间关联由单一几何尺度支配。作者旨在检验这一预期在孤立填充平带的RKKY交换中是否成立，揭示量子几何对磁关联范围的真实影响。

Method: 基于孤立填充平带的RKKY相互作用理论框架：带内通道缺失，交换作用仅通过跨带隙的虚带间跃迁进行。将RKKY核表示为逆带隙加权的平带投影子与空带投影子在实空间的迹乘积。利用动量空间投影子的解析性，通过解析延拓至复动量平面，由其最近奇点位置确定指数衰减长度ξ_RKKY。进一步将该形式推广至Chern平带系统，并分析了恢复色散后费米面反演表示对渐近行为的修正。

Result: RKKY核呈指数衰减，衰减长度由复动量平面中解析延拓投影子的最近奇点决定。在Chern平带中，小带隙下ξ_RKKY与量子度规长度呈非单调依赖关系：先减小后增大，表明更强的量子几何可缩短磁交换范围。恢复色散后，反演表示可使反节点费米点处Bloch态交叠在栅极调控下消失，产生1/R³的RKKY尾迹（而非传统1/R²），即几何选择效应。

Conclusion: 平带RKKY交换的空间范围并非由量子度规单一决定，而是平带几何与带隙共同作用的结果。量子几何增强在一定范围内可缩短磁交换长度，颠覆了传统认知。几何选择效应为通过调控量子几何来设计磁关联提供了新机制。

Abstract: In flat-band systems, quantum metric bounds physical observables like superfluid weight and coherence length, suggesting a single geometric scale for spatial correlations. Here, we show that the RKKY exchange in an isolated filled flat band can violate this expectation. With the intraband channel absent, the exchange proceeds via virtual interband transitions across the gap; the kernel becomes the inverse-gap-weighted trace product of the real-space flat-band projector and empty-band projectors. Because the corresponding momentum-space projectors are analytic, the kernel decays exponentially, with a decay length $ξ_\text{RKKY}$ set by the closest singularities of the analytically continued projectors in the complex momentum plane. Thus, this length depends on both the flat-band geometry and the band gap. Applying this formalism to Chern flat-band systems, we find that for small gaps, $ξ_\text{RKKY}$ depends non-monotonically on the quantum metric length: it first decreases, then increases, revealing that stronger quantum geometry can shorten the magnetic exchange range. Upon restoring dispersion, a nontrivial inversion representation can force the overlap between Bloch states at antipodal Fermi points to vanish under gate tuning, producing a $1/R^3$ RKKY tail instead of the conventional $1/R^2$---a geometric selection effect.

</details>


### [146] [Algebraic Diagrammatic Construction of the Multichannel Dyson Equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26609)
*Thibault Demartini,J. Arjan Berger,Guillaume Blanchon,Thomas Duguet,Denis Lacroix,Pina Romaniello,Vittorio Somà*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文阐明了多通道戴森方程(MCDE)近似方案的物理内涵，证明其可通过扩展的代数图构造(ADC)截断方案推导得出，且介于ADC(2)与ADC(3)之间。在一维周期Hubbard模型上的测试表明，MCDE在准粒子峰和卫星峰描述上均优于ADC(2)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MCDE是近期提出的一种计算多体系统单粒子格林函数的新近似方案，但其物理内涵尚不清晰。本文旨在通过建立MCDE与ADC截断方案之间的联系，澄清该近似的物理本质与理论定位。

Method: 从扩展版本的ADC截断方案出发，重新推导MCDE近似，揭示其理论层次介于ADC(2)与ADC(3)之间。随后在一维周期Hubbard模型（4、6、8格点）上进行数值测试，比较MCDE与ADC(2)在谱强度分布中的表现。

Result: 成功从扩展ADC框架恢复出MCDE近似，证明其截断级别介于ADC(2)与ADC(3)之间。在Hubbard模型测试中，MCDE对准粒子峰和卫星峰的描述均较ADC(2)有显著改善。

Conclusion: MCDE近似可被理解为一种介于ADC(2)与ADC(3)之间的动力学自能截断方案，且在实际模型计算中展现出比ADC(2)更高的精度，尤其体现在对谱函数中卫星结构的刻画上。

Abstract: The multichannel Dyson equation (MCDE) was recently introduced as a new approximation scheme to compute the one-body Green function in many-body systems, as reported by Riva et al. in Physical Review Letters, volume 131, article 216401, published in 2023. The physical content of this novel approximation scheme is further clarified by recovering it from an extended version of the algebraic diagrammatic construction (ADC) truncation scheme. It is thus demonstrated that the MCDE approximation lies in between the so-called ADC(2) and ADC(3) truncations of the dynamical self energy. Building on this clarification, the MCDE approximation is tested on the periodic one-dimensional Hubbard model with 4, 6, and 8 site lattices and shown to deliver an improved treatment over ADC(2) of both the quasiparticle peaks and the so-called satellites in the spectral strength distribution.

</details>


### [147] [Disentangling bulk and surface states in the electronic structure of PtBi$_2$(0001)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26804)
*Stefanie Suzanne Brinkman,Xin Liang Tan,Anders Christian Mathisen,Fabian Göhler,Øyvind Finnseth,Chul-Hee Min,Grigory Shipunov,Falk Pabst,Manuel Alonso Lemos,Balasubramanian Thiagarajan,Craig Polley,Masashi Arita,Kenya Shimada,Anna Isaeva,Jorge I. Facio,Hendrik Bentmann*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 通过ARPES和第一性原理计算，系统研究了PtBi₂(0001)两种不同表面终止面的体态和表面态电子结构，给出了跨终止面的连贯物理图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期报道三角结构PtBi₂中存在表面局域拓扑超导，促使深入理解其表面电子结构。

Method: 采用光子能量依赖和偏振依赖的角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）结合第一性原理计算，区分体态色散和表面态，并分析轨道组分。

Result: 在PtBi₂(0001)两种不同终止面上标定了多个表面态，实验与计算高度吻合；轨道组分分析与偏振依赖ARPES结果一致，建立了完整的表面电子结构图像。

Conclusion: 该工作为PtBi₂两种表面终止面提供了统一的表面电子结构理解，为后续拓扑超导研究奠定了电子结构基础。

Abstract: Recent reports of surface-localized topological superconductivity in trigonal PtBi$_2$ highlight the importance of understanding its surface electronic structure. We investigate the bulk and surface band structure of PtBi$_2$ using angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and first-principles calculations. Through photon-energy- and polarization-dependent measurements, we disentangle bulk dispersions from surface states on the two distinct surface terminations of PtBi$_2$(0001). For both terminations, we assign several different surface states and find good agreement between experiment and calculations. Based on our calculations, we analyze the orbital composition in the surface and bulk bands and compare the results to polarization-dependent ARPES measurements. Together, our results provide a coherent picture of the surface electronic structure of PtBi$_2$ across both surface terminations.

</details>


### [148] [Electronic and magnetic properties of small one-dimensional Wigner crystals from an ab initio approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26858)
*Daniele Lagasco,Jan Arjan Berger*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种从头算方法，用于研究一维小尺寸Wigner晶体的电子和磁性性质，重点关注电子电荷分布和交换耦合常数的计算，并通过映射到Heisenberg哈密顿量来获得交换耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于2019年Science杂志报道的碳纳米管中少电子Wigner晶体的实验观测，需要发展理论方法来准确描述这类强关联一维电子体系。

Method: 将电子限制在带有无限高侧壁势垒的一维势阱中模拟实验装置；采用箱中粒子波函数作为单粒子基组，在Slater行列式基底下表示系统哈密顿量，并进行全组态相互作用（full CI）计算以完整捕获给定基组下的所有电子关联效应。最后通过将从头算结果映射到Heisenberg哈密顿量上来提取交换耦合常数。

Result: 获得了小尺寸一维Wigner晶体精确的电子密度分布，其清晰地展示了电子的局域化特征。以Wigner二聚体为例验证了交换耦合常数计算方法，所得结果与文献中已有结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 所发展的从头算方法能够准确描述一维少电子Wigner晶体的电子结构和磁性，特别是电子局域化和交换耦合，为理解碳纳米管中Wigner晶体实验提供了可靠的理论工具。

Abstract: We present an \emph{ab initio} method to study the electronic and magnetic properties of small one-dimensional Wigner crystals. In particular, we focus on the calculation of the electronic charge distribution and the exchange coupling constant. Our theoretical studies are motivated by the experimental observation of few-electron Wigner crystals in a carbon nanotube [Science 364, 870 (2019)]. We model the experimental setup by confining electrons in a one-dimensional potential well with infinite side barriers. We represent the Hamiltonian of the system in a basis of Slater determinants and perform full configuration interaction to ensure we capture all the electron correlation for a given basis set. As the one-particle basis set we use particle-in-a-box wave functions which by construction satisfy the boundary conditions. With our approach, we obtain accurate electronic density profiles of small one-dimensional Wigner crystals. These profiles clearly show the localisation of the electrons. Finally, we present a simple approach to obtain the exchange coupling constant by mapping our \textit{ab initio} method on a Heisenberg Hamiltonian. We illustrate our approach on a Wigner dimer. We obtain excellent agreement with a result in the literature.

</details>


### [149] [Microscopic Emergence of Ancilla Lattice Physics in the Emery Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26869)
*Mikołaj Walicki,Johannes Knolle,Krzysztof Wohlfeld*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过Schrieffer-Wolff变换，从三带Emery模型出发，在Lieb格子上建立了 ancilla（辅助）物理的微观基础。研究表明，常规铜氧化物参数自然给出费米液体相，而实现分数化费米液体相（FL*）需要非标准参数及额外的自旋液体稳定相互作用。论文还讨论了在冷原子模拟中复现该物理的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为 multiorbital 材料中的 ancilla 物理提供严格的微观理论基础，澄清从常规费米液体相过渡到分数化费米液体相（FL*）所需的具体微观条件。

Method: 以 Lieb 格子上近 n=2 填充的三带 Emery 模型为出发点，在正电荷转移区间内，运用一系列 Schrieffer-Wolff 正则变换，建立到辅助晶格结构的直接微观映射。

Result: 在类铜氧化物参数下，模型自然给出偏移一个电子每格点的费米液体相；发现实现 FL* 相需要非标准铜氧化物参数区间，以及额外能在背景 ancilla 层中稳定量子自旋液体的相互作用。

Conclusion: 该工作为多轨道材料中的 ancilla 物理奠定了微观基础，明确了实现 FL* 的条件，并指出在 Lieb 晶格冷原子模拟中复现 ancilla 模型物理的可能性。

Abstract: We derive a controlled low-energy effective theory for the three-band Emery model on the Lieb lattice near (n=2) filling. Starting from the positive charge-transfer regime, a sequence of Schrieffer--Wolff transformations rigorously establishes a direct microscopic mapping onto an ancillary lattice structure. For realistic cuprate-like parameters, this framework naturally reproduces the conventional Fermi-liquid (FL) phase at a filling shifted by one electron per site. Our analysis identifies the precise microscopic conditions required to access the exotic fractionalized Fermi-liquid (FL$^*$) phase. We show that realizing FL$^*$ requires nonstandard cuprate parameter regimes together with additional interactions that stabilize a quantum spin liquid in the background ancilla layer. These results establish a microscopic foundation for ancilla physics in multiorbital materials and clarify the conditions for realizing FL$^*$. We discuss the possibility to replicate ancilla model physics in Lieb lattice cold atom simulations.

</details>


### [150] [The electric field gradient tensor as a symmetry-adapted order parameter in Landau theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26934)
*L. Scalise,A. W. Carbonari*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文将电场梯度（EFG）系统性地纳入朗道理论框架，通过对称性分析将EFG分解为不可约表示分量，建立了EFG响应与序参量之间的线性（初级）和二次（次级）关系，并用五个十年的四极矩实验数据和第一性原理计算验证了该分类框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EFG在四极矩超精细谱学（NQR、NMR、TDPAC、Mössbauer谱）中长期以来被用作结构相变和电子相变序参量的经验代理，但从未被系统性地纳入朗道理论。本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 将EFG视为定义在晶体学位点上的无迹对称二阶张量，在位点对称群下分解，再通过Wyckoff轨道诱导到母空间群，确定其不可约表示内容。当布里渊区中心的相变表示出现在EFG分解中时，对称性要求对应的EFG组合在相变温度以上为零，以下与序参量线性增长；对称性正交的通道则呈二次响应。由此建立初级、次级和禁戒EFG响应的可证伪分类。

Result: 框架成功复现了五十年来的临界指数、一级相变不连续性和零结果；第一性原理计算满足预言的宇称定理和零定理；α-石英的全电子计算确认了轨道选择规则——只有对应于软模不可约表示的EFG组合随畸变幅度线性变化，正交组合被抑制两个数量级；对BaFe₂As₂中⁷⁵As位点的向列相变给出了五个可证伪预言，包括一个此前未被陈述的零结果。

Conclusion: EFG可被正式纳入朗道理论，其对称性分解为理解结构相变和电子相变中的四极矩响应提供了严格的理论基础，所得分类框架具有可证伪性，并得到实验和第一性原理计算的双重验证。

Abstract: Quadrupolar hyperfine spectroscopies, including Nuclear Quadrupole Resonance (NQR), Nuclear Magnetic Resonance (NMR), Time-Differential Perturbed Angular Correlations (TDPAC), and Mössbauer spectroscopy, have long used the electric field gradient (EFG) at a nuclear site as an empirical proxy for order parameters in structural and electronic phase transitions, yet the EFG has never been systematically incorporated into Landau theory. Here we provide that framework. The EFG is an exactly traceless, symmetric rank-2 tensor defined at a crystallographic site. Decomposing it under the site-symmetry group and inducing over the Wyckoff orbit determines its irreducible representation content in the parent space group. Whenever the representation of a zone-center transition is present, symmetry requires the corresponding EFG combination to vanish above the transition and grow linearly with the order parameter below it, inheriting its critical exponent, sign, and domain structure. Symmetry-orthogonal channels are quadratic, recovering the classic empirical relations. This yields a falsifiable classification of primary, secondary, and forbidden EFG responses. The framework is validated against five decades of quadrupolar experiments, reproducing critical exponents, first-order discontinuities, and a null result, and by first-principles calculations satisfying the predicted parity and zero theorems. All-electron calculations for $α$-quartz confirm the orbit-selection rule: only the EFG combination transforming as the soft-mode irreducible representation varies linearly with distortion amplitude, while the orthogonal combination remains suppressed by two orders of magnitude. A proposed study of the $^{75}$As site across the nematic transition in BaFe$_2$As$_2$ provides five falsifiable predictions, including a previously unstated null result.

</details>


### [151] [Interactions between structural and magnetic domains through the Verwey transition in stoichiometric and doped magnetite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27020)
*Mateusz Ślęzak,Zbigniew Kąkol,Ryszard Zalecki,Andrzej Kozłowski,Naveen Kumar Chogondahalli Muniraju,Neven Barišić,Stuart Gilder,Wojciech Tabiś*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了磁铁矿在通过Verwey转变（~120 K）时的磁响应，通过在多种外加场条件下测量合成化学计量比、掺杂（Zn、Ti、Al）单晶及天然多晶样品，揭示了磁化强度变化的机制，并发现磁场诱导的单斜c轴重取向会加宽转变，尤其在化学计量比磁铁矿中显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁铁矿在Verwey转变温度（~120 K）附近的磁响应仍未被完全理解。虽然交流磁化率在升温通过T_V时增加，但磁化强度可能增加也可能减少。由于T_V处存在多种相互交织的过程，需要一个多层面的视角来阐明这些过程的本质，并确立转变的具体特征作为诊断工具。

Method: 在宽范围的外加场条件下，测量合成化学计量比磁铁矿以及Zn、Ti、Al掺杂单晶和天然多晶样品在升温通过T_V时的磁响应。

Result: 磁场诱导的单斜c轴重取向可能使转变加宽，这一效应在化学计量比磁铁矿中尤为显著。

Conclusion: 这些发现有助于阐明Verwey转变的机制，并为利用磁响应特征作为诊断工具奠定了基础。

Abstract: The magnetic response of magnetite as it cycles through the Verwey transition at $T_{\rm V}\approx 120$ K remains incompletely understood, despite numerous studies of both structural and magnetic domains. For example, although the alternating-current susceptibility increases upon heating through the Verwey temperature $T_{\rm V}$, the magnetization may either increase or decrease. Since many intertwined processes occur at $T_{\rm V}$, a multifaceted view of the magnetic changes may help clarify the nature of these processes and establish specific features of the transition as diagnostic tools. To this end, we report measurements of the magnetic response of magnetite upon heating through $T_{\rm V}$ under a wide range of applied-field conditions. We studied synthetic stoichiometric and doped (Zn, Ti, and Al) single crystals, as well as a natural polycrystalline sample, with the aim of explaining how and why the magnetization changes as a function of temperature through $T_{\rm V}$. The magnetic-field-induced reorientation of the monoclinic $c$ axis may broaden the transition, primarily in stoichiometric magnetite. These findings help clarify the mechanism of the transition.

</details>


### [152] [Interaction-induced sign reversal of the orbital magnetic susceptibility in Chern insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27112)
*Ke Huang,Xiao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示了在关联电子体系中，轨道磁化率的计算不能简单套用单粒子公式，并发展了一套在有限磁场下求解Hartree-Fock问题的代数框架。该框架通过"逆向Peierls替换"将问题转化为可按磁场展开的函数方程，发现相互作用会引入一个内禀的修正项。在扭转MoTe₂的Chern绝缘相中，该修正甚至反转了磁化率的符号，并由有限磁场HF计算定量验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为，相互作用电子体系的轨道磁化可将单粒子公式应用于自洽HF能带来计算。但作者指出，这一做法对轨道磁化率（尤其在拓扑体系中）会定性失效，需要建立新的理论框架来正确处理有限磁场下相互作用对磁化率的贡献。

Method: 作者提出了一套代数框架：利用"逆向Peierls替换"将磁平移不变的算符映射为唯一的双变量函数，从而把有限磁场下的自洽问题转化为可按B系统展开的函数方程。在一阶展开下，得到Fock势的场致变化δX的线性方程，由此分离出单粒子型磁化率和内禀的相互作用诱导磁化率。对绝缘体，该框架重现了Středa公式；并通过辅助Hilbert空间构造推广到周期和moire系统。最后在带隙Dirac模型和扭转MoTe₂ Chern绝缘体中进行有限磁场HF数值计算验证。

Result: 在扭转MoTe₂的Chern绝缘相中，相互作用诱导的修正使磁化率符号发生反转——这是单粒子型公式完全无法捕捉的效应。带隙Dirac模型和MoTe₂中的有限磁场HF计算结果与理论框架定量一致，同时该框架自然重现了绝缘体的Středa公式。

Conclusion: 相互作用电子体系的轨道磁化率包含一个内禀的、由相互作用诱导的贡献，在拓扑体系中可导致定性不同的结果（如符号反转）。所发展的逆向Peierls替换代数框架为有限磁场下关联电子体系的计算提供了系统、可控的新方法。

Abstract: It has been well established that the orbital magnetization of interacting electrons can be simply evaluated by applying the single-particle formula to self-consistent HF bands. However, we show that such procedure fails qualitatively for orbital magnetic susceptibility, especially in topological systems: in a Chern-insulating phase of twisted MoTe$_2$, the interaction-induced correction reverses the sign of the susceptibility. This result follows from an algebraic framework that solves the HF problem at finite magnetic field, where noncommuting canonical momenta obstruct a direct calculation: a \emph{reverse Peierls substitution} maps every magnetic-translation-invariant operator to a unique bivariate function, converting the finite-field self-consistency into a function equation that can be expanded systematically in $B$. At first order, this yields a linear equation for the field-induced change $δX$ of the Fock potential, leading to an intrinsically interaction-induced susceptibility in addition to a single-particle-like one. The framework reproduces the Středa formula for insulators, and an auxiliary-Hilbert-space construction carries it to periodic and moiré systems. Finite-field HF calculations in a gapped Dirac model and in the twisted-MoTe$_2$ Chern insulator confirm the theory quantitatively.

</details>


### [153] [Direct minimization versus iterative embedding in the ghost-Gutzwiller method: a comparative study of magnetism in Mott insulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27156)
*Antonio Maria Tagliente,Ivan Pasqua,Michele Fabrizio*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示了Ghost-Gutzwiller方法中迭代嵌入方案与直接变分极小化在Mott绝缘体描述上的行为差异：迭代方案在顺磁Mott相中失效并产生伪影，而直接极小化则能获得正确的顺磁解；但在允许对称性破缺的反铁磁相中，迭代方案则恢复可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决长期以来迭代量子嵌入方法在准确描述假设的对称性不变Mott绝缘体方面的挑战，澄清Ghost-Gutzwiller方法中两种形式等价求解途径（迭代嵌入与直接变分极小化）为何在Mott相中表现迥异。

Method: 采用Ghost-Gutzwiller方法，在单带Hubbard模型的Mott转变背景下，系统比较两种求解策略：(1) 迭代嵌入方案（类比动力学平均场理论）；(2) 直接极小化变分能量泛函。重点考察顺磁相（有无Zeeman场）和反铁磁相两种情形。

Result: 迭代嵌入方案计算高效但脆弱，在顺磁Mott相中需依赖ad-hoc处方，且在Zeeman场下完全失效，导致能量不连续和虚假的完全极化绝缘体；直接极小化避免了这些伪影，稳定获得真正的顺磁解。然而在反铁磁相中，迭代方案恢复正确，与动力学平均场理论结果高度一致。

Conclusion: 明确了迭代嵌入方案的可信条件与必须采用直接极小化的情形：当系统保持对称性不变时需用直接极小化，而当允许对称性破缺时迭代嵌入方案即可靠。该发现为量子嵌入方法的合理选用提供了明确判据。

Abstract: Accurately describing a hypothetical symmetry-invariant Mott insulator presents a long-standing ing challenge in iterative quantum embedding methods. We address this issue within the ghost- Gutzwiller method, which can be solved either through an iterative embedding scheme, analogous to dynamical mean-field theory, or by directly minimizing its variational energy functional. Across the Mott transition of the single-band Hubbard model, these formally equivalent approaches behave very differently: the iterative scheme is computationally efficient but fragile, necessitating ad-hoc recipes in the Mott phase that fail in a Zeeman field, leading to a discontinuous energy and a spurious fully-polarized insulator. Direct minimization avoids these artifacts, stabilizing a genuinely paramagnetic solution. Conversely, when symmetry breaking is allowed, as in an antiferromagnetic phase, the iterative scheme yields the correct solution, closely aligning with dynamical mean-field theory. Our findings delineate the conditions under which the iterative embedding can be trusted and when direct minimization is instead required.

</details>


### [154] [Quantum-Geometric Raman Response in Multiorbital Flat-Band Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27200)
*Wai Ting Tai,Martin Claassen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文建立了平带体系中非弹性光散射的完整理论框架，证明即使平带色散为零，多轨道系统中的虚 interband 过程会产生由量子几何张量决定的有限拉曼散射顶点，并将库仑散射与光-物质耦合统一处理，最终提出偏振分辨拉曼光谱可作为探测平带量子几何的敏感探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论在平带极限下（色散消失）拉曼散射顶点会完全消失，但该图像忽略了多轨道系统中虚拟 interband 过程的作用。目前缺乏一个将量子几何与库仑相互作用统一纳入的平带非弹性光散射完整理论。

Method: 作者在光子能量远离 interband 共振的极限下，以 interband 库仑散射和光-物质耦合平等对待，发展了一套系统微扰理论。将拉曼散射顶点分解为：(1) 直接由量子几何张量表示的几何项（与相互作用无关）；(2) 由虚拟 interband 库仑散射产生的有效共振和非共振贡献。

Result: 在远离 interband 跃迁共振的大失谐条件下，该框架的偏振分辨拉曼响应结果与完整多轨道计算实现了定量吻合，验证了理论的有效性。

Conclusion: 量子几何对平带体系的非弹性光散射有内禀贡献；偏振分辨拉曼光谱可作为探测关联平带平台中集体激发的量子几何敏感探针，为实验探测平带量子几何提供了新的光谱学途径。

Abstract: Flat-band materials host rich collective phenomena, yet a complete theory of their signatures in inelastic light scattering remains lacking. While naive theories of interacting flat bands would predict that Raman scattering vertices vanish identically in the limit of vanishing dispersion, we show that this picture is incomplete upon including the multiorbital character of such systems. We show that virtual interband processes generate a finite subgap Raman vertex controlled by the quantum geometric tensor even in the strict flat-band limit. We develop a systematic perturbative theory for Raman scattering from flat bands in the limit where the photon energy is far from resonance with interband transitions. Treating interband Coulomb scattering and light-matter coupling on equal footing, we decompose the Raman scattering vertices into an interaction-independent geometric term expressible directly in terms of the quantum geometric tensor, together with effective resonant and non-resonant pieces generated by virtual interband Coulomb scattering. We then study the polarization-resolved Raman response from collective excitations of an interacting flat band with nontrivial quantum geometry, and demonstrate quantitative agreement of our framework with a full multi-orbital calculation at large photon detuning from interband transitions. These results establish quantum geometry as an intrinsic contribution to inelastic light scattering in flat-band systems, and suggest polarization-resolved Raman spectroscopy as a quantum geometry-sensitive probe of the collective excitations of correlated flat-band platforms.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Randomizing the Number of Centers in k-means++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26202)
*Vaclav Rozhon*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 在预算平滑设定下，当k从{K,...,2K-1}中均匀随机选取时，k-means++算法以常数概率实现O(1)近似比，克服了传统设定下Θ(log k)的最坏情况期望近似比下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-means++是最广泛使用的k-means聚类初始化方法，但传统分析表明其最坏情况期望近似比为Θ(log k)。研究者希望探究：是否能通过对k的随机化（而非固定k）来规避这一理论下界，从而在保持算法本身不变的前提下获得更好的理论保证。

Method: 采用"预算平滑"(budget-smoothed)分析框架：对手先固定数据集和一个上界K，然后聚类中心数k从{K,...,2K-1}中均匀随机选取。在此设定下分析k-means++的近似比，证明其以常数概率为O(1)近似。

Result: 证明了在预算平滑设定下，k-means++以常数概率实现O(1)近似比，与固定k时Θ(log k)的最坏情况期望近似比形成鲜明对比。

Conclusion: 通过对k引入随机性（预算平滑），可以显著改善k-means++的理论近似保证，表明该算法的实际良好表现可能部分源于现实场景中k并非由对手针对性地固定这一事实。

Abstract: The $k$-means++ algorithm is a standard and widely used seeding method for $k$-means clustering, but for a fixed number $k$ of centers its worst-case expected approximation ratio is
  $Θ(\log k)$. We consider the same algorithm when an adversary first fixes the dataset and some $K$; the number of centers $k$ is then chosen uniformly from $\{K,\ldots,2K-1\}$.
  We prove that $k$-means++ is an $O(1)$-approximation with constant probability in this budget-smoothed setup.

</details>

# physics.bio-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [156] [A behavior-environment information loop drives sensory navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26295)
*Kevin S. Chen,Matthew P. Leighton,Damon A. Clark,Thierry Emonet*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于传递熵的信息论框架，通过量化感觉输入与行为输出之间的双向信息流来解析导航策略：感觉→行为的"反应性"信息流和行为→感觉的"主动性"信息流。该框架成功预测了细菌、线虫、果蝇及强化学习智能体的导航效率，并揭示出不同系统背后的独特导航策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物体在环境中导航时，行为与感觉输入紧密耦合，但缺乏一个统一的定量框架来同时捕捉这种耦合的双向性——即感觉驱动行为（反应性）和行为塑造感觉（主动性）。作者旨在填补这一空白，建立一个可用于解析和预测导航策略的通用信息论框架。

Method: 作者采用传递熵（transfer entropy）作为核心度量，构建双向信息流框架：从感觉输入到行为输出的传递熵定义为"反应性"信息流，从行为输出到感觉输入的传递熵定义为"主动性"信息流。首先在一个能捕捉主/被动成分的最小模型中建立宏观导航性能与微观信息流之间的联系，然后将该框架应用于细菌趋化性、线虫和果蝇的导航轨迹数据，以及强化学习智能体的导航行为分析。

Result: 双向信息流能够可靠预测各系统的导航效率，揭示了普遍存在的行为-环境反馈环路。进一步分解发现：细菌趋化性具有独特的主/被动信息流特征；果蝇嗅觉导航中导航策略存在空间依赖性；强化学习智能体学习到的策略也可通过信息流分解加以揭示。

Conclusion: 双向信息流可作为理解生物和人工系统导航行为的统一原理，为跨系统比较和导航策略诊断提供了强有力的分析工具。

Abstract: As organisms navigate the environment to locate critical resources, their behavioral actions must be tightly coupled to their sensory inputs. Here, we introduce an information-theoretic framework that quantifies this coupling using transfer entropy, which measures information flow between sensory inputs and behavioral outputs. Information flow from sensory inputs to behavior defines a "reactive" component of a navigational strategy, whereas information flow from behavior to sensory inputs defines an "active" component, whereby actions shape subsequent sensory experiences. Analyzing these bidirectional information flows enables us to both predict navigational performance and dissect navigation strategies from trajectories. Using a minimal model that captures the active and reactive components, we connect macroscopic performance to microscopic information flows. We then apply the framework to experimentally measured trajectories of bacteria, worms, and flies, as well as to machine learning agents navigating sensory landscapes. Across systems, bidirectional information flow reliably predicts navigation efficiency, revealing a common behavioral-environment feedback loop. Decomposing active and reactive information flows further exposes distinct strategies underlying bacterial chemotaxis, the spatial dependency of the navigation strategy in fly olfactory navigation, and the learned policies of a reinforcement-trained agent. Together, these results establish bidirectional information flow as a unifying principle for understanding navigation in biological and artificial systems.

</details>


### [157] [Navigation driven by bidirectional information transmission between sensing and actuation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26798)
*Avishek Das,Pieter Rein ten Wolde*

Main category: physics.bio-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出了"行为状态方程"（BESTs），将细胞导航性能完全归因于感知与 actuation 之间双向信息传递的强度和时标，并以大肠杆菌趋化性模拟验证了这一理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物导航（如细胞趋化性）由感知与 actuation 之间的反馈环路驱动，但双向信息传递的精确性如何控制导航性能尚未被理解。该研究旨在揭示信息传递与导航性能之间的普适性定量关系。

Method: 作者解析求解了两类通用模型（分别为空间感知与时间感知细胞），在线性响应（浅梯度）区域推导出导航性能的解析表达式；进而提出BESTs，将性能映射为双向信息传递强度与时标的函数；并使用TE-PWS算法精确计算大肠杆菌趋化性随机模拟中的传递熵，验证理论预测。

Result: 在浅梯度线性响应区，导航性能完全由双向信息传递的强度和时标决定，与导航器的具体微观参数无关（BESTs）。不同参数组合的导航器性能会出现数据坍塌（data collapse），且大肠杆菌趋化性模拟结果在无拟合参数下服从BESTs预测。

Conclusion: 感知与 actuation 之间的双向信息传递是生物导航的组织性原则，BESTs 提供了一种系统无关的、"信息到功能"的映射框架，可供实验验证。

Abstract: A wide variety of biological functions are driven by feedback between sensing and actuation. A paradigmatic example is cellular navigation. During navigation, the sensory system maps the environmental input signal onto a sensory output, which then drives an actuation response, changing the future sensory input. How the accuracy of this bidirectional information transmission controls navigation is not currently understood. Here, we study how information controls navigation by analytically solving two generic models that describe two major classes of biological navigators: spatial- and temporal-sensing cells. We find that, in the linear-response regime of shallow gradients, navigation performance is fully determined by the strengths and timescales of bidirectional information transmission, without any explicit dependence on the elementary parameters of the navigator. We call these relations Behavioral Equations of State (BESTs): equalities that map information to function in a system-independent way. BESTs predict an experimentally testable data collapse for the performance of navigators with different sensing and actuation parameters. We test the validity of our theory by performing stochastic simulations of chemotaxis of the bacterium Escherichia coli, computing transfer entropies exactly with the TE-PWS algorithm. The observed performance obeys the BEST without fitting or scaling parameters. Thus, our theory identifies bidirectional information transmission between sensing and actuation as an organizing principle for navigation.

</details>
