### [158] [Percolating Multifractal Domains at a Polymorphic Phase Boundary](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26586)
*Yuan-Jinsheng Liu,Xu Tian,Jiahui Zhai,Shi Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过大规模分子动力学模拟，研究发现(K,Na)NbO₃-(Bi,Na)ZrO₃固溶体中的多晶型相界(PPB)包含一个逾渗的多重分形极性畴网络。该分形畴的全局分形维数和多重分形谱宽度可作为PPB的微结构序参量。当分形畴体积分数接近三维逾渗阈值时，可获得最大的可逆压电响应，表明近临界的极性连通性是实现巨可逆机电耦合的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电体中的巨压电性通常与相界不稳定性相关，但多晶型相界(PPB)增强压电性的微观机制尚不完全清楚。传统观点将PPB归因于铁电相的共存，本研究旨在揭示PPB中极性畴的微观组织特征及其与压电性能之间的定量关系。

Method: 采用大规模分子动力学模拟，以无铅(K,Na)NbO₃-(Bi,Na)ZrO₃固溶体为模型体系，通过计算全局分形维数和多重分形谱宽度来量化极性网络的分形连通性和多尺度异质性。

Result: PPB中形成逾渗的多重分形极性畴网络；分形连通性和多尺度异质性可作为PPB的微结构序参量；当分形畴体积分数接近三维逾渗阈值时，可逆压电响应达到最大值；分形骨架保持极性记忆并提供恢复力，非分形区域提供极性柔顺性以实现大极化旋转和应变。

Conclusion: 逾渗多重分形极性畴是PPB增强压电性的微观机制，分形连通性可作为高性能压电材料的设计参量。

Abstract: Giant piezoelectricity in ferroelectrics is commonly associated with phase-boundary instabilities, among which the polymorphic phase boundary (PPB) is a prominent example conventionally attributed to the coexistence of ferroelectric phases. Here, using large-scale molecular dynamics simulations of the lead-free (K,Na)NbO3-(Bi,Na)ZrO3 solid solutions, we show that the PPB hosts a percolating multifractal polar domain which governs the dielectric and piezoelectric responses. By quantifying the global fractal dimension and multifractal spectrum width of this polar network, we identify fractal connectivity and multiscale heterogeneity as microstructural order parameters for the PPB. The maximum reversible piezoelectric response occurs when the fractal-domain volume fraction approaches the three-dimensional percolation threshold, suggesting that near-critical polar connectivity enables giant reversible electromechanical coupling. In this mechanism, the fractal backbone preserves polar memory and provides the restoring force required for reversibility, while the surrounding nonfractal regions supply the polar compliance needed for large polarization rotation and strain. These results establish percolating multifractal polar domains as a microscopic mechanism for PPB-enhanced piezoelectricity and suggest fractal connectivity as a design parameter for high-performance piezoelectrics.

</details>


### [159] [Inferring Magnetic Material Parameters from Statistical Measures in Strongly Fluctuating Magnetization Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26833)
*Kübra Kalkan,Atreya Majumdar,Ross Knapman,Omer Fetai,Franziska Scheibel,Sabrina Disch,Illia Horenko,Karin Everschor-Sitte*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种仅依赖磁化强度数据的框架，利用热驱动磁化动力学中的统计描述符（特别是潜在熵）来反演磁性材料参数（如交换刚度、磁各向异性），在强涨落和多晶/多相微结构条件下仍能有效推断参数并检测晶界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在强涨落体系（如多晶或多相微结构）中，传统的基于磁畴纹理的方法难以从磁化数据中局部推断交换刚度、磁各向异性等关键材料参数，亟需一种在高温和强涨落条件下仍可靠的新方法。

Method: 利用微磁学模拟生成热驱动磁化动力学数据，从中提取时序均值和潜在熵等统计描述符，对这些描述符建立拟合模型，再通过模型反演来推断材料参数。该方法应用于异质样品中，同时实现参数推断和晶界检测。

Result: 该框架成功实现了异质样品中的材料参数推断和晶界检测；在所考察的描述符中，潜在熵比时序均值能提供更准确的参数估计。

Conclusion: 潜在熵是一种高效描述符，适用于从动态磁化数据中推断磁性材料参数，在高温及强涨落条件下的实验参数提取中具有广阔应用前景。

Abstract: Magnetic material parameters such as the exchange stiffness and magnetic anisotropy govern the behavior and functionality of magnetic systems, yet their local inference from magnetization data remains challenging, particularly in strongly fluctuating regimes with polycrystalline or multiphase microstructure, where conventional texture-based methods become unreliable. We introduce a magnetization-only framework for inferring material parameters from thermally driven magnetization dynamics. Using micromagnetic simulations, we extract statistical quantities such as temporal mean and latent entropy from the magnetization dynamics, fit models to these descriptors, and invert the models to infer material parameters. We show that this framework enables material-parameter inference as well as grain-boundary detection in a heterogeneous sample. Among the descriptors considered, latent entropy yields more accurate parameter estimates than the temporal mean. Our results establish latent entropy as an efficient descriptor for inferring magnetic material parameters from dynamical magnetization data and point toward its use for experimental parameter extraction at high temperatures and, more broadly, under strongly fluctuating conditions.

</details>


### [160] [Formation of $\mathrm{L}1_2$-ordered $γ'$-$\mathrm{Ni}_3\mathrm{Al}$ precipitates in ternary Cu-Ni-Al alloys modelled using an ab initio concentration wave theory and atomistic simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27108)
*Christopher D. Woodgate,Hubert J. Naguszewski,Samuel L. Deacon,Mathys P. Potel,Ján Minár,David Quigley,Julie B. Staunton*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过第一性原理计算结合浓度波分析与原子蒙特卡洛模拟，研究了伪二元Cu-Ni-Al合金体系的热力学与相稳定性，揭示了三种随成分变化的相行为机制，并建立了高效的计算框架来捕捉化学有序化和共格析出行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 沉淀强化Cu-Ni-Al合金中L1₂有序γ'-Ni₃Al相可在保持优良传输性能的同时提供高力学强度。然而，对该伪二元体系从热力学和相稳定性角度的系统理论理解仍不充分，需要建立能够同时描述化学有序化和共格析出的计算框架。

Method: 采用第一性原理电子结构计算结合浓度波分析方法，提取原子间有效对相互作用（EPI），进而用于原子尺度蒙特卡洛模拟，系统研究Cuₓ(Ni₃/₄Al₁/₄)₁₋ₓ体系（0≤x≤1）的热力学与相稳定性。

Result: 揭示了三种成分依赖的相行为机制：(1)低Cu含量时，Cu溶于L1₂有序Ni₃Al相，仅存在Ni-Al化学有序化转变；(2)中间成分时，高温化学有序化后低温发生Cu与L1₂-Ni₃Al的相分离；(3)高Cu含量时，L1₂-Ni₃Al直接从固溶体中析出，无明显的二次转变。这些相变行为与合金的电子结构特征相关联。

Conclusion: 该工作展示了一种计算高效的工作流程，能够同时捕捉多组分置换合金中的化学有序化和共格析出行为，对沉淀强化等相关现象研究具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: Precipitation-strengthened Cu-Ni-Al alloys are of interest for technological applications because coherent, $\mathrm{L}1_2$-ordered $γ'$-$\mathrm{Ni}_3\mathrm{Al}$ precipitates can confer high mechanical strength while allowing the material to retain many of the good transport properties characteristic of elemental Cu. In this work, we study the thermodynamics and phase stability of the pseudobinary $\textrm{Cu}_x (\textrm{Ni}_{3/4} \textrm{Al}_{1/4})_{1-x}$ system, $0 \leq x \leq 1$. We use a computational modelling framework combining first-principles electronic structure calculations with a concentration wave analysis from which atom-atom effective pair interactions are extracted for use in atomistic Monte Carlo simulations. Our modelling reveals three distinct, composition-dependent regimes of phase behaviour, in qualitative agreement with the experimentally determined phase diagram. At low Cu content, Cu is soluble in the $\mathrm{L}1_2$-ordered $\mathrm{Ni}_3\mathrm{Al}$ phase, with a single identifiable phase transition corresponding to chemical ordering between Ni and Al. At intermediate compositions, this high-temperature ordering is followed at lower temperatures by phase separation of Cu and $\mathrm{L}1_2$-ordered $\mathrm{Ni}_3\mathrm{Al}$. Finally, at high Cu content, $\mathrm{L}1_2$-ordered $\mathrm{Ni}_3\mathrm{Al}$ precipitates directly from the solid solution, with no clearly identifiable secondary transition. We relate these phase transformations to features of the underlying electronic structures of the considered alloys. Overall, this work demonstrates a computationally efficient workflow capturing both chemical ordering and coherent precipitation in multicomponent substitutional alloys, with relevance to the study of phenomena such as precipitation strengthening.

</details>


### [161] [Physics-informed Machine Learning Prediction of Hubbard Interaction Parameters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26422)
*Jiyeon Kim,Indukuru Ramesh Reddy,Bongjae Kim,Sooran Kim*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出用机器学习模型预测过渡金属氧化物中cRPA导出的Hubbard相互作用参数（Ueff, V, J），结合集成学习与暴力搜索回归方法，在保持预测精度的同时给出显式解析表达式，揭示了这些参数背后的物理因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hubbard相互作用参数对DFT+U、DFT+DMFT等方法至关重要，但通常凭经验选取，缺乏可迁移性。cRPA虽能严格计算这些参数，但计算成本高昂，难以用于大规模材料筛选。

Method: 采用集成学习模型结合基于回归的暴力搜索（BFS）方法，构建涵盖电子、结构和原子性质的特征（如TM-d带宽、TM-d/O-p带心间距等作为局域化和屏蔽的物理描述符），预测cRPA导出的Ueff、V和J参数，并导出显式解析表达式。

Result: 集成模型对Ueff、V、J的RMSE分别达到0.148 eV、0.062 eV和0.007 eV。解析表达式显示：Ueff与电子局域化和TM-d/O-p杂化直接相关；V受杂化程度和结构紧凑性影响；J主要由过渡金属离子的元素描述符决定。

Conclusion: 该研究为预测cRPA导出的Hubbard参数提供了高效途径，兼具预测精度与物理可解释性，有助于大规模材料筛选和深入理解Hubbard相互作用的物理本质。

Abstract: Accurate determination of Hubbard interaction parameters is essential for beyond-DFT approaches such as DFT+$U$, DFT+DMFT, and DFT+$U$+$V$ in correlated materials. In practice, however, these parameters are often chosen empirically, limiting their transferability across materials. Advanced computational approaches such as the constrained random-phase approximation (cRPA) provide a rigorous route for evaluating Hubbard interactions, but their computational cost remains a bottleneck for large-scale materials screening. Here, we present machine-learning (ML) models for predicting cRPA-derived Hubbard interaction parameters: effective on-site $U_{\rm eff}$, inter-site $V$, and Hund's coupling $J$ for transition-metal oxides (TMOs). We combine ensemble-learning models with a regression-based brute-force search (BFS) approach to achieve both predictive accuracy and explicit analytical expressions. We construct features that capture electronic, structural, and atomic properties, including the TM-$d$ bandwidth and TM-$d$/O-$p$ band-center separation, as physically motivated descriptors of localization and screening. Our ensemble models achieve RMSEs of 0.148 eV, 0.062 eV, and 0.007 eV for $U_{\rm eff}$, $V$, and $J$, respectively. The derived analytical forms directly relate $U_{\rm eff}$ to electron localization and TM-$d$/O-$p$ hybridization, suggest the importance of hybridization and structural compactness in determining $V$, and indicate that $J$ is governed primarily by elemental descriptors of the TM ion. Together, the present study provides an efficient approach for predicting cRPA-derived $U_{\rm eff}$, $V$, and $J$, while offering physical insight into the factors underlying these Hubbard interactions.

</details>


### [162] [Ferroelectric switchable altermagnetic-like compensated ferrimagnets with charge ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26971)
*Xinyu Yang,Shuai Dong*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文预测Fe₃O₅单层具有混合自旋劈裂机制，叠加了交替磁体样的k路径交替劈裂和亚铁磁体样的塞曼劈裂，且可通过电场完全切换，其自旋极化率超过99%但净磁化为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将铁电性和电荷有序等更多自由度引入非常规共线磁体（如交替磁体），以实现兼具铁磁体和反铁磁体优势且功能更丰富的新型二维材料。

Method: 基于第一性原理计算，以Fe₃O₅单层为模型体系，分析其电子结构中的混合自旋劈裂机制——交替磁体样的k路径依赖劈裂与亚铁磁体样塞曼劈裂的叠加；进一步研究其隐藏的磁电效应（基于自旋-电荷耦合），评估电场对自旋劈裂的调控能力及自旋极化输运性质。

Result: Fe₃O₅单层展现出混合自旋劈裂机制：同时具有交替磁体特征的k路径交替劈裂和亚铁磁体的塞曼劈裂。基于自旋-电荷耦合的隐藏磁电效应，该自旋劈裂可被电场完全切换。其导电性高度自旋极化，极化率超过99%，可与半金属媲美但净磁化为零。

Conclusion: 通过引入铁电性和电荷有序，可在非常规磁体中实现混合自旋劈裂与电场调控，为设计零磁化、高自旋极化的新型自旋电子学材料提供了新思路。

Abstract: Unconventional collinear magnets with almost zero magnetization but prominent nonrelativistic spin-splitting, such as altermagnets, can inherit the advantages of both ferromagnets and antiferromagnets. By incorporating more degrees of freedom such as ferroelectricity and charge ordering, these unconventional magnets can be even more interesting and functionalized. With this design principle, the Fe$_3$O$_5$ monolayer is predicted to exhibit a hybrid spin-splitting mechanism, with the superposition of the altermagnetic-like $k$-path alternating splitting and ferrimagnet-like Zeeman splitting. Benefiting from the hidden magnetoelectricity based on the spin-charge coupling, such spin-splitting can be fully switched by an electric field. Its conductivity is highly spin-polarized, with a polarization ratio above $99\%$, comparable to half-metals but with zero magnetization.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

# cond-mat.supr-con [[Back]](#toc)

### [163] [DC Josephson transport in a three-terminal Yu-Shiba-Rusinov system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26568)
*Subrata Chakraborty*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了三端Josephson结中YSR态介导的非互易超流输运，证明通过控制端和粒子-空穴对称性破缺可实现磁场可调的非互易超流。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索原子尺度上非互易超导输运的实现机制，将YSR态平台从单纯的磁-超导相互作用研究拓展到相位相干量子输运和非互易器件工程领域。

Method: 构建三端BCS超导体Josephson结模型，每个超导体耦合一个承载YSR态的磁性杂质。以A、C端为主输运电极，B端为相位控制节点，分析超流输运的互易性条件，考察控制端存在性与粒子-空穴对称性破缺对非互易性的影响。

Result: 当存在控制端B且至少一个耦合于主电极的杂质中粒子-空穴对称性破缺时，主电极间出现磁场可调的非互易超流；无控制端时超流保持互易。

Conclusion: 多端YSR Josephson结为原子尺度上实现非互易超导输运和对称性破缺现象提供了可行的工程化平台。

Abstract: Scanning tunneling microscopy (STM) of magnetic adatoms on superconducting surfaces has established Yu-Shiba-Rusinov (YSR) states as a versatile platform for studying magnetic-superconducting interactions and phase-coherent quantum transport. Here, we investigate nonreciprocal supercurrent transport in a three-terminal Josephson junction comprising three BCS superconductors (`A', `B' and `C'), each coupled to a magnetic impurity hosting a pair of YSR states. Treating terminals `A' and `C' as primary transport electrodes and terminal `B' as a phase-control node, we demonstrate field-tunable nonreciprocal supercurrent between the primary electrodes. This effect requires both: the control terminal and broken particle-hole symmetry at least in one impurity attached to a primary electrode. Without the control terminal we show that the supercurrent remains reciprocal. Our results establish multi-terminal YSR junctions as a promising platform for engineering nonreciprocal superconducting transport and symmetry-breaking phenomena at the atomic scale.

</details>


### [164] [Dual Enhancement of Superconductivity in FeSe/SrTiO3 via Orbital and Correlation Synergy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27104)
*Guihao Jia,Jingming Yan,Yucong Peng,Shendong Su,Pei Ouyang,Xiaopeng Hu,Qi-Kun Xue,Wei Li*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过针尖诱导拉伸应变，单层FeSe/SrTiO₃中dz²轨道带向费米能级移动，与dxy带杂化，引发两阶段超导增强机制，超导能隙从17.8 meV提升至23.6 meV，揭示了轨道选择性配对的新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁基超导体中dz²轨道带通常远低于费米能级，被认为不参与库珀配对。本文旨在探索能否通过应变调控激活该轨道，揭示此前未被认识的轨道选择性配对机制。

Method: 以单层FeSe/SrTiO₃为模型体系，利用扫描隧道显微镜针尖施加原位可控的拉伸应变，结合隧道谱学测量超导能隙演化，追踪费米波矢变化以排除载流子掺杂效应。

Result: 拉伸应变引发两阶段超导增强：(1) 面内晶格膨胀首先增强电子关联，初步提升超导能隙；(2) 进一步应变使dz²带上移至费米能级附近并与dxy带杂化，重构配对活性轨道，引发更强的二次能隙增强。超导能隙从17.8 meV增大至23.6 meV，费米波矢保持不变，证实增强源于能带重整化与重构而非载流子掺杂。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了通过应变激活电子关联与能带工程来裁剪超导态的新途径，揭示了此前未被认识的轨道选择性配对机制，对关联多带超导体具有广泛意义。

Abstract: In iron-based superconductors, the dz2 orbital band typically resides far below the Fermi level and has not been considered to participate in Cooper pairing. Here, using monolayer FeSe/SrTiO3 as a model system, we demonstrate that tip-induced tensile strain controllably shifts the dz2 band toward the Fermi level, driving a two-stage enhancement of superconductivity. In-plane lattice expansion first enhances electronic correlation, amplifying superconductivity in the initial stage. As strain further increases, the upward-shifted dz2 band hybridizes with the dxy band, reconstructing the pairing-active d-orbital bands and inducing a secondary, stronger gap enhancement. Collectively, these two stages enlarge the superconducting gap from 17.8 to 23.6 meV. Throughout this process, invariant Fermi wave vectors confirm that the enhancement originates from band renormalization and reconstruction rather than carrier doping. Our work establishes a route to tailor superconducting states via strain-activated electronic correlations and band engineering, and reveals a previously unrecognized orbital-selective pairing mechanism with broad implications for correlated multiband superconductors.

</details>


### [165] [Momentum Structure of Superconductivity and Sublattice Effects from Quasiparticle Interference in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27148)
*Aaron G. Greenberg,Xinze Yang,Junze Deng,Pranab Kumar Nag,Kirsty Scott,Yi Jiang,Haoyu Hu,Chandra Shekhar,Dong Chen,Claudia Felser,Santiago Blanco-Canosa,Päivi Törmä,B. Andrei Bernevig,Eduardo H. da Silva Neto*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 通过高分辨率STM/QPI研究CsV₃Sb₅的超导能隙和电荷序，发现V的M_z⁺轨道上具有各向同性超导能隙，未发现PDW迹象，并揭示了费米面上对亚晶格特征的谱学敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome材料中量子干涉导致的关联态（手性电荷序、非常规超导、配对密度波PDW）是当前研究热点，但CsV₃Sb₅低能电子结构的实验图像尚不清晰，尤其是超导能隙对称性和PDW是否存在仍存争议。

Method: 采用亚开尔文扫描隧道显微镜/谱（STM/STS），对CsV₃Sb₅进行高能量分辨率、密集能量采样的超导能隙谱学成像；通过准粒子干涉（QPI）分析结合第一性原理和对称性计算，解析费米面上不同轨道的贡献；利用CDW峰选dI/dV谱追踪态密度空间分布。

Result: 1) V的M_z⁺ d轨道衍生的费米面呈现各向同性超导能隙，限制了可能的能隙对称性；2) 能隙各向异性仅可能存在于V的M_z⁻和Sb p_z带；3) CDW峰选的dI/dV谱未显示亚能隙能量范围内的增强，不支持PDW调制的存在；4) 特定QPI散射矢量的选择性缺失揭示了费米面上亚晶格特征的谱学敏感性。

Conclusion: 该工作为CsV₃Sb₅中kagome超导电性提供了更清晰的低能电子结构实验图像：超导能隙在主要费米面上各向同性，排除了PDW在本实验灵敏度内的存在，并建立了QPI与亚晶格纹理之间的谱学联系。

Abstract: Quantum interference encoded in the sublattice texture of kagome Bloch wavefunctions has been widely invoked as a route to correlated states, including chiral charge order, unconventional superconductivity, and their possible intertwining in pair-density-wave (PDW) states. Using sub-Kelvin scanning tunneling microscopy, we conducted spectroscopic mapping of the kagome material CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ with high energy resolution and dense energy sampling through the superconducting gap. Quasiparticle interference (QPI) analysis, aided by ab initio and symmetry calculations, reveals an isotropic superconducting gap on the Fermi surfaces derived from V $M_z$-even ($M_z^+$) $d$ orbitals, thereby constraining possible gap symmetries and limiting any gap anisotropy to the remaining V $M_z$-odd ($M_z^-$) and Sb $p_z$ bands. Meanwhile, the CDW-peak-selected d$I$/d$V$ spectra closely track the spatially averaged density of states and show no distinct enhancement restricted to subgap energies, which do not support an additional PDW modulation within our sensitivity. Finally, the selective absence of specific QPI scattering vectors points to a spectroscopic sensitivity to sublattice character on the Fermi surface. Together, these results provide a clearer experimental picture of the low-energy electronic structure relevant to kagome superconductivity in CsV$_3$Sb$_5$.

</details>


<div id='physics.flu-dyn'></div>

# physics.flu-dyn [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Extracting informative vortical structures of turbulent wake-extreme vortex gust interactions with machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26683)
*Ryo Koshikawa,Kai Fukami*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于卷积信息论学习的数据驱动方法，从涡-翼型相互作用流场中提取对特定未来目标变量具有因果重要性的涡结构，对比了基于升力系数和基于尺度间能量传递两种目标变量下的信息结构差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端涡阵风与翼型相互作用涉及瞬态、多尺度流动特性，传统分析方法难以从因果层面识别哪些涡结构对特定气动现象（如升力变化、能量传递）起关键作用。需要一种数据驱动方法，能根据任意未来目标变量，从流场快照中分解出具有因果重要性的信息结构。

Method: 采用卷积信息论学习（convolutional information-theoretic learning）方法，将涡流场快照分解为信息分量和残差分量，分解依据是各分量对任意未来目标变量的贡献程度。以弦长雷诺数5000下的极端涡阵风-翼型相互作用为研究对象，分别以未来升力系数和尺度依赖能量传递作为目标变量，提取重要的涡结构，并与瞬时力元分析进行对比验证。

Result: 以升力系数为目标时，涡核在涡冲击前被主要突出，而大规模分离后剪切层也被捕获；以尺度依赖能量传递为目标时，在阵风遭遇早期其信息结构与基于升力的结构明显不同，但冲击后二者趋于相似，揭示了不同瞬态气动机制间信息结构的类比性。

Conclusion: 该方法能从因果、数据驱动视角选择性地提取对特定物理机制重要的流场结构，可推广用于研究各类瞬态气动流动。

Abstract: This study considers extracting causally important vortical structures from the extreme vortex gust-airfoil interaction at a chord-based Reynolds number of $5000$. This extraction is achieved by decomposing a given vortical flow snapshot into its informative and residual components based on the contribution to an arbitrary future target variable with convolutional information-theoretic learning. For the current vortex-airfoil interactions that exhibit transient and multiscale flow characteristics, we first examine the important vortical structures with respect to a future lift coefficient. While the vortex cores are primarily highlighted before vortex impingement, the emerging shear layers are additionally captured after the massive separation, which is evident from a comparison to an instantaneous force-element analysis. We further take the scale-dependent energy transfer as a future variable of interest to examine its impact on the extracted informative structures compared to the lift-associated structures. They are distinct from the lift-based structures in the early stage of the gust encounter yet become similar after impingement, revealing an analogy between informative structures across different transient aerodynamic mechanisms. The present data-driven approach selectively extracts the specific important flow structures responsible for the physics of interest, which can support studying a range of transient aerodynamic flows from the causal, data-driven perspective.

</details>


### [167] [Rotational equivariance and locality in data-driven subgrid-scale closures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26850)
*Ryley McConkey,Julia Balla,Elyssa Hofgard,Tess Smidt,Abigail Bodner*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了旋转等变性作为归纳偏置对亚网格尺度闭合模型的影响，发现等变非局部架构在所有泛化测试中表现最优，且参数效率和数据效率均优于非等变模型，等变性的收益随感受野增大而增加。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大涡模拟的数据驱动亚网格尺度闭合研究中，旋转等变性作为归纳偏置的作用尚不明确，尤其在真实滤波比下，等变性是否能提升精度、参数效率和泛化能力有待系统验证。

Method: 以湍流通道流动为对象，对比数据增强的非等变架构与具有等变性归纳偏置的架构，在逐点和非局部两种模型类别下，以匹配的参数数量评估二者在时空、各向异性和雷诺数上的泛化表现。

Result: 非增强模型能从湍流数据中自发学到一定程度的等变性；等变非局部架构在约一半参数量下取得所有泛化测试的最高相关系数；逐点架构均未超越解析Clark基线；等变模型数据效率更高，且等变性的收益随感受野增大而扩大。

Conclusion: 等变性和非局部性对亚网格尺度闭合任务均有重要价值，二者结合可在真实数据集规模、参数量和滤波尺寸约束下实现更优的建模效果。

Abstract: Data-driven subgrid-scale closures for large eddy simulation are of significant interest in many engineering and geoscience applications. In this context, several important questions remain about the role of rotational equivariance as an inductive bias for learned tensorial mappings. We investigate whether equivariance improves accuracy, parameter efficiency, and generalization for subgrid-scale modelling at realistic filter ratios. For turbulent channel flow, we compare data-augmented non-equivariant architectures to those with equivariance as an inductive bias. We compare both pointwise and nonlocal versions of these two model classes. All models are evaluated at matched parameter counts across spatiotemporal, anisotropy, and Reynolds number generalization. We show that non-augmented models learn a small degree of equivariance directly from turbulence data, especially when that data is more isotropic. The equivariant nonlocal architecture attains the highest correlation coefficient on every generalization test at approximately half the parameter count of its non-equivariant counterpart, while the pointwise architectures do not improve on the analytical Clark baseline. Additionally, the equivariant model is more data-efficient than a non-equivariant model. The benefit of equivariance grows with the receptive field of the model, indicating that equivariance and nonlocality are both useful for the subgrid-scale closure task at realistic dataset size, parameter counts, and filter size.

</details>


### [168] [Disentangling intermittent flow structure contributions to anomalous scaling and multifractality in turbulence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26896)
*Ritwik Mukherjee,Siddhartha Mukherjee*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 通过基于Biot-Savart定律的涡度阈值滤波方法，选择性地去除湍流中的极端涡旋结构，揭示了间歇性对能量谱、结构函数标度、多重分形及涡拉伸等统计量的定量影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 湍流间歇性（表现为强涡旋和耗散尖峰）导致Kolmogorov自相似理论失效，引发反常标度和多重分形，但间歇性流动结构如何定量影响这些测量指标尚不清楚。

Method: 提出一种简单的滤波方法：对涡度场进行阈值截断，再通过Biot-Savart定律反演生成滤波后的速度场，从而分离间歇性流动结构的贡献。

Result: 滤除极端涡度贡献后：(1) 能谱标度保持不变，但瓶颈效应被抹平；(2) 结构函数标度趋于Kolmogorov理论值，且横向指数收敛更快；(3) 多重分形程度降低，粗糙度奇异性指数范围缩小；(4) 残余场反而呈现更强的多重分形，但其结构开始偏离底层湍流骨架。同时量化了间歇性对涡拉伸和应变自放大的影响。

Conclusion: 基于Biot-Savart的方法可以选择性地从湍流及其标度律中移除间歇性效应，为理解间歇性流动结构的作用提供了定量工具。

Abstract: Intermittency in turbulence manifests as intense vortices and sharp peaks of dissipation. Causing the breakdown of Kolmogorov's simple self-similar theory, it leads to anomalous scaling, multifractality and so far remains beyond the scope of a complete theoretical description. How intermittent flow structures influence these different measurements is not known quantitatively. With a simple filtering procedure-thresholding vorticity and inverting the Biot-Savart law to generate filtered velocity fields-we show the effects of intermittent flow structures can be disentangled. As extreme vorticity contributions to the velocity field are filtered out, the energy spectrum scaling persists, while the bottleneck is flattened, and structure function scalings tend towards their Kolmogorov values. The approach is more rapid for transverse exponents, revealing the selective importance of intensely swirling flow regions. Similarly, the extent of multifractality reduces as intermittency is filtered, shrinking the range of roughness singularity exponents. The residual fields are curiously more multifractal, but their structure begins to break away from an underlying turbulence skeleton. The effects on vortex stretching and strain self-amplification are quantified. Our work shows that a Biot-Savart approach can selectively remove the effects of intermittency from turbulence, and hence from its scalings.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [169] [Denoising growth complexity: Data geometry and certified schedules for diffusion sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26285)
*Martin J. Wainwright*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出「去噪增长复杂度」(Denoising Growth Complexity, DGC) 这一几何测度，将其作为连接扩散采样理论与实践的核心桥梁。DGC 通过沿高斯热流对去噪均方误差导数进行对数时间加权积分来定义，能够给出欧拉格式 KL 误差的显式局部界，进而推导出优化步长调度下的采样保证，并推广到数据认证版本。该框架统一、锐化并拓展了现有扩散采样理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散采样在高维场景中表现卓越，但其理论解释和具有认证性能保证的算法设计仍是两大核心挑战。作者试图通过一个统一的几何测度来桥接这两个问题，既解释扩散采样的有效性，又能指导算法设计并给出可证明的保证。

Method: 作者引入 DGC 作为核心分析工具，其定义为沿高斯热流对去噪均方误差导数进行对数时间加权的积分。基于 DGC 的增量性质，对随机创新表示的欧拉离散格式建立 KL 误差的局部界（每步误差由对应 DGC 增量和相对步长控制）。利用 DGC 的鞅结构开发了全数据认证的算法版本。在单块和 K 块两种设置下推导优化步长调度。进一步通过协方差、率失真、度量熵和庞加莱常数等信息论量给出 DGC 的上界。在细分区极限下，分析涉及 DGC 密度的平方根积分，并与单块调度下的普通积分进行比较。

Result: DGC 框架恢复并锐化了现有扩散采样保证，同时给出了新结果。在简单高斯混合模型上，证明了自适应数据几何的调度可带来「对数到常数」量级的计算增益。步长优化调度在单块和 K 块设置下均获得了 KL 采样保证。通过信息论上界建立了 DGC 与协方差、率失真、度量熵和庞加莱常数的联系。

Conclusion: 去噪增长复杂度 (DGC) 为扩散采样提供了统一的几何分析框架，将理论与实践紧密耦合。DGC 不仅能给出显式的局部误差界和优化步长调度，还能通过信息论量进行上界估计，精确刻画了自适应数据几何所带来的计算收益，为扩散模型的算法设计和理论理解开辟了新路径。

Abstract: Two central challenges in diffusion-based sampling are the theoretical one of understanding their remarkable effectiveness even in high-dimensional settings, and the practical one of designing algorithms with certified performance guarantees. We show that these questions are intimately connected via the \emph{denoising growth complexity} ($\mathsf{DGC}$). It is a geometric measure defined by a log-time weighted integral of the derivative of the denoising mean-squared error along the Gaussian heat flow. We show how the $\mathsf{DGC}$ increments lead to a simple and explicit bound on the KL error of an Euler scheme applied to the stochastic innovations representation. The bound is local along the path: each step is controlled by the corresponding $\mathsf{DGC}$ increment and its relative stepsize. This structure allows us to derive KL sampling guarantees for optimized stepsize schedules, both in a simpler single-block setting and in a more refined $K$-block setting. The $\mathsf{DGC}$ function has a natural martingale structure, which we exploit to develop fully data-certified versions of these algorithms. It also admits information-theoretic upper bounds in terms of covariance, rate distortion, metric entropy, and the Poincar'e constant, thereby recovering and sharpening a range of existing diffusion-sampling guarantees, as well as giving new results. In log heat-time, the fine partition limit is governed by an integral involving the square root of the $\mathsf{DGC}$ density, whereas a single-block schedule depends on its ordinary integral. This comparison precisely characterizes when adaptation to data geometry yields substantial computational gains, including logarithmic-to-constant separations for simple Gaussian mixture models.

</details>


### [170] [Toward a Unified Statistical Theory of Unsupervised Pretraining and Supervised Neural Knowledge Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26346)
*Jifan Zhang,Miklos Racz,Suqi Liu*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两阶段的知识图谱学习框架：先在大规模异构语料上进行无监督预训练，再进行多关系类型的监督学习。作者建立了非渐近风险界，将预训练表征误差与标注样本复杂度解耦，从理论上量化了大规模无标注数据对下游知识预测的收益，并在合成与真实基准上验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱学习中存在两个核心问题：一是每个实体可用的关系特定标注三元组稀缺，限制了表达性模型的训练；二是评分函数的设计通常是即兴的（ad hoc），缺乏理论依据且泛化性差。作者旨在用一个统一、有理论基础的端到端框架同时解决这两个问题。

Method: 提出一个两阶段的端到端训练框架：第一阶段在异构语料上进行无监督预训练以学习实体表征；第二阶段基于多种关系类型进行监督学习。核心理论贡献是建立了一个非渐近风险界（nonasymptotic risk bound），该界限将预训练带来的表征误差与标注样本复杂度分离，从而能够形式化地刻画大规模无标注数据对下游预测任务的增益。该框架能够扩展并涵盖（subsume）现有的多种方法。

Result: 合成实验逐一验证了理论分析中的各个组成部分；真实世界实验在大规模知识图谱基准上证实了所提方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 通过将无监督预训练与监督微调有机结合，并辅以严格的理论分析（非渐近风险界），该框架有效缓解了标注三元组稀缺问题，并为评分函数设计提供了理论支撑。实验结果充分验证了理论发现和方法的实用性。

Abstract: Knowledge graph learning provides a powerful framework for representing and inferring structured knowledge, with broad practical applications. However, the scarcity of relation-specific labeled triples per entity hinders the training of expressive models, and the ad hoc design of scoring functions limits generalizability and lacks theoretical grounding. We address both issues with a theoretically grounded, end-to-end training framework that extends and subsumes existing methods. Our framework is a two-stage procedure: unsupervised pretraining over heterogeneous corpora followed by supervised learning with multiple relation types. We establish a nonasymptotic risk bound that disentangles pretraining representation error from labeled-sample complexity, formally quantifying the benefit of large-scale unlabeled data for downstream knowledge prediction. Synthetic experiments validate each theoretical component, and real-world experiments confirm the effectiveness of our approach on large-scale knowledge graph benchmarks.

</details>


### [171] [Compactly supported radial basis functions as probability density functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26759)
*Sergio Díaz-Elbal,Andrei Martínez-Finkelshtein,Darío Ramos-López*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文将紧支撑径向基函数（CS-RBF），特别是Wendland 𝒞²核，作为一种新型参数化概率密度函数族进行探索，推导了其作为单变量和条件密度时的矩、累积分布函数等统计性质的解析表达式，并提出了基于CS-RBF混合模型的增量学习算法，在合成与真实数据集上取得了与高斯混合模型相竞争的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CS-RBF在多元逼近理论中是基础工具，但在统计与概率建模中未得到充分探索，此前主要局限于高斯过程的协方差函数或核函数。作者希望填补这一空白，将CS-RBF系统地引入概率密度建模领域。

Method: （1）推导CS-RBF作为单变量和条件密度时矩及累积分布函数的解析表达式，分完整支撑域和截断支撑域两种情形处理；（2）构建CS-RBF混合模型并分析其性质；（3）提出增量学习算法：用k-means确定中心，用随机梯度下降优化权重和形状参数。

Result: 在合成与真实数据集上，CS-RBF密度在似然性和模型复杂度方面与高斯混合模型相比具有竞争力，同时支持单变量与条件设置下关键分布性质的精确计算。

Conclusion: CS-RBF可作为一类有前景的参数化概率密度函数族，兼具紧支撑的计算优势和解析可处理性，适用于密度估计和概率建模任务。

Abstract: Compactly Supported Radial Basis Functions (CS-RBFs) are a fundamental tool in multivariate approximation theory. However, their use in statistics and probability modeling remains underexplored, having been used mainly to express covariance functions in Gaussian processes or as kernel functions. This work explores CS-RBFs as a novel parametric family of probability density functions, focusing in particular on Wendland $\mathscr{C}^2$ kernels. The primary contribution of this work is the derivation of analytical expressions for various statistical properties, such as moments and the cumulative distribution function, of CS-RBFs as univariate and conditional densities. The approach comprises two alternative scenarios: when the CS-RBF support lies entirely within the variable's domain (untruncated support) and when part of it is outside (truncated support). Mixture models employing CS-RBFs are also analyzed, and their main properties are detailed. Furthermore, we introduce an incremental learning algorithm for density estimation with CS-RBF mixture models, in which centers are determined using k-means and weights and shape parameters are optimized by stochastic gradient descent. Experiments on synthetic and real-world datasets show that CS-RBF densities provide competitive results in terms of likelihood and model complexity in comparison with Gaussian mixture models. In addition, these CS-RBF densities allow the exact computation of key distributional properties in univariate and conditional settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [172] [Top-$k$ Pareto Bandits: Hypervolume Regret for Multi-Objective Slate Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26273)
*Nicolas Gutowski,Fabien Chhel,Alexandre Letard,Sylvain Lamprier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究随机多目标bandit问题，每轮选择大小为k的臂集合并观察d维奖励向量（半bandit反馈）。目标不是找到单一最优臂，而是维护一个能近似Pareto前沿的小集合。作者提出THV-UCB算法，基于超体积贡献的乐观估计进行贪婪选择，并给出与实例无关的遗憾界Õ(d√(nkT))和与gap相关的遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在许多实际多目标决策场景中（如推荐系统、资源分配），决策者需要在多个冲突目标间权衡。传统多臂bandit方法通常只关注单一最优臂，无法刻画Pareto前沿的全局结构。本文旨在通过维护一个小规模臂子集来近似整个Pareto前沿，以支配超体积作为度量标准，从而更好地支持多目标应用中的权衡决策。

Method: 作者将问题形式化为：选择k个臂以最大化支配超体积（dominated hypervolume），该目标函数是单调次模的。以此为出发点，提出THV-UCB算法：在每轮中，为每个臂维护d维奖励的置信上界（UCB），然后贪婪地依次选择对当前已选集合边际超体积贡献最大的臂，直至选出k个臂。算法利用greedy对单调次模函数(1-1/e)近似保证的特性，定义了α-近似超体积遗憾。

Result: 作者为THV-UCB建立了两个遗憾界：(1) 与实例无关的界 Õ(d√(nkT))，对所有实例成立；(2) 与gap相关的界 Õ(nk²·⁵/Δ_min)，当臂之间充分分离时，该界关于T呈多重对数增长。这表明算法在理论上能够有效用小规模子集近似Pareto前沿。

Conclusion: THV-UCB算法为多目标bandit中Pareto前沿近似问题提供了首个具有理论保证的方法。遗憾界从理论上支撑了用小规模子集近似Pareto前沿的可行性，对推荐系统、资源分配等多目标应用具有指导意义。

Abstract: We consider a stochastic multi-objective bandit problem where, at each round, the agent selects a slate of $k$ arms and observes their $d$-dimensional reward vectors under semi-bandit feedback. We do not aim at identifying a single optimal arm; instead, we consider the problem of maintaining a small set of actions that jointly approximate the Pareto frontier. We formalize this objective through the dominated hypervolume induced by the selected subset of arms, and define an $α$-approximate hypervolume regret with respect to the best size-$k$ subset achievable in hindsight, where $α= 1 - 1/e$ reflects the approximation guarantee of greedy maximization for monotone submodular functions. To address this problem, we introduce \textit{THV-UCB}, an optimistic algorithm that selects arms greedily based on optimistic estimates of their marginal hypervolume contributions. We establish a gap-free regret bound $\tilde{O}(d\sqrt{nkT})$ that holds on every instance, together with a gap-dependent bound $\tilde{O}(nk^{2.5}/Δ_{\min})$ that becomes polylogarithmic in $T$ once the arms are sufficiently well separated. Our results provide theoretical support for using small subsets to approximate Pareto fronts in various multi-objective applications.

</details>


### [173] [High-Order Markov Blanket Discovery via a k-Order Relaxation of the Faithfulness Assumption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26357)
*Loong Kuan Lee,Ragavi Krishnamoorthy,Nico Piatkowski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种对忠实性假设的"k阶"松弛，以捕获变量间的奇偶型高阶依赖关系，并设计了k阶马尔可夫毯（kOMB）算法用于马尔可夫毯发现，在忠实性被违反时仍能有效恢复目标变量的MB。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的马尔可夫毯学习方法依赖忠实性假设（faithfulness），但该假设会被XOR、奇偶校验等高价依赖关系破坏，有限样本下的经验违反甚至会产生虚假依赖。因此需要一种能处理此类高阶依赖的松弛方法。

Method: 提出"k阶"忠实性松弛，用于刻画k+2个变量之间的奇偶型关系；基于该松弛设计概念验证算法kOMB（k-order Markov blanket），进行马尔可夫毯发现。

Result: 实验表明，kOMB算法在忠实性被真实违反以及经验违反两种情形下，均能有效恢复目标变量的马尔可夫毯。

Conclusion: k阶忠实性松弛能够有效应对高阶依赖对标准忠实性假设的破坏，kOMB算法为不满足忠实性条件下的马尔可夫毯发现提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: The problem of learning the graphical Markov blanket (MB) of a variable from data has applications in many areas such as structure learning for Bayesian networks and Markov random fields, causal discovery, and feature selection. However, a common assumption most methods make is that the conditional independencies in the distribution imply the same separation in the graphical structure -- also known as the faithfulness assumption. Unfortunately, this assumption can be violated by higher-order dependencies such as XOR and parity-type relations, and -- on finite samples -- by empirical violations that, in extreme cases, even induce spurious dependencies absent from the true distribution. Therefore, in this paper we propose a "k-order" relaxation of the faithfulness assumption that captures parity type relationships between k+2 variables. We then propose a proof of concept algorithm called k-order Markov blanket (kOMB) that uses this relaxation for MB discovery. Finally, we empirically show how kOMB can recover the MB of a variable under both true and empirical violations of faithfulness. Code available at: https://github.com/lklee9/k-order-Markov-blanket

</details>


### [174] [Existence-Field Diffusion Model for Spatial Point Processes with Variable Cardinality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26428)
*Xiaoyin Pan,Christian R. Shelton,Rakshith Mahishi,Chengkuan Hong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出存在场扩散模型(EFDM)，通过为每个潜在点引入存在变量来统一建模空间点过程的空间位置与基数，避免了传统方法中离散跨维度操作的刚性，在可变基数数据集上取得了更好的生成建模效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在可变基数的空间点过程建模中存在局限：方法要么将基数建模与空间结构解耦，要么依赖离散跨维度操作来改变点数，导致生成动力学僵化且不对称。需要一种能统一、灵活地联合建模点数与空间配置的方法。

Method: 提出存在场扩散模型(EFDM)：为每个潜在点关联一个"存在变量"(existence variable)，表示其存在程度，从而将空间位置和基数纳入统一的连续扩散过程。该框架无需显式的离散跨维度跃迁，即可联合建模点数与空间分布。

Result: EFDM 提供了一个灵活通用的空间点过程生成建模框架，在具有可变基数的数据集上，相较于现有方法展现出更强的建模能力。

Conclusion: EFDM 通过存在变量的引入，成功将离散的基数变化融入连续扩散过程，克服了传统方法的刚性与不对称性问题，为空间点过程的生成建模提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: We study generative modeling of spatial point processes (SPP), where both the number of points and their spatial configuration are governed by a joint distribution. While diffusion models have achieved strong performance in modeling complex distributions, extending them to variable-cardinality SPP remains challenging. Existing approaches either decouple the modeling of cardinality and spatial structure, or rely on discrete trans-dimensional operations to modify the number of points, resulting in inflexible and asymmetric generative dynamics. We propose the existence-field diffusion model (EFDM) for spatial point processes modeling, where each potential point is associated with an existence variable representing its degree of presence. This enables a unified diffusion process that jointly models both spatial locations and cardinality without requiring explicit discrete transitions. We demonstrate that our approach provides a flexible and general framework for generative modeling of spatial point processes, achieving improved modeling capability on datasets with varying cardinality.

</details>


### [175] [Universality and Approximation Rates of Graph Neural Networks with Random Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26699)
*Lukas Gonon,Thilo Meyer-Brandis,Niklas Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究带有随机节点特征的消息传递图神经网络，证明了置换等变神经网络（PENN）结合部分随机节点特征，可以在概率意义上以任意精度逼近固定规模有向图上的任意可测置换不变/等变函数，并对连续可微函数给出了逼近速率的理论上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机节点特征已被理论和实验证明能增强图神经网络的表达能力，但缺乏关于其通用逼近性质（universality）的系统性理论结果。本文旨在填补这一空白，为结合随机节点特征的GNN建立严格的通用逼近定理及逼近速率分析。

Method: 作者聚焦于置换等变神经网络（PENN），这是由前馈神经网络组件构建的一类GNN，涵盖多种主流架构。通过概率分析和函数逼近理论，证明PENN配合部分随机节点特征可逼近任意可测置换不变/等变函数，并对k阶连续可微函数（k≥2）推导出逼近速率上界，该上界将前馈组件的深度和非零权重数与逼近精度联系起来。

Result: 建立了PENN的通用逼近定理：结合部分随机节点特征，可在概率意义下以任意精度逼近固定规模有向图（含多维节点和边特征）上的任意可测置换不变或置换等变函数。对于k≥2阶连续可微函数，进一步给出了逼近速率的理论上界。

Conclusion: 随机节点特征能够从理论上保证PENN类GNN的通用逼近能力，且逼近效率与网络组件的复杂度存在定量关系，为理解和设计更具表达力的图神经网络提供了理论基础。

Abstract: We investigate message-passing graph neural networks with random node features. Random node features are known to enhance the expressiveness of graph neural networks (GNNs) both theoretically and empirically. Here, we establish a novel universality result focusing on permutation-equivariant neural networks (PENNs), a class of GNNs built from feedforward neural network components that subsumes many prominent GNN architectures. We show that PENNs, combined with partially random node features, can approximate arbitrarily well in probability any measurable permutation-invariant or permutation-equivariant function on directed graphs of fixed size with multidimensional node and edge features. For $k$-times continuously differentiable functions, $k\geq 2$, we also derive upper bounds on the approximation rates, relating the complexity of the feedforward components of a PENN in terms of layer depth and number of nonzero weights to the desired approximation accuracy.

</details>


### [176] [BayesAME: Bayesian Active Model Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.27023)
*Paula Cordero Encinar,Taylan Cemgil,Arnaud Doucet,Virginia Aglietti,Silvia Chiappa*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: BayesAME 是一种序列贝叶斯框架，能自动确定评估大模型所需的核心集大小。它通过历史模型表现建模潜在能力，利用信息增益逐步选择样本，直到估计波动和不确定性低于阈值，从而在保证估计可靠性的同时减少评估成本。多目标扩展可进一步压缩核心集，实验表明其在多个基准上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前核心集方法需人工预设大小，无法在优先保证估计可靠性时自动确定核心集规模。此外，文献中对非随机采样是否优于随机采样存在争议，且传统二值分数忽略了连续响应中的细粒度信息。

Method: 提出 BayesAME 序列贝叶斯框架：(1) 将模型性能建模为随机变量，基于共享历史表现的题目组定义潜在能力参数，并引入联合先验编码目标模型与历史模型行为相似的信念；(2) 通过后验分布推导性能估计量、量化不确定性，以信息增益准则选择下一个题目加入核心集；(3) 迭代扩展核心集，直到性能估计波动和不确定性均低于用户设定阈值；(4) 提出多目标扩展，捕捉多模型间的性能相关性以进一步压缩核心集；(5) 采用连续响应的对数似然替代传统二值分数以提升估计精度。

Result: 在多个多样化基准上的大量实验表明，BayesAME 一致优于现有方法的序列化适配版本。验证了非随机核心集选择确实优于随机选择，回应了文献中的质疑。此外，使用连续响应对数似然相比传统二值分数显著提升了估计精度。

Conclusion: BayesAME 成功实现了核心集大小的自动确定，在估计可靠性与评估效率之间取得了良好平衡。非随机核心集选择的价值被实验证实，连续响应信息的利用也是提升估计质量的关键因素。

Abstract: Evaluating large generative models across benchmarks is time-consuming and computationally expensive. This drives the need for methods that can estimate full benchmark performance by evaluating models on only a subset of items, known as a coreset. Current literature mostly requires the practitioner to input a coreset size. However, when reliable performance estimation takes priority over efficiency, an evaluation method should also be capable of automatically determining a coreset size that reflects this priority. We introduce BayesAME, a sequential Bayesian framework specifically targeting automatic determination of the coreset size. BayesAME models performance as a random variable by defining a latent ability for each group of items sharing the same historical model performances, with a joint prior distribution encoding the belief that the target model behaves similarly to these historical models. The posterior distribution over these abilities is used to derive performance estimators, quantify performance uncertainty, and select items to add to the coreset via an information-gain criterion. The coreset is iteratively augmented until the performance estimate fluctuation and the performance uncertainty fall below their respective user-defined thresholds. We propose a multi-target extension that captures performance correlations across multiple target models to further reduce the coreset size. Through extensive experiments across diverse benchmarks, we demonstrate that BayesAME consistently outperforms sequential adaptations of existing methods. Crucially, our comprehensive analysis addresses recent skepticism in the literature, establishing that non-random coreset selection is advantageous over random selection. Finally, we highlight that leveraging continuous response log-likelihoods over traditional binary scores significantly enhances estimation accuracy.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [177] [Astroid Spinodal Boundary in Phase-Based Ising Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26213)
*Malihe Farasat,Nikhil Shukla*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文揭示了振荡器伊辛机与动力学伊辛机共享同一星形线旋节边界，该边界决定了条件能量景观在单稳态与双稳态之间的转变，并发现一阶谐波条件能量在数学上等价于单轴磁性粒子的Stoner-Wohlfarth能量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 振荡器伊辛机(OIM)与动力学伊辛机(DIM)均利用二次谐波注入(SHI)稳定的相位态编码二进制自旋，但在耦合网络中SHI与局域网络场的竞争如何重塑条件能量景观、驱动稳定性转变的机制尚不明确。

Method: 作者通过理论分析，推导SHI与瞬态局域网络场竞争下的条件能量景观，识别出星形线旋节边界(spinodal boundary)，并分析该边界附近势垒的标度行为（一般光滑点处 ΔE_i ∝ μ_i^{3/2}，纵向尖点处 ΔE_i ∝ μ_i^2），同时对比OIM与DIM的旋节几何关系，并建立一阶谐波条件能量与Stoner-Wohlfarth能量的数学等价性。

Result: （1）SHI与局域网络场的竞争通过星形线旋节边界驱动单稳态-双稳态转变；（2）势垒在旋节边界附近的一般光滑点按μ_i^{3/2}标度，在纵向尖点按μ_i^2标度；（3）OIM与DIM共享相同的旋节几何，仅横向场方向相反；（4）一阶谐波条件景观等价于单轴磁性粒子的Stoner-Wohlfarth能量，仅差一个加性常数。

Conclusion: OIM与DIM的统一旋节框架为理解此类伊辛机的稳定性转变提供了解析工具，与Stoner-Wohlfarth能量的等价性建立了磁学与非线性振荡器网络之间的深刻联系，有望指导新型计算硬件的设计与优化。

Abstract: Oscillator Ising machines (OIMs) and dynamical Ising machines (DIMs) encode binary spins in phase states stabilized by second-harmonic injection (SHI). In a coupled network, the competition between SHI and the instantaneous local network field reshapes each oscillator's conditional energy landscape. We show that this competition drives a transition between monostable and bistable regimes through an astroid spinodal boundary. Near this boundary, the barrier scales as $ΔE_i\proptoμ_i^{3/2}$ at generic smooth points and as $ΔE_i\proptoμ_i^{2}$ at the longitudinal cusp. OIMs and DIMs obey the same spinodal geometry, with their conditional landscapes related by a reversal of the transverse field. Finally, the first-harmonic conditional landscape is mathematically equivalent, up to an additive constant, to the Stoner--Wohlfarth energy of a uniaxial magnetic particle.

</details>


<div id='nlin.AO'></div>

# nlin.AO [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [Determining Critical Temperature Differences of Low-Temperature-Differential Stirling Engines: Nonlinear Dynamics Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.26539)
*Momoko Amemiya,Yuki Izumida*

Main category: nlin.AO

TL;DR: 本文利用非线性动力学方法，通过将临界温差确定为LTD斯特林发动机动力学模型的同宿分岔点，推导了自洽方程，揭示了决定临界温差的参数组合，从而建立了超越经验设计的性能优化基本原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管低温差（LTD）斯特林发动机能够在日常生活的低温度差下运行，但决定其停止旋转的临界温差问题尚未被探索。该研究旨在填补这一空白，为发动机设计提供理论依据。

Method: 采用非线性动力学方法，建立LTD斯特林发动机的动力学模型，将临界温差问题转化为同宿分岔问题，推导并求解自洽方程来确定临界温差。

Result: 自洽方程的解揭示了一组决定临界温差的参数组合，从而为LTD斯特林发动机的性能优化提供了明确的设计原则。

Conclusion: 通过非线性动力学分析，成功确定了LTD斯特林发动机的临界温差，并建立了超越传统经验设计的根本性设计原则，为该类发动机的性能提升提供了理论指导。

Abstract: While the low-temperature-differential (LTD) Stirling engines are innovative engines that can operate with low temperature differentials in our daily life, the problem of determining the critical temperature differences below which the engine ceases to rotate remains unexplored. In this study, we solve this problem using a nonlinear dynamics approach. We derive the self-consistent equations that determine the critical temperature differences as homoclinic bifurcation points of a dynamical model of the LTD Stirling engines. The solutions of the self-consistent equations reveal a combination of parameters that determines the critical temperature differences. This enables us to establish the fundamental design principles for improving the performance of the LTD Stirling engines beyond empirical designs.

</details>
